<file path="src/presentation/cli.rs">
//! Command-line definition (clap derive).

use std::num::NonZeroUsize;
use std::path::PathBuf;

use clap::{Args, Parser, Subcommand};
use clap_complete::Shell;

use crate::domain::gateway::Release;

/// Diagnose, build, serve and benchmark local AI backends on a single GPU.
#[derive(Debug, Parser)]
#[command(name = "ai-ctl", version, about)]
pub struct Cli {
    #[command(subcommand)]
    pub command: Command,
}

/// Top-level commands.
#[derive(Debug, Subcommand)]
pub enum Command {
    /// Install the inventory of backends and models into the config directory
    Init {
        /// Directory holding the model files (GGUF), one subdirectory per model
        #[arg(long, value_name = "DIR")]
        models_dir: PathBuf,
    },
    /// Check everything the stack needs on this machine, read-only
    Doctor {
        /// Print the checks as JSON
        #[arg(long)]
        json: bool,
    },
    /// Build a backend from its sources, and manage the installed builds
    Build(BuildCommand),
    /// Install the gateway, llama-swap, and show its configuration
    Gateway {
        #[command(subcommand)]
        action: GatewayAction,
    },
    /// Start the gateway on the inventory's address, and return once it answers
    Up,
    /// Stop the gateway and its models, and return once their GPU memory is released
    Down,
    /// Show whether the gateway runs, its models and the GPU memory in use; exit 3 when it does not run
    Status,
    /// Print the gateway's log, which holds every model's output, or stream one model's output
    Logs {
        /// Keep printing the gateway's log as it grows, until the gateway stops
        #[arg(short, long)]
        follow: bool,
        /// Stream this model's output instead: what the gateway kept of it, then its live
        /// output, until Ctrl-C or the gateway stops
        model: Option<String>,
    },
    /// Print a shell completion script
    Completions {
        /// Target shell
        #[arg(value_enum)]
        shell: Shell,
    },
}

/// `ai-ctl build <project>` builds; its subcommands manage the builds.
#[derive(Debug, Args)]
#[command(args_conflicts_with_subcommands = true, subcommand_negates_reqs = true)]
pub struct BuildCommand {
    #[command(subcommand)]
    pub action: Option<BuildAction>,
    /// Project to build, such as llama.cpp
    #[arg(required = true)]
    pub project: Option<String>,
    /// Git tag, branch or commit to build; `latest` is the newest release
    #[arg(long = "ref", value_name = "REF", default_value = "latest")]
    pub reference: String,
    /// Build again a build that is already installed
    #[arg(long)]
    pub force: bool,
    /// Parallel compile jobs [default: one per processor]
    #[arg(long, value_name = "N")]
    pub jobs: Option<NonZeroUsize>,
}

/// Managing the installed builds.
#[derive(Debug, Subcommand)]
pub enum BuildAction {
    /// List the installed builds; `*` marks the current one
    List {
        /// Only this project's builds
        project: Option<String>,
    },
    /// Make an installed build the current one, without compiling
    Use {
        /// Project, such as llama.cpp
        project: String,
        /// Build id, as `build list` shows it
        id: String,
    },
    /// Remove all but the newest builds; the current one is always kept
    Prune {
        /// How many of the newest builds to keep, besides the current one
        #[arg(long, value_name = "N")]
        keep: usize,
        /// Only this project's builds
        project: Option<String>,
    },
}

/// Managing the gateway.
#[derive(Debug, Subcommand)]
pub enum GatewayAction {
    /// Download a llama-swap release, verify it and make it current
    Install {
        /// Release, such as 263 [default: the release this ai-ctl pins]
        #[arg(long, value_name = "N", value_parser = parse_release)]
        version: Option<Release>,
    },
    /// Print the configuration `up` gives llama-swap, generated from the inventory
    Config,
}

fn parse_release(text: &str) -> Result<Release, String> {
    Release::parse(text).map_err(|err| err.to_string())
}
</file>

<file path="src/main.rs">
//! Entry point and composition root.
//!
//! `main` is the only place that knows every layer: it builds the
//! infrastructure adapters, hands them to the application use cases, and
//! passes the parsed command line to the presentation layer.

use std::process::ExitCode;

use ai_ctl::infrastructure::build_store::{self, FsBuildStore};
use ai_ctl::infrastructure::cmake::CmakeBuilder;
use ai_ctl::infrastructure::command::SystemPrograms;
use ai_ctl::infrastructure::download::Curl;
use ai_ctl::infrastructure::gateway_api::LlamaSwapApi;
use ai_ctl::infrastructure::gateway_process::LocalGateway;
use ai_ctl::infrastructure::gateway_store::FsGatewayStore;
use ai_ctl::infrastructure::git::GitSources;
use ai_ctl::infrastructure::host::SystemHost;
use ai_ctl::infrastructure::inventory_file::InventoryFile;
use ai_ctl::infrastructure::nvml::NvmlProbe;
use ai_ctl::infrastructure::paths::Paths;
use ai_ctl::infrastructure::podman::Podman;
use ai_ctl::presentation::{self, Services, cli::Cli};
use anyhow::Result;
use clap::Parser;

fn main() -> ExitCode {
    let cli = Cli::parse();

    match presentation::run(cli, services) {
        Ok(code) => code,
        Err(err) => {
            eprintln!("error: {err:#}");
            ExitCode::FAILURE
        }
    }
}

fn services() -> Result<Services> {
    let paths = Paths::from_env()?;
    Ok(Services {
        gpu: Box::new(NvmlProbe),
        host: Box::new(SystemHost::new()),
        inventory: Box::new(InventoryFile::new(paths.inventory_file())),
        sources: Box::new(GitSources::new(paths.sources_dir())),
        cmake: Box::new(CmakeBuilder::new(paths.work_dir(), paths.logs_dir())),
        builds: Box::new(FsBuildStore::new(paths.builds_dir())),
        programs: Box::new(SystemPrograms),
        downloads: Box::new(Curl::new()),
        gateway: Box::new(FsGatewayStore::new(paths.gateway_dir())),
        processes: Box::new(LocalGateway::new(
            paths.gateway_state_dir(),
            paths.logs_dir(),
        )),
        api: Box::new(LlamaSwapApi),
        containers: Box::new(Podman),
        clock: build_store::now,
        pause: std::thread::sleep,
    })
}
</file>

<file path="src/presentation/mod.rs">
//! Presentation layer: command-line parsing, dispatch and output.
//!
//! Depends on `application` and on the `domain` types it returns, never on
//! `infrastructure`: the adapters arrive already wired by `main.rs`.

pub mod build;
pub mod cli;
pub mod completions;
pub mod doctor;
pub mod gateway;
pub mod lifecycle;

use std::io::{self, IsTerminal, Write};
use std::path::Path;
use std::process::ExitCode;
use std::time::Duration;

use anyhow::{Context, Result};

use crate::application::doctor::Doctor;
use crate::application::init;
use crate::application::ports::{
    BuildStore, Cmake, Containers, Downloads, GatewayApi, GatewayProcesses, GatewayStore, GpuProbe,
    HostProbe, InventoryStore, Programs, Sources,
};
use crate::domain::timestamp::Timestamp;
use cli::{Cli, Command};

/// The adapters the commands run against, built by the composition root.
pub struct Services {
    pub gpu: Box<dyn GpuProbe>,
    pub host: Box<dyn HostProbe>,
    pub inventory: Box<dyn InventoryStore>,
    pub sources: Box<dyn Sources>,
    pub cmake: Box<dyn Cmake>,
    pub builds: Box<dyn BuildStore>,
    pub programs: Box<dyn Programs>,
    pub downloads: Box<dyn Downloads>,
    pub gateway: Box<dyn GatewayStore>,
    pub processes: Box<dyn GatewayProcesses>,
    pub api: Box<dyn GatewayApi>,
    pub containers: Box<dyn Containers>,
    pub clock: fn() -> Timestamp,
    pub pause: fn(Duration),
}

/// Runs a parsed command line. `services` is called only by the commands
/// that need the adapters, so `completions` works on any machine.
pub fn run(cli: Cli, services: impl FnOnce() -> Result<Services>) -> Result<ExitCode> {
    match cli.command {
        Command::Init { models_dir } => {
            let services = services()?;
            init::init(services.inventory.as_ref(), &models_dir)?;
            print(&init_message(services.inventory.location(), &models_dir))?;
            Ok(ExitCode::SUCCESS)
        }
        Command::Doctor { json } => {
            let services = services()?;
            let report = Doctor {
                gpu: services.gpu.as_ref(),
                host: services.host.as_ref(),
                inventory: services.inventory.as_ref(),
                builds: services.builds.as_ref(),
                programs: services.programs.as_ref(),
                releases: services.gateway.as_ref(),
                processes: services.processes.as_ref(),
                api: services.api.as_ref(),
                containers: services.containers.as_ref(),
            }
            .run();
            let output = if json {
                let mut text = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&doctor::render_json(&report))
                    .context("cannot serialize the report")?;
                text.push('\n');
                text
            } else {
                doctor::render_text(&report, use_color())
            };
            print(&output)?;
            Ok(if report.has_failures() {
                ExitCode::FAILURE
            } else {
                ExitCode::SUCCESS
            })
        }
        Command::Build(command) => build::run(command, &services()?),
        Command::Gateway { action } => gateway::run(action, &services()?),
        Command::Up => lifecycle::up(&services()?),
        Command::Down => lifecycle::down(&services()?),
        Command::Status => lifecycle::status(&services()?),
        Command::Logs { follow, model } => lifecycle::logs(&services()?, follow, model.as_deref()),
        Command::Completions { shell } => {
            let mut script = Vec::new();
            completions::write(shell, &mut script);
            print(&String::from_utf8_lossy(&script))?;
            Ok(ExitCode::SUCCESS)
        }
    }
}

fn init_message(inventory: &Path, models_dir: &Path) -> String {
    let models_dir = std::path::absolute(models_dir).unwrap_or_else(|_| models_dir.to_owned());
    format!(
        "Installed {}\nModels directory: {}\nNext: download the models it lists, then run `ai-ctl doctor`.\n",
        inventory.display(),
        models_dir.display()
    )
}

/// Colors only on a terminal, and never when `NO_COLOR` is set.
fn use_color() -> bool {
    io::stdout().is_terminal() && std::env::var_os("NO_COLOR").is_none_or(|value| value.is_empty())
}

/// Writes `text` to stdout. A closed pipe (`ai-ctl doctor | head`) is not an
/// error: the reader has what it wanted.
fn print(text: &str) -> Result<()> {
    let mut stdout = io::stdout().lock();
    match stdout
        .write_all(text.as_bytes())
        .and_then(|()| stdout.flush())
    {
        Err(err) if err.kind() != io::ErrorKind::BrokenPipe => {
            Err(err).context("cannot write to standard output")
        }
        _ => Ok(()),
    }
}

/// Whether `err` comes from writing to a pipe whose reader has gone.
fn closed_pipe(err: &anyhow::Error) -> bool {
    err.chain().any(|cause| {
        cause
            .downcast_ref::<io::Error>()
            .is_some_and(|err| err.kind() == io::ErrorKind::BrokenPipe)
    })
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn a_closed_pipe_is_told_from_other_errors_through_their_context() {
        let closed = anyhow::Error::from(io::Error::from(io::ErrorKind::BrokenPipe))
            .context("cannot write the log");
        assert!(closed_pipe(&closed));
        let refused = anyhow::Error::from(io::Error::from(io::ErrorKind::ConnectionRefused))
            .context("GET http://127.0.0.1:8080/logs/stream/x");
        assert!(!closed_pipe(&refused));
        assert!(!closed_pipe(&anyhow::anyhow!("no gateway runs")));
    }
}
</file>

<file path="src/presentation/lifecycle.rs">
//! `ai-ctl up`, `down`, `status` and `logs`: start, stop and read the
//! gateway, with their steps on stderr.

use std::fmt::Write as _;
use std::io::{self, Write};
use std::process::ExitCode;

use anyhow::Result;

use crate::application::gateway_config::Configurator;
use crate::application::lifecycle::{Down, Event, Lifecycle, LogEnd, Status, Up, mib};
use crate::application::ports::Liveness;
use crate::domain::machine::GpuProcess;

use super::Services;

/// The exit status of `status` when no gateway runs, as `systemctl status`
/// gives for a unit that does not run.
const NOT_RUNNING: u8 = 3;

fn lifecycle(services: &Services) -> Lifecycle<'_> {
    Lifecycle {
        configurator: Configurator {
            inventory: services.inventory.as_ref(),
            builds: services.builds.as_ref(),
            gpu: services.gpu.as_ref(),
        },
        releases: services.gateway.as_ref(),
        processes: services.processes.as_ref(),
        api: services.api.as_ref(),
        containers: services.containers.as_ref(),
        pause: services.pause,
    }
}

/// Runs `ai-ctl up`.
pub fn up(services: &Services) -> Result<ExitCode> {
    let mut stderr = io::stderr();
    let up = lifecycle(services).up(&mut |event| {
        // A write error is ignored: starting goes on.
        let _ = writeln!(stderr, "{}", render_event(&event));
    })?;
    super::print(&render_up(&up))?;
    Ok(ExitCode::SUCCESS)
}

/// Runs `ai-ctl down`.
pub fn down(services: &Services) -> Result<ExitCode> {
    let mut stderr = io::stderr();
    let down = lifecycle(services).down(&mut |event| {
        // A write error is ignored: stopping goes on.
        let _ = writeln!(stderr, "{}", render_event(&event));
    })?;
    super::print(&render_down(&down))?;
    Ok(ExitCode::SUCCESS)
}

/// Runs `ai-ctl status`: exits 0 when the gateway runs, 3 when it does not.
pub fn status(services: &Services) -> Result<ExitCode> {
    let mut stderr = io::stderr();
    let status = lifecycle(services).status(&mut |event| {
        // A write error is ignored: reading goes on.
        let _ = writeln!(stderr, "{}", render_event(&event));
    })?;
    super::print(&render_status(&status))?;
    Ok(if status.gateway.is_some() {
        ExitCode::SUCCESS
    } else {
        ExitCode::from(NOT_RUNNING)
    })
}

/// Runs `ai-ctl logs`: the gateway's log, or one model's stream. A reader
/// that goes (`ai-ctl logs | head`) ends it without an error.
pub fn logs(services: &Services, follow: bool, model: Option<&str>) -> Result<ExitCode> {
    let lifecycle = lifecycle(services);
    let mut stdout = io::stdout().lock();
    let ended = match model {
        Some(model) => lifecycle
            .model_log(model, &mut stdout)
            .map(|()| Some("The gateway ended the stream")),
        None => lifecycle.log(follow, &mut stdout).map(render_log_end),
    };
    match ended {
        Ok(Some(line)) => {
            let _ = writeln!(io::stderr(), "{line}");
        }
        Ok(None) => {}
        Err(err) if super::closed_pipe(&err) => {}
        Err(err) => return Err(err),
    }
    Ok(ExitCode::SUCCESS)
}

pub fn render_event(event: &Event) -> String {
    match event {
        Event::Forgotten { pid, liveness } => {
            let found = match liveness {
                Liveness::Exited => "has exited".to_owned(),
                Liveness::Reused(Some(program)) => {
                    format!("is gone; PID {pid} now runs {}", program.display())
                }
                Liveness::Reused(None) | Liveness::Running => {
                    format!("is gone; PID {pid} now runs another user's program")
                }
            };
            format!("The gateway recorded with PID {pid} {found}; record removed")
        }
        Event::Pulling { image } => format!("Pulling {image}; an image takes several minutes"),
        Event::Starting { release, listen } => format!("Starting llama-swap {release} on {listen}"),
        Event::Unloading => "Unloading the models".to_owned(),
        Event::Stopping { pid } => format!("Stopping llama-swap (PID {pid})"),
        Event::Killing { pid } => {
            format!("llama-swap (PID {pid}) outlived SIGTERM; sending SIGKILL")
        }
        Event::Releasing { processes } => {
            let pids: Vec<String> = processes.iter().map(|p| p.pid.to_string()).collect();
            format!(
                "Waiting for PID {} to release {} MiB of GPU memory",
                pids.join(", "),
                mib(processes)
            )
        }
        Event::Warning(message) => format!("warning: {message}"),
    }
}

pub fn render_up(up: &Up) -> String {
    match up {
        Up::Started {
            pid,
            release,
            listen,
            log,
            preload,
        } => {
            let mut out =
                format!("Gateway up on http://{listen} (llama-swap {release}, PID {pid})\n");
            if !preload.is_empty() {
                let _ = writeln!(out, "Loading in the background: {}", preload.join(", "));
            }
            let _ = writeln!(out, "Log: {}", log.display());
            out
        }
        Up::Running { pid, listen } => {
            format!("Gateway up already on http://{listen} (PID {pid})\n")
        }
    }
}

/// The line `logs` ends with on stderr, if any.
pub fn render_log_end(end: LogEnd) -> Option<&'static str> {
    match end {
        LogEnd::Printed => None,
        LogEnd::NoGateway => Some("No gateway running; nothing more to follow"),
        LogEnd::Stopped => Some("The gateway stopped"),
        LogEnd::Replaced => {
            Some("A new gateway log replaced this one; run `ai-ctl logs -f` again to follow it")
        }
    }
}

/// One line of `status`: its label in a column, then its value.
fn line(out: &mut String, label: &str, value: &str) {
    let _ = writeln!(out, "{label:<12}{value}");
}

pub fn render_status(status: &Status) -> String {
    let mut out = String::new();
    let Some(gateway) = &status.gateway else {
        line(&mut out, "Gateway", "stopped");
        if let Some(gpu) = status.gpu {
            line(
                &mut out,
                "GPU memory",
                &format!("{} of {} MiB in use", gpu.used_mib, gpu.usable_mib),
            );
        }
        render_leftovers(&mut out, &status.leftovers);
        return out;
    };
    line(
        &mut out,
        "Gateway",
        &format!("running on http://{} (PID {})", gateway.listen, gateway.pid),
    );
    match gateway.models.as_deref() {
        Some([]) => line(&mut out, "Models", "none loaded"),
        Some(models) => {
            let models: Vec<String> = models
                .iter()
                .map(|model| format!("{} ({})", model.id, model.state))
                .collect();
            line(&mut out, "Models", &models.join(", "));
        }
        // The warning on stderr says why.
        None => {}
    }
    if let Some(gpu) = status.gpu {
        let mut memory = format!("{} of {} MiB in use", gpu.used_mib, gpu.usable_mib);
        if !gateway.held.is_empty() {
            let _ = write!(
                memory,
                ", {} MiB by the gateway's models",
                mib(&gateway.held)
            );
        }
        line(&mut out, "GPU memory", &memory);
    }
    if gateway.current == Some(false) {
        out.push_str(
            "The inventory, a build or llama-swap changed since the gateway started; \
             run `ai-ctl down`, then `ai-ctl up`, to apply it\n",
        );
    }
    render_leftovers(&mut out, &status.leftovers);
    out
}

/// The processes an earlier gateway left on the GPU, with how to end them.
fn render_leftovers(out: &mut String, leftovers: &[GpuProcess]) {
    if leftovers.is_empty() {
        return;
    }
    let named: Vec<String> = leftovers
        .iter()
        .map(|process| match process.memory {
            Some(bytes) => format!(
                "PID {} ({}, {} MiB)",
                process.pid,
                process.name,
                bytes >> 20
            ),
            None => format!("PID {} ({})", process.pid, process.name),
        })
        .collect();
    let pids: Vec<String> = leftovers
        .iter()
        .map(|process| process.pid.to_string())
        .collect();
    let _ = writeln!(
        out,
        "Left on the GPU by an earlier gateway: {}; `ai-ctl up` refuses to start until they \
         are gone, and ai-ctl never stops a backend itself: end them with `kill {}`",
        named.join(", "),
        pids.join(" ")
    );
}

pub fn render_down(down: &Down) -> String {
    match down {
        Down::Stopped { pid, released } if released.is_empty() => {
            format!("Gateway stopped (PID {pid})\n")
        }
        Down::Stopped { pid, released } => format!(
            "Gateway stopped (PID {pid}); {} MiB of GPU memory released\n",
            mib(released)
        ),
        Down::NotRunning => "No gateway running\n".to_owned(),
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::application::lifecycle::{GpuMemory, Serving};
    use crate::application::ports::RunningModel;
    use crate::domain::gateway::Release;

    fn listen() -> std::net::SocketAddr {
        "127.0.0.1:8080".parse().unwrap()
    }

    fn process(pid: u32, mib: Option<u64>) -> GpuProcess {
        GpuProcess {
            pid,
            name: "llama-server".into(),
            memory: mib.map(|mib| mib << 20),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_stopped_gateway_says_what_it_released() {
        assert_eq!(
            render_down(&Down::Stopped {
                pid: 4242,
                released: vec![process(5000, Some(21094)), process(6000, Some(906))],
            }),
            "Gateway stopped (PID 4242); 22000 MiB of GPU memory released\n"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            render_down(&Down::Stopped {
                pid: 4242,
                released: vec![],
            }),
            "Gateway stopped (PID 4242)\n"
        );
        assert_eq!(render_down(&Down::NotRunning), "No gateway running\n");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_started_gateway_names_its_address_its_models_and_its_log() {
        let up = Up::Started {
            pid: 4242,
            release: Release::parse("263").unwrap(),
            listen: listen(),
            log: "/home/me/.local/state/ai-ctl/logs/gateway.log".into(),
            preload: vec!["qwen3.8-27b".into()],
        };
        assert_eq!(
            render_up(&up),
            "Gateway up on http://127.0.0.1:8080 (llama-swap v263, PID 4242)\n\
             Loading in the background: qwen3.8-27b\n\
             Log: /home/me/.local/state/ai-ctl/logs/gateway.log\n"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            render_up(&Up::Running {
                pid: 4242,
                listen: listen()
            }),
            "Gateway up already on http://127.0.0.1:8080 (PID 4242)\n"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn each_step_is_one_line() {
        let events = [
            Event::Forgotten {
                pid: 7,
                liveness: Liveness::Exited,
            },
            Event::Forgotten {
                pid: 7,
                liveness: Liveness::Reused(Some("/usr/bin/bash".into())),
            },
            Event::Forgotten {
                pid: 7,
                liveness: Liveness::Reused(None),
            },
            Event::Pulling {
                image: "ghcr.io/ggml-org/llama.cpp:server-cuda13".into(),
            },
            Event::Starting {
                release: Release::parse("263").unwrap(),
                listen: listen(),
            },
            Event::Unloading,
            Event::Stopping { pid: 4242 },
            Event::Killing { pid: 4242 },
            Event::Releasing {
                processes: vec![process(5000, Some(21094)), process(6000, None)],
            },
            Event::Warning("cannot remove /x".into()),
        ];
        let lines: Vec<String> = events.iter().map(render_event).collect();
        assert_eq!(
            lines,
            [
                "The gateway recorded with PID 7 has exited; record removed",
                "The gateway recorded with PID 7 is gone; PID 7 now runs /usr/bin/bash; record removed",
                "The gateway recorded with PID 7 is gone; PID 7 now runs another user's program; record removed",
                "Pulling ghcr.io/ggml-org/llama.cpp:server-cuda13; an image takes several minutes",
                "Starting llama-swap v263 on 127.0.0.1:8080",
                "Unloading the models",
                "Stopping llama-swap (PID 4242)",
                "llama-swap (PID 4242) outlived SIGTERM; sending SIGKILL",
                "Waiting for PID 5000, 6000 to release 21094 MiB of GPU memory",
                "warning: cannot remove /x",
            ]
        );
    }

    fn serving(models: Option<Vec<RunningModel>>, held: Vec<GpuProcess>) -> Serving {
        Serving {
            pid: 4242,
            listen: listen(),
            models,
            current: Some(true),
            held,
        }
    }

    const GPU: Option<GpuMemory> = Some(GpuMemory {
        used_mib: 19891,
        usable_mib: 24075,
    });

    #[test]
    fn a_running_gateway_shows_its_models_and_their_memory() {
        let status = Status {
            gateway: Some(serving(
                Some(vec![RunningModel {
                    id: "qwen3.6-35b-a3b".into(),
                    state: "ready".into(),
                }]),
                vec![process(5000, Some(19876))],
            )),
            gpu: GPU,
            leftovers: vec![],
        };
        assert_eq!(
            render_status(&status),
            "Gateway     running on http://127.0.0.1:8080 (PID 4242)\n\
             Models      qwen3.6-35b-a3b (ready)\n\
             GPU memory  19891 of 24075 MiB in use, 19876 MiB by the gateway's models\n"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_gateway_without_models_or_an_api_says_what_it_knows() {
        let mut gateway = serving(Some(vec![]), vec![]);
        let status = Status {
            gateway: Some(gateway.clone()),
            gpu: None,
            leftovers: vec![],
        };
        assert_eq!(
            render_status(&status),
            "Gateway     running on http://127.0.0.1:8080 (PID 4242)\n\
             Models      none loaded\n"
        );
        gateway.models = None;
        gateway.current = Some(false);
        let status = Status {
            gateway: Some(gateway),
            gpu: None,
            leftovers: vec![],
        };
        assert_eq!(
            render_status(&status),
            "Gateway     running on http://127.0.0.1:8080 (PID 4242)\n\
             The inventory, a build or llama-swap changed since the gateway started; \
             run `ai-ctl down`, then `ai-ctl up`, to apply it\n"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_stopped_gateway_shows_the_gpu_and_what_was_left_on_it() {
        let status = Status {
            gateway: None,
            gpu: GPU,
            leftovers: vec![process(5000, Some(19876)), process(6000, None)],
        };
        assert_eq!(
            render_status(&status),
            "Gateway     stopped\n\
             GPU memory  19891 of 24075 MiB in use\n\
             Left on the GPU by an earlier gateway: PID 5000 (llama-server, 19876 MiB), \
             PID 6000 (llama-server); `ai-ctl up` refuses to start until they are gone, \
             and ai-ctl never stops a backend itself: end them with `kill 5000 6000`\n"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn following_says_why_it_ended() {
        assert_eq!(render_log_end(LogEnd::Printed), None);
        assert_eq!(
            render_log_end(LogEnd::NoGateway),
            Some("No gateway running; nothing more to follow")
        );
        assert_eq!(render_log_end(LogEnd::Stopped), Some("The gateway stopped"));
        assert!(render_log_end(LogEnd::Replaced).is_some());
    }
}
</file>

<file path="src/application/lifecycle.rs">
//! The gateway's lifecycle: `up` starts it on the configuration the
//! inventory gives, once the images its models run are in local storage;
//! `down` stops it, returning once the GPU memory its models held is
//! released; `status` and `logs` read it.

use std::collections::BTreeSet;
use std::io::Write;
use std::net::SocketAddr;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::time::Duration;

use anyhow::{Context, Result, anyhow, bail, ensure};

use crate::domain::gateway::Release;
use crate::domain::gateway_config::CONTAINER_PREFIX;
use crate::domain::inventory::is_valid_id;
use crate::domain::machine::{Gpu, GpuProcess, Probed};

use super::gateway_config::{Configurator, Prepared};
use super::ports::{
    Containers, GatewayApi, GatewayProcesses, GatewayRecord, GatewayStore, Liveness, RunningModel,
    Signal, StartedGateway,
};

/// How often a starting or stopping gateway is checked.
const POLL: Duration = Duration::from_millis(100);
/// llama-swap listens within a second; 30 s leaves room for a loaded
/// machine.
const START_POLLS: u32 = 300;
/// llama-swap stops its models within 30 s of SIGTERM, then forces its own
/// exit 5 s later.
const STOP_POLLS: u32 = 400;
/// What SIGKILL leaves the process to end.
const KILL_POLLS: u32 = 50;
/// The driver releases a process's memory as it exits: 30 s is for a
/// process still exiting.
const RELEASE_POLLS: u32 = 300;
/// The lines of the log a failure quotes.
const TAIL: usize = 15;
/// The most of the log read at once.
const LOG_CHUNK: usize = 1 << 20;

/// A step of `up` or `down`, for the user to follow.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Event {
    /// The recorded gateway no longer runs; its record was removed.
    Forgotten {
        pid: u32,
        liveness: Liveness,
    },
    Pulling {
        image: String,
    },
    Starting {
        release: Release,
        listen: SocketAddr,
    },
    /// Asking the gateway to stop its models.
    Unloading,
    /// SIGTERM sent to the gateway.
    Stopping {
        pid: u32,
    },
    /// The gateway outlived SIGTERM; SIGKILL follows.
    Killing {
        pid: u32,
    },
    /// Waiting for the gateway's processes to leave the GPU.
    Releasing {
        processes: Vec<GpuProcess>,
    },
    /// Something went wrong that does not change the outcome.
    Warning(String),
}

/// What `up` found or did.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Up {
    Started {
        pid: u32,
        release: Release,
        listen: SocketAddr,
        log: PathBuf,
        /// The models the gateway loads now that it runs.
        preload: Vec<String>,
    },
    /// The gateway ran already, on the same configuration.
    Running { pid: u32, listen: SocketAddr },
}

/// What `down` found or did.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Down {
    Stopped {
        pid: u32,
        /// The gateway's processes that held GPU memory, all gone now;
        /// empty when the GPU could not be read.
        released: Vec<GpuProcess>,
    },
    NotRunning,
}

/// The GPU's memory, in MiB.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct GpuMemory {
    pub used_mib: u64,
    /// What processes can allocate: the total less what the driver reserves.
    pub usable_mib: u64,
}

/// The gateway `status` found running.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Serving {
    pub pid: u32,
    pub listen: SocketAddr,
    /// The models it runs; `None` when its API did not answer.
    pub models: Option<Vec<RunningModel>>,
    /// Whether it runs on the configuration, address and release `up` would
    /// start it with now; `None` when they cannot be worked out.
    pub current: Option<bool>,
    /// Its processes on the GPU; empty when the GPU cannot be read.
    pub held: Vec<GpuProcess>,
}

/// What `status` found.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Status {
    /// `None` when no gateway runs.
    pub gateway: Option<Serving>,
    /// `None` when the GPU cannot be read.
    pub gpu: Option<GpuMemory>,
    /// The processes earlier gateways left on the GPU.
    pub leftovers: Vec<GpuProcess>,
}

/// What `doctor` reads of the gateway, changing nothing.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Inspection {
    pub status: Status,
    /// A record whose process is gone: its PID, and what runs under it now.
    pub stale: Option<(u32, Liveness)>,
}

/// How printing the gateway's log ended.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum LogEnd {
    /// The log is printed, not followed.
    Printed,
    /// Nothing to follow: no gateway runs.
    NoGateway,
    /// The followed gateway stopped; its log is printed to the end.
    Stopped,
    /// A new log replaced the one followed.
    Replaced,
}

/// What `up` starts the gateway with.
struct Target {
    prepared: Prepared,
    /// The configuration file.
    config: String,
    release: Release,
    binary: PathBuf,
}

/// The processes the GPU's holders belong to.
struct Owners {
    /// The running gateway's processes; empty when none runs.
    gateway: BTreeSet<u32>,
    /// The processes of ai-ctl's containers.
    containers: BTreeSet<u32>,
}

/// The lifecycle use cases, with the ports they drive.
pub struct Lifecycle<'a> {
    pub configurator: Configurator<'a>,
    pub releases: &'a dyn GatewayStore,
    pub processes: &'a dyn GatewayProcesses,
    pub api: &'a dyn GatewayApi,
    pub containers: &'a dyn Containers,
    /// Waits between two checks of a starting or stopping gateway.
    pub pause: fn(Duration),
}

impl Lifecycle<'_> {
    /// Starts the gateway, unless it runs already on the same configuration,
    /// address and release. A gateway that does not answer is stopped and
    /// its record removed.
    pub fn up(&self, events: &mut dyn FnMut(Event)) -> Result<Up> {
        let _lock = self.processes.lock()?;
        let target = self.target()?;
        let listen = target.prepared.listen;

        if let Some(record) = self.running(events)? {
            ensure!(
                self.is_current(&record, &target)?,
                "the gateway (PID {}) runs on another configuration, address or release \
                 than the inventory, the builds and llama-swap give now; \
                 run `ai-ctl down`, then `ai-ctl up`",
                record.pid
            );
            return Ok(Up::Running {
                pid: record.pid,
                listen,
            });
        }

        ensure!(
            self.processes.is_free(listen)?,
            "{listen} is taken by another program; stop it, or give the gateway another \
             `listen` address in the inventory"
        );
        if let Some(gpu) = self.read_gpu("not looking for processes left on it", events) {
            let owners = self.owners(None, events);
            let left = self.leftovers(&gpu, &owners, events);
            if !left.is_empty() {
                let (named, pids) = named(&left);
                bail!(
                    "processes an earlier gateway left on the GPU hold {} MiB of its memory: {named}; \
                     a model started now would not find the memory the inventory gives it, \
                     and ai-ctl never stops a backend itself: end them with `kill {pids}`, \
                     then run `ai-ctl up` again",
                    mib(&left)
                );
            }
        }
        let prepared = &target.prepared;
        for image in &prepared.images {
            if !self.containers.image_exists(image)? {
                events(Event::Pulling {
                    image: image.clone(),
                });
                self.containers.pull(image)?;
            }
        }

        events(Event::Starting {
            release: target.release,
            listen,
        });
        let mut started = self
            .processes
            .start(&target.binary, &target.config, listen)?;
        self.wait_until_healthy(started.as_mut(), listen, events)?;
        Ok(Up::Started {
            pid: started.pid(),
            release: target.release,
            listen,
            log: self.processes.log(),
            preload: target.prepared.config.preload,
        })
    }

    /// Stops the gateway: its models first, through its API, then the
    /// gateway itself, SIGTERM then SIGKILL; and returns once the processes
    /// it ran on the GPU are gone. ai-ctl never stops a backend itself: a
    /// process still on the GPU after that is an error naming it.
    pub fn down(&self, events: &mut dyn FnMut(Event)) -> Result<Down> {
        let _lock = self.processes.lock()?;
        let Some(record) = self.running(events)? else {
            return Ok(Down::NotRunning);
        };
        let held = self
            .read_gpu("not waiting for its memory", events)
            .map(|gpu| held(&gpu, &self.owners(Some(record.pid), events)));

        events(Event::Unloading);
        if let Err(err) = self.api.unload_all(record.listen) {
            events(Event::Warning(format!(
                "cannot unload the models ({err:#}); stopping the gateway stops them"
            )));
        }
        events(Event::Stopping { pid: record.pid });
        self.stop(
            record.pid,
            &mut || Ok(self.processes.liveness(&record)? != Liveness::Running),
            events,
        )?;
        self.processes.forget()?;

        let released = match held {
            Some(held) => self.wait_for_release(held, events)?,
            None => Vec::new(),
        };
        Ok(Down::Stopped {
            pid: record.pid,
            released,
        })
    }

    /// Whether the gateway runs, with what it serves, and what the GPU
    /// holds. A record whose process is gone is removed.
    pub fn status(&self, events: &mut dyn FnMut(Event)) -> Result<Status> {
        let _lock = self.processes.lock()?;
        let record = self.running(events)?;
        Ok(self.observe(record, events))
    }

    /// What `status` reports, read without the lock: a stale record is
    /// reported, not removed.
    pub fn inspect(&self, events: &mut dyn FnMut(Event)) -> Result<Inspection> {
        let (record, stale) = match self.processes.record()? {
            Some(record) => match self.processes.liveness(&record)? {
                Liveness::Running => (Some(record), None),
                liveness => (None, Some((record.pid, liveness))),
            },
            None => (None, None),
        };
        Ok(Inspection {
            status: self.observe(record, events),
            stale,
        })
    }

    /// The gateway of `record`, running, with what it serves, and what the
    /// GPU holds.
    fn observe(&self, record: Option<GatewayRecord>, events: &mut dyn FnMut(Event)) -> Status {
        let gpu = self.read_gpu("its memory is left out", events);
        let (held, leftovers) = match &gpu {
            Some(gpu) => {
                let owners = self.owners(record.as_ref().map(|record| record.pid), events);
                (held(gpu, &owners), self.leftovers(gpu, &owners, events))
            }
            None => (Vec::new(), Vec::new()),
        };
        let gateway = record.map(|record| {
            let models = self
                .api
                .running(record.listen)
                .map_err(|err| {
                    events(Event::Warning(format!(
                        "cannot list the gateway's models ({err:#})"
                    )));
                })
                .ok();
            let current = self
                .target()
                .and_then(|target| self.is_current(&record, &target))
                .map_err(|err| {
                    events(Event::Warning(format!(
                        "cannot tell whether the gateway runs on the configuration \
                         the inventory gives ({err:#})"
                    )));
                })
                .ok();
            Serving {
                pid: record.pid,
                listen: record.listen,
                models,
                current,
                held,
            }
        });
        Status {
            gateway,
            gpu: gpu.map(|gpu| GpuMemory {
                used_mib: gpu.memory_used >> 20,
                usable_mib: gpu.usable_mib(),
            }),
            leftovers,
        }
    }

    /// Copies the gateway's log to `out`; with `follow`, then copies what
    /// the running gateway adds to it, until it stops.
    pub fn log(&self, follow: bool, out: &mut dyn Write) -> Result<LogEnd> {
        let Some(mut offset) = self.copy_log(0, out)? else {
            bail!(
                "no gateway log at {}: the gateway has not run yet; start it with `ai-ctl up`",
                self.processes.log().display()
            );
        };
        if !follow {
            return Ok(LogEnd::Printed);
        }
        let record = match self.processes.record()? {
            Some(record) if self.processes.liveness(&record)? == Liveness::Running => record,
            _ => return Ok(LogEnd::NoGateway),
        };
        loop {
            (self.pause)(POLL);
            // Read after the check, the log holds all the gateway wrote
            // before it stopped.
            let running = self.processes.liveness(&record)? == Liveness::Running;
            let Some(copied) = self.copy_log(offset, out)? else {
                return Ok(LogEnd::Replaced);
            };
            offset += copied;
            if !running {
                return Ok(LogEnd::Stopped);
            }
        }
    }

    /// Copies to `out` the output of `model` the running gateway kept, then
    /// its live output, until the gateway ends the stream.
    pub fn model_log(&self, model: &str, out: &mut dyn Write) -> Result<()> {
        ensure!(
            is_valid_id(model),
            "{model:?} is not a model ID: an ID starts with a letter or a digit and holds only \
             ASCII letters, digits, `.`, `_` and `-`"
        );
        let record = match self.processes.record()? {
            Some(record) if self.processes.liveness(&record)? == Liveness::Running => record,
            _ => bail!(
                "no gateway runs, and a model's output is kept by the gateway while it runs; \
                 `ai-ctl logs` prints the gateway's log, which holds the output of every model \
                 since the last `ai-ctl up`"
            ),
        };
        self.api.stream_log(record.listen, model, out)
    }

    /// Copies the log from `offset` to its end; how much it copied, or
    /// `None` when there is no log or another one replaced it.
    fn copy_log(&self, offset: u64, out: &mut dyn Write) -> Result<Option<u64>> {
        let mut copied = 0;
        loop {
            let Some(bytes) = self.processes.read_log(offset + copied, LOG_CHUNK)? else {
                return Ok(None);
            };
            if bytes.is_empty() {
                return Ok(Some(copied));
            }
            out.write_all(&bytes)
                .and_then(|()| out.flush())
                .context("cannot write the log")?;
            copied += bytes.len() as u64;
        }
    }

    /// What `up` would start the gateway with now.
    fn target(&self) -> Result<Target> {
        let prepared = self.configurator.prepare()?;
        let release = self
            .releases
            .current()?
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("llama-swap is not installed; run `ai-ctl gateway install`"))?;
        Ok(Target {
            config: prepared.config.to_yaml(),
            prepared,
            release,
            binary: self.releases.binary(release),
        })
    }

    /// Whether the gateway of `record` runs on `target`.
    fn is_current(&self, record: &GatewayRecord, target: &Target) -> Result<bool> {
        Ok(record.binary == target.binary
            && record.listen == target.prepared.listen
            && self.processes.config()?.as_deref() == Some(target.config.as_str()))
    }

    /// The recorded gateway when it runs; a record whose process is gone is
    /// removed.
    fn running(&self, events: &mut dyn FnMut(Event)) -> Result<Option<GatewayRecord>> {
        let Some(record) = self.processes.record()? else {
            return Ok(None);
        };
        match self.processes.liveness(&record)? {
            Liveness::Running => Ok(Some(record)),
            liveness => {
                self.processes.forget()?;
                events(Event::Forgotten {
                    pid: record.pid,
                    liveness,
                });
                Ok(None)
            }
        }
    }

    /// The GPU; `None`, with a warning ending in `consequence`, when it
    /// cannot be read.
    fn read_gpu(&self, consequence: &str, events: &mut dyn FnMut(Event)) -> Option<Gpu> {
        match self.configurator.gpu.gpu() {
            Probed::Found(gpu) => Some(gpu),
            Probed::Missing => {
                events(Event::Warning(format!(
                    "no NVIDIA GPU found; {consequence}"
                )));
                None
            }
            Probed::Failed(err) => {
                events(Event::Warning(format!(
                    "cannot read the GPU ({err}); {consequence}"
                )));
                None
            }
        }
    }

    /// The processes of the gateway `gateway` — llama-swap, its descendants
    /// and the processes of ai-ctl's containers, which run under conmon —
    /// and those of the containers alone. A list that cannot be read is
    /// left out, with a warning.
    fn owners(&self, gateway: Option<u32>, events: &mut dyn FnMut(Event)) -> Owners {
        let roots = self
            .containers
            .pids(CONTAINER_PREFIX)
            .unwrap_or_else(|err| {
                events(Event::Warning(format!(
                    "cannot list ai-ctl's containers ({err:#}); leaving their processes out"
                )));
                Vec::new()
            });
        let mut family = |root: u32| {
            let mut family = BTreeSet::from([root]);
            match self.processes.descendants(root) {
                Ok(descendants) => family.extend(descendants),
                Err(err) => events(Event::Warning(format!(
                    "cannot list the processes of PID {root} ({err:#}); leaving them out"
                ))),
            }
            family
        };
        let containers: BTreeSet<u32> = roots.into_iter().flat_map(&mut family).collect();
        let gateway = match gateway {
            Some(pid) => {
                let mut ours = family(pid);
                ours.extend(&containers);
                ours
            }
            None => BTreeSet::new(),
        };
        Owners {
            gateway,
            containers,
        }
    }

    /// The processes on the GPU that a gateway left behind: the programs of
    /// the build store the running gateway does not own, and, while no
    /// gateway runs, the processes of ai-ctl's containers.
    fn leftovers(
        &self,
        gpu: &Gpu,
        owners: &Owners,
        events: &mut dyn FnMut(Event),
    ) -> Vec<GpuProcess> {
        let mut from_a_build = |pid: u32| match self.processes.program(pid) {
            Ok(program) => {
                program.is_some_and(|program| self.configurator.builds.contains(&program))
            }
            Err(err) => {
                events(Event::Warning(format!(
                    "cannot read the program of PID {pid} ({err:#}); leaving it out"
                )));
                false
            }
        };
        gpu.processes
            .iter()
            .filter(|process| {
                !owners.gateway.contains(&process.pid)
                    && (owners.containers.contains(&process.pid) || from_a_build(process.pid))
            })
            .cloned()
            .collect()
    }

    /// Polls the GPU until none of `held` is on it any more.
    fn wait_for_release(
        &self,
        held: Vec<GpuProcess>,
        events: &mut dyn FnMut(Event),
    ) -> Result<Vec<GpuProcess>> {
        if held.is_empty() {
            return Ok(held);
        }
        events(Event::Releasing {
            processes: held.clone(),
        });
        let mut left = Vec::new();
        for _ in 0..RELEASE_POLLS {
            let gpu = match self.configurator.gpu.gpu() {
                Probed::Found(gpu) => gpu,
                Probed::Missing => bail!("the GPU disappeared while its memory was released"),
                Probed::Failed(err) => {
                    bail!("cannot read the GPU while its memory is released: {err}")
                }
            };
            left = held
                .iter()
                .filter(|process| gpu.processes.iter().any(|on| on.pid == process.pid))
                .cloned()
                .collect();
            if left.is_empty() {
                return Ok(held);
            }
            (self.pause)(POLL);
        }
        let (named, pids) = named(&left);
        bail!(
            "the gateway stopped, but its processes still hold GPU memory after {} s: {named}; \
             ai-ctl never stops a backend itself: end them with `kill {pids}`",
            POLL.as_millis() * u128::from(RELEASE_POLLS) / 1000,
        )
    }

    /// Polls the started gateway until it answers. When it exits first, or
    /// never answers and is stopped, its record goes and the error quotes
    /// its log.
    fn wait_until_healthy(
        &self,
        started: &mut dyn StartedGateway,
        listen: SocketAddr,
        events: &mut dyn FnMut(Event),
    ) -> Result<()> {
        let mut last_error = None;
        for _ in 0..START_POLLS {
            if let Some(status) = started.exited()? {
                self.forget(events);
                bail!(
                    "llama-swap exited ({status}) before answering on {listen}{}",
                    self.tail()
                );
            }
            match self.api.healthy(listen) {
                Ok(true) => return Ok(()),
                Ok(false) => {}
                Err(err) => last_error = Some(err),
            }
            (self.pause)(POLL);
        }
        let pid = started.pid();
        let stopped = self.stop(pid, &mut || Ok(started.exited()?.is_some()), events);
        self.forget(events);
        let stopped = match stopped {
            Ok(()) => "stopped it".to_owned(),
            Err(err) => format!("could not stop it: {err:#}"),
        };
        let cause = last_error.map_or_else(String::new, |err| format!(" (last error: {err:#})"));
        bail!(
            "llama-swap did not answer on {listen} within {} s{cause}; {stopped}{}",
            POLL.as_millis() * u128::from(START_POLLS) / 1000,
            self.tail()
        )
    }

    /// Stops the gateway `pid`: SIGTERM, then SIGKILL when it outlives
    /// llama-swap's own shutdown; `gone` tells whether it has ended.
    fn stop(
        &self,
        pid: u32,
        gone: &mut dyn FnMut() -> Result<bool>,
        events: &mut dyn FnMut(Event),
    ) -> Result<()> {
        for (signal, polls) in [(Signal::Terminate, STOP_POLLS), (Signal::Kill, KILL_POLLS)] {
            if signal == Signal::Kill {
                events(Event::Killing { pid });
            }
            self.processes.signal(pid, signal)?;
            for _ in 0..polls {
                if gone()? {
                    return Ok(());
                }
                (self.pause)(POLL);
            }
        }
        bail!("PID {pid} still runs after SIGKILL")
    }

    /// Removes the record of a gateway that no longer runs; a failure is
    /// reported, since the error being raised matters more.
    fn forget(&self, events: &mut dyn FnMut(Event)) {
        if let Err(err) = self.processes.forget() {
            events(Event::Warning(format!("{err:#}")));
        }
    }

    /// The end of the log, for an error message.
    fn tail(&self) -> String {
        let log = self.processes.log();
        match self.processes.log_tail(TAIL) {
            Ok(tail) if !tail.trim().is_empty() => {
                format!(
                    "; the last lines of {}:\n{}",
                    log.display(),
                    tail.trim_end()
                )
            }
            Ok(_) => format!("; {} is empty", log.display()),
            Err(err) => format!("; cannot read {}: {err:#}", log.display()),
        }
    }
}

/// The processes on the GPU of `gpu` that `owners` gives the gateway.
fn held(gpu: &Gpu, owners: &Owners) -> Vec<GpuProcess> {
    gpu.processes
        .iter()
        .filter(|process| owners.gateway.contains(&process.pid))
        .cloned()
        .collect()
}

/// The GPU memory `processes` hold, in MiB, as far as the driver reports it.
pub fn mib(processes: &[GpuProcess]) -> u64 {
    processes
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|process| process.memory)
        .sum::<u64>()
        >> 20
}

/// `processes` named for a message, `PID 5000 (llama-server), …`, and their
/// PIDs, for `kill`.
fn named(processes: &[GpuProcess]) -> (String, String) {
    let named: Vec<String> = processes
        .iter()
        .map(|process| format!("PID {} ({})", process.pid, process.name))
        .collect();
    let pids: Vec<String> = processes
        .iter()
        .map(|process| process.pid.to_string())
        .collect();
    (named.join(", "), pids.join(" "))
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use std::collections::BTreeMap;

    use super::*;
    use crate::application::fakes::{
        FakeApi, FakeBuilds, FakeContainers, FakeInventory, FakeProcesses, FakeReleases, Logged,
        ScriptedGpu, gpu, gpu_running, inventory, inventory_parts, listen, record,
    };
    use crate::domain::inventory::{Backend, BackendKind, BackendSource, Inventory};

    struct World {
        inventory: FakeInventory,
        builds: FakeBuilds,
        gpu: ScriptedGpu,
        releases: FakeReleases,
        processes: FakeProcesses,
        api: FakeApi,
        containers: FakeContainers,
    }

    impl World {
        fn new() -> Self {
            Self {
                inventory: FakeInventory(Ok(Some(inventory("/models")))),
                builds: FakeBuilds::current_b11538(),
                gpu: ScriptedGpu::new(vec![Probed::Found(gpu())]),
                releases: FakeReleases::current("263"),
                processes: FakeProcesses::default(),
                api: FakeApi::answering_after(2),
                containers: FakeContainers::default(),
            }
        }

        fn lifecycle(&self) -> Lifecycle<'_> {
            Lifecycle {
                configurator: Configurator {
                    inventory: &self.inventory,
                    builds: &self.builds,
                    gpu: &self.gpu,
                },
                releases: &self.releases,
                processes: &self.processes,
                api: &self.api,
                containers: &self.containers,
                pause: |_| {},
            }
        }

        fn up(&self) -> (Result<Up>, Vec<Event>) {
            let mut events = Vec::new();
            let result = self.lifecycle().up(&mut |event| events.push(event));
            (result, events)
        }

        fn down(&self) -> (Result<Down>, Vec<Event>) {
            let mut events = Vec::new();
            let result = self.lifecycle().down(&mut |event| events.push(event));
            (result, events)
        }

        fn status(&self) -> (Result<Status>, Vec<Event>) {
            let mut events = Vec::new();
            let result = self.lifecycle().status(&mut |event| events.push(event));
            (result, events)
        }

        /// What `log` printed, and how it ended.
        fn log(&self, follow: bool) -> (Result<LogEnd>, String) {
            let mut out = Vec::new();
            let result = self.lifecycle().log(follow, &mut out);
            (result, String::from_utf8(out).unwrap())
        }

        /// The configuration `up` writes.
        fn config(&self) -> String {
            Configurator {
                inventory: &self.inventory,
                builds: &self.builds,
                gpu: &self.gpu,
            }
            .generate()
            .unwrap()
            .to_yaml()
        }
    }

    fn release() -> Release {
        Release::parse("263").unwrap()
    }

    fn message<T: std::fmt::Debug>(result: Result<T>) -> String {
        format!("{:#}", result.unwrap_err())
    }

    #[test]
    fn up_starts_the_gateway_and_returns_once_it_answers() {
        let world = World::new();
        let (result, events) = world.up();
        assert_eq!(
            result.unwrap(),
            Up::Started {
                pid: 4242,
                release: release(),
                listen: listen(),
                log: "/state/logs/gateway.log".into(),
                preload: vec!["qwen3.8-27b".into()],
            }
        );
        assert_eq!(
            events,
            [Event::Starting {
                release: release(),
                listen: listen(),
            }]
        );
        assert_eq!(world.api.calls.get(), 3);
        assert_eq!(
            world.processes.config.borrow().as_deref(),
            Some(world.config().as_str())
        );
        assert_eq!(
            world.processes.record.borrow().as_ref(),
            Some(&record(4242))
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn up_needs_an_installed_llama_swap() {
        let mut world = World::new();
        world.releases = FakeReleases::none();
        assert_eq!(
            message(world.up().0),
            "llama-swap is not installed; run `ai-ctl gateway install`"
        );
        assert_eq!(world.processes.started.get(), 0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn up_needs_an_inventory() {
        let mut world = World::new();
        world.inventory = FakeInventory(Ok(None));
        assert!(message(world.up().0).starts_with("no inventory at "));
        assert_eq!(world.processes.started.get(), 0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_gateway_running_on_the_same_configuration_is_left_alone() {
        let world = World::new();
        *world.processes.record.borrow_mut() = Some(record(1234));
        *world.processes.config.borrow_mut() = Some(world.config());
        let (result, events) = world.up();
        assert_eq!(
            result.unwrap(),
            Up::Running {
                pid: 1234,
                listen: listen(),
            }
        );
        assert!(events.is_empty());
        assert_eq!(world.processes.started.get(), 0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_gateway_running_on_another_configuration_is_not_restarted() {
        let cases: [fn(&World); 3] = [
            |world| *world.processes.config.borrow_mut() = Some("models: {}\n".into()),
            |world| {
                world.processes.record.borrow_mut().as_mut().unwrap().listen =
                    "127.0.0.1:8081".parse().unwrap();
            },
            |world| {
                world.processes.record.borrow_mut().as_mut().unwrap().binary =
                    "/data/llama-swap/v262/llama-swap".into();
            },
        ];
        for change in cases {
            let world = World::new();
            *world.processes.record.borrow_mut() = Some(record(1234));
            *world.processes.config.borrow_mut() = Some(world.config());
            change(&world);
            assert_eq!(
                message(world.up().0),
                "the gateway (PID 1234) runs on another configuration, address or release than \
                 the inventory, the builds and llama-swap give now; \
                 run `ai-ctl down`, then `ai-ctl up`"
            );
            assert_eq!(world.processes.started.get(), 0);
            assert!(world.processes.signals.borrow().is_empty());
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_stale_record_is_removed_before_starting() {
        for liveness in [
            Liveness::Exited,
            Liveness::Reused(Some("/usr/bin/bash".into())),
            Liveness::Reused(None),
        ] {
            let mut world = World::new();
            *world.processes.record.borrow_mut() = Some(record(1234));
            world.processes.liveness = Some(liveness.clone());
            let (result, events) = world.up();
            assert!(matches!(result.unwrap(), Up::Started { pid: 4242, .. }));
            assert_eq!(
                events[0],
                Event::Forgotten {
                    pid: 1234,
                    liveness
                }
            );
            assert_eq!(world.processes.started.get(), 1);
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn up_refuses_a_taken_address() {
        let mut world = World::new();
        world.processes.taken = true;
        assert_eq!(
            message(world.up().0),
            "127.0.0.1:8080 is taken by another program; stop it, or give the gateway another \
             `listen` address in the inventory"
        );
        assert_eq!(world.processes.started.get(), 0);
    }

    fn on_images(images: &[&str]) -> World {
        let mut parts = inventory_parts("/models");
        parts.backends.push(Backend {
            id: "llama-cuda13".into(),
            kind: BackendKind::LlamaServer,
            source: BackendSource::Image("ghcr.io/ggml-org/llama.cpp:server-cuda13".into()),
        });
        parts.models[0].backend = "llama-cuda13".into();
        let mut world = World::new();
        world.inventory = FakeInventory(Ok(Some(Inventory::new(parts).unwrap())));
        world.containers.local = images.iter().map(|&image| image.to_owned()).collect();
        world
    }

    #[test]
    fn up_pulls_a_missing_image_before_starting() {
        let world = on_images(&[]);
        let (result, events) = world.up();
        result.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            world.containers.pulled.borrow().as_slice(),
            ["ghcr.io/ggml-org/llama.cpp:server-cuda13"]
        );
        assert_eq!(
            events[0],
            Event::Pulling {
                image: "ghcr.io/ggml-org/llama.cpp:server-cuda13".into()
            }
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn up_leaves_a_local_image_alone() {
        let world = on_images(&["ghcr.io/ggml-org/llama.cpp:server-cuda13"]);
        world.up().0.unwrap();
        assert!(world.containers.pulled.borrow().is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_gateway_exiting_before_it_answers_is_forgotten_and_its_log_quoted() {
        let mut world = World::new();
        world.api = FakeApi::silent();
        world.processes.exits_after = Some(3);
        world.processes.tail = "ERROR http server error: bind: address already in use\n".into();
        let message = message(world.up().0);
        assert_eq!(
            message,
            "llama-swap exited (exit status: 1) before answering on 127.0.0.1:8080; \
             the last lines of /state/logs/gateway.log:\n\
             ERROR http server error: bind: address already in use"
        );
        assert_eq!(world.processes.record.borrow().as_ref(), None);
        assert!(world.processes.signals.borrow().is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_gateway_that_never_answers_is_stopped_and_forgotten() {
        let mut world = World::new();
        world.api = FakeApi::silent();
        let message = message(world.up().0);
        assert_eq!(
            message,
            "llama-swap did not answer on 127.0.0.1:8080 within 30 s; stopped it; \
             /state/logs/gateway.log is empty"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            world.processes.signals.borrow().as_slice(),
            [Signal::Terminate]
        );
        assert_eq!(world.processes.record.borrow().as_ref(), None);
        assert_eq!(world.api.calls.get(), START_POLLS);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_gateway_that_outlives_sigterm_is_killed() {
        let mut world = World::new();
        world.api = FakeApi::silent();
        world.processes.stubborn = true;
        assert!(message(world.up().0).contains("; stopped it;"));
        assert_eq!(
            world.processes.signals.borrow().as_slice(),
            [Signal::Terminate, Signal::Kill]
        );
    }

    /// A world whose gateway, PID 1234, runs the model PID 5000, with Xorg
    /// beside it on the GPU; the model leaves after `stays` more readings.
    fn serving(stays: usize) -> World {
        let mut world = World::new();
        *world.processes.record.borrow_mut() = Some(record(1234));
        world.processes.descendants = vec![5000];
        let both = gpu_running(&[(777, "Xorg", 50), (5000, "llama-server", 21094)]);
        let mut readings = vec![both.clone(); stays + 1];
        readings.push(gpu_running(&[(777, "Xorg", 50)]));
        world.gpu = ScriptedGpu::new(readings);
        world
    }

    fn llama_server(pid: u32) -> GpuProcess {
        GpuProcess {
            pid,
            name: "llama-server".into(),
            memory: Some(21094 << 20),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn down_unloads_stops_and_waits_for_the_gateways_gpu_memory() {
        let world = serving(2);
        let (result, events) = world.down();
        assert_eq!(
            result.unwrap(),
            Down::Stopped {
                pid: 1234,
                released: vec![llama_server(5000)],
            }
        );
        assert_eq!(
            events,
            [
                Event::Unloading,
                Event::Stopping { pid: 1234 },
                Event::Releasing {
                    processes: vec![llama_server(5000)]
                },
            ]
        );
        assert_eq!(world.api.unloads.get(), 1);
        assert_eq!(
            world.processes.signals.borrow().as_slice(),
            [Signal::Terminate]
        );
        assert_eq!(world.processes.record.borrow().as_ref(), None);
        // One reading before the stop, then until the model is gone.
        assert_eq!(world.gpu.reads.get(), 4);
    }

    #[test]
    fn down_waits_for_the_processes_of_the_gateways_containers() {
        let mut world = World::new();
        *world.processes.record.borrow_mut() = Some(record(1234));
        world.containers.running = vec![6000];
        world.gpu = ScriptedGpu::new(vec![
            gpu_running(&[(777, "Xorg", 50), (6000, "llama-server", 21094)]),
            gpu_running(&[(777, "Xorg", 50)]),
        ]);
        let (result, _) = world.down();
        assert_eq!(
            result.unwrap(),
            Down::Stopped {
                pid: 1234,
                released: vec![llama_server(6000)],
            }
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn down_without_a_gateway_does_nothing() {
        let world = World::new();
        let (result, events) = world.down();
        assert_eq!(result.unwrap(), Down::NotRunning);
        assert!(events.is_empty());
        assert_eq!(world.api.unloads.get(), 0);
        assert!(world.processes.signals.borrow().is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn down_removes_a_stale_record_and_signals_nothing() {
        let mut world = World::new();
        *world.processes.record.borrow_mut() = Some(record(1234));
        world.processes.liveness = Some(Liveness::Reused(Some("/usr/bin/bash".into())));
        let (result, events) = world.down();
        assert_eq!(result.unwrap(), Down::NotRunning);
        assert_eq!(
            events,
            [Event::Forgotten {
                pid: 1234,
                liveness: Liveness::Reused(Some("/usr/bin/bash".into())),
            }]
        );
        assert!(world.processes.signals.borrow().is_empty());
        assert_eq!(world.processes.record.borrow().as_ref(), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_failed_unload_still_stops_the_gateway() {
        let mut world = serving(0);
        world.api.unload_fails = true;
        let (result, events) = world.down();
        assert!(matches!(result.unwrap(), Down::Stopped { .. }));
        assert_eq!(
            events[1],
            Event::Warning(
                "cannot unload the models (connection reset); stopping the gateway stops them"
                    .into()
            )
        );
        assert_eq!(
            world.processes.signals.borrow().as_slice(),
            [Signal::Terminate]
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_gateway_that_outlives_sigterm_is_killed_by_down() {
        let mut world = serving(0);
        world.processes.stubborn = true;
        let (result, events) = world.down();
        assert!(matches!(result.unwrap(), Down::Stopped { .. }));
        assert!(events.contains(&Event::Killing { pid: 1234 }));
        assert_eq!(
            world.processes.signals.borrow().as_slice(),
            [Signal::Terminate, Signal::Kill]
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_gateway_that_outlives_sigkill_keeps_its_record() {
        let mut world = serving(0);
        world.processes.immortal = true;
        assert_eq!(message(world.down().0), "PID 1234 still runs after SIGKILL");
        assert_eq!(
            world.processes.record.borrow().as_ref(),
            Some(&record(1234))
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_process_left_on_the_gpu_is_named_not_killed() {
        let world = serving(usize::try_from(RELEASE_POLLS).unwrap() + 5);
        assert_eq!(
            message(world.down().0),
            "the gateway stopped, but its processes still hold GPU memory after 30 s: \
             PID 5000 (llama-server); ai-ctl never stops a backend itself: end them with `kill 5000`"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            world.processes.signals.borrow().as_slice(),
            [Signal::Terminate]
        );
        assert_eq!(world.processes.record.borrow().as_ref(), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn down_stops_the_gateway_even_when_the_gpu_cannot_be_read() {
        let mut world = serving(0);
        world.gpu = ScriptedGpu::new(vec![Probed::Failed("NVML: driver not loaded".into())]);
        let (result, events) = world.down();
        assert_eq!(
            result.unwrap(),
            Down::Stopped {
                pid: 1234,
                released: vec![],
            }
        );
        assert_eq!(
            events[0],
            Event::Warning(
                "cannot read the GPU (NVML: driver not loaded); not waiting for its memory".into()
            )
        );
    }

    /// A native model of the build store.
    const BUILD: &str = "/data/builds/llama.cpp/b11538/llama-server";

    fn model(id: &str, state: &str) -> RunningModel {
        RunningModel {
            id: id.into(),
            state: state.into(),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn status_reports_the_gateway_its_models_and_its_gpu_memory() {
        // Generating the configuration below reads the GPU once.
        let mut world = serving(1);
        *world.processes.config.borrow_mut() = Some(world.config());
        world.api.models = Some(vec![model("qwen3.8-27b", "ready")]);
        let (result, events) = world.status();
        assert_eq!(
            result.unwrap(),
            Status {
                gateway: Some(Serving {
                    pid: 1234,
                    listen: listen(),
                    models: Some(vec![model("qwen3.8-27b", "ready")]),
                    current: Some(true),
                    held: vec![llama_server(5000)],
                }),
                gpu: Some(GpuMemory {
                    used_mib: 16,
                    usable_mib: 24075,
                }),
                leftovers: vec![],
            }
        );
        assert!(events.is_empty());
        assert!(world.processes.signals.borrow().is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn status_reports_a_gateway_killed_outside_ai_ctl_as_stopped_and_removes_its_record() {
        let mut world = World::new();
        *world.processes.record.borrow_mut() = Some(record(1234));
        world.processes.liveness = Some(Liveness::Exited);
        let (result, events) = world.status();
        let status = result.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(status.gateway, None);
        assert_eq!(
            events,
            [Event::Forgotten {
                pid: 1234,
                liveness: Liveness::Exited,
            }]
        );
        assert_eq!(world.processes.record.borrow().as_ref(), None);
        assert_eq!(world.api.calls.get(), 0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn inspecting_reports_a_stale_record_and_leaves_it() {
        let mut world = World::new();
        *world.processes.record.borrow_mut() = Some(record(1234));
        world.processes.liveness = Some(Liveness::Reused(Some("/usr/bin/bash".into())));
        let mut events = Vec::new();
        let inspection = world
            .lifecycle()
            .inspect(&mut |event| events.push(event))
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(inspection.status.gateway, None);
        assert_eq!(
            inspection.stale,
            Some((1234, Liveness::Reused(Some("/usr/bin/bash".into()))))
        );
        assert!(events.is_empty());
        assert_eq!(
            world.processes.record.borrow().as_ref(),
            Some(&record(1234))
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn status_tells_a_gateway_running_on_an_older_configuration() {
        let world = serving(0);
        *world.processes.config.borrow_mut() = Some("models: {}\n".into());
        let gateway = world.status().0.unwrap().gateway.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(gateway.current, Some(false));
    }

    #[test]
    fn status_without_the_gateways_api_or_the_gpu_still_reports_it_running() {
        let mut world = serving(0);
        world.api.models = None;
        world.gpu = ScriptedGpu::new(vec![Probed::Failed("NVML: driver not loaded".into())]);
        let (result, events) = world.status();
        let status = result.unwrap();
        let gateway = status.gateway.unwrap();
        assert_eq!((gateway.pid, gateway.models), (1234, None));
        assert!(gateway.held.is_empty());
        assert_eq!(status.gpu, None);
        assert_eq!(
            events,
            [
                Event::Warning(
                    "cannot read the GPU (NVML: driver not loaded); its memory is left out".into()
                ),
                Event::Warning(
                    "cannot list the gateway's models \
                     (GET http://127.0.0.1:8080/running: timed out)"
                        .into()
                ),
                Event::Warning(
                    "cannot tell whether the gateway runs on the configuration the inventory \
                     gives (cannot read the GPU's memory: NVML: driver not loaded)"
                        .into()
                ),
            ]
        );
    }

    /// No gateway runs; on the GPU, Xorg, a native model and a container's
    /// process a killed gateway left.
    fn left_behind() -> World {
        let mut world = World::new();
        world.processes.programs = BTreeMap::from([
            (777, "/usr/bin/Xorg".into()),
            (5000, BUILD.into()),
            (6000, "/app/llama-server".into()),
        ]);
        world.containers.running = vec![6000];
        world.gpu = ScriptedGpu::new(vec![gpu_running(&[
            (777, "Xorg", 50),
            (5000, "llama-server", 21094),
            (6000, "llama-server", 21094),
        ])]);
        world
    }

    #[test]
    fn status_lists_the_processes_a_killed_gateway_left_on_the_gpu() {
        let world = left_behind();
        let status = world.status().0.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(status.gateway, None);
        assert_eq!(status.leftovers, [llama_server(5000), llama_server(6000)]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_removed_build_still_running_is_left_behind_too() {
        let mut world = left_behind();
        world
            .processes
            .programs
            .insert(5000, format!("{BUILD} (deleted)").into());
        assert!(
            world
                .status()
                .0
                .unwrap()
                .leftovers
                .contains(&llama_server(5000))
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn beside_a_running_gateway_only_another_gateways_build_is_left_behind() {
        let mut world = left_behind();
        *world.processes.record.borrow_mut() = Some(record(1234));
        world.processes.descendants = vec![5000];
        world.processes.programs.insert(5100, BUILD.into());
        world.gpu = ScriptedGpu::new(vec![gpu_running(&[
            (5000, "llama-server", 21094),
            (5100, "llama-server", 21094),
            (6000, "llama-server", 21094),
        ])]);
        let status = world.status().0.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(status.leftovers, [llama_server(5100)]);
        assert_eq!(
            status.gateway.unwrap().held,
            [llama_server(5000), llama_server(6000)]
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn up_refuses_to_start_beside_processes_a_killed_gateway_left() {
        let world = left_behind();
        assert_eq!(
            message(world.up().0),
            "processes an earlier gateway left on the GPU hold 42188 MiB of its memory: \
             PID 5000 (llama-server), PID 6000 (llama-server); a model started now would not \
             find the memory the inventory gives it, and ai-ctl never stops a backend itself: \
             end them with `kill 5000 6000`, then run `ai-ctl up` again"
        );
        assert_eq!(world.processes.started.get(), 0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_log_is_printed_whole() {
        let world = World::new();
        *world.processes.log.borrow_mut() = Some(b"[INFO] llama-swap listening\n".to_vec());
        let (result, out) = world.log(false);
        assert_eq!(result.unwrap(), LogEnd::Printed);
        assert_eq!(out, "[INFO] llama-swap listening\n");
    }

    #[test]
    fn no_log_is_an_error_naming_up() {
        assert_eq!(
            message(World::new().log(false).0),
            "no gateway log at /state/logs/gateway.log: the gateway has not run yet; \
             start it with `ai-ctl up`"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn following_without_a_gateway_prints_the_log_and_ends() {
        let world = World::new();
        *world.processes.log.borrow_mut() = Some(b"[INFO] shutdown complete\n".to_vec());
        let (result, out) = world.log(true);
        assert_eq!(result.unwrap(), LogEnd::NoGateway);
        assert_eq!(out, "[INFO] shutdown complete\n");
    }

    fn writing(changes: Vec<Logged>) -> World {
        let mut world = World::new();
        *world.processes.record.borrow_mut() = Some(record(1234));
        *world.processes.log.borrow_mut() = Some(b"one\n".to_vec());
        world.processes.writes = true;
        *world.processes.writing.borrow_mut() = changes.into();
        world
    }

    #[test]
    fn following_prints_what_the_gateway_writes_until_it_stops() {
        let world = writing(vec![
            Logged::Appends("two\n"),
            Logged::Appends("three\n"),
            Logged::Appends("[INFO] shutdown complete\n"),
        ]);
        let (result, out) = world.log(true);
        assert_eq!(result.unwrap(), LogEnd::Stopped);
        assert_eq!(out, "one\ntwo\nthree\n[INFO] shutdown complete\n");
    }

    #[test]
    fn following_ends_when_a_new_log_replaces_the_old_one() {
        let world = writing(vec![
            Logged::Appends("two\n"),
            Logged::Appends("three\n"),
            Logged::Replaces("x\n"),
            Logged::Appends("never read\n"),
        ]);
        let (result, out) = world.log(true);
        assert_eq!(result.unwrap(), LogEnd::Replaced);
        assert_eq!(out, "one\ntwo\nthree\n");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_models_log_streams_from_the_running_gateway() {
        let world = World::new();
        *world.processes.record.borrow_mut() = Some(record(1234));
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        world
            .lifecycle()
            .model_log("qwen3.8-27b", &mut out)
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(out, b"srv  load_model: loading model\n");
        assert_eq!(
            world.api.streamed.borrow().as_slice(),
            [(listen(), "qwen3.8-27b".to_owned())]
        );
    }

    #[test]
