    fn a_models_log_needs_a_running_gateway_and_a_model_id() {
        let world = World::new();
        *world.processes.record.borrow_mut() = Some(record(1234));
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        assert_eq!(
            message(world.lifecycle().model_log("../health", &mut out)),
            "\"../health\" is not a model ID: an ID starts with a letter or a digit and holds \
             only ASCII letters, digits, `.`, `_` and `-`"
        );
        let mut world = World::new();
        *world.processes.record.borrow_mut() = Some(record(1234));
        world.processes.liveness = Some(Liveness::Exited);
        assert!(
            message(world.lifecycle().model_log("qwen3.8-27b", &mut out))
                .starts_with("no gateway runs, and a model's output is kept by the gateway")
        );
        assert!(world.api.streamed.borrow().is_empty());
        assert!(out.is_empty());
    }
}
</file>

<file path="src/application/build.rs">
//! `build`: compiles a project at a git reference into a new installed
//! build, and manages the installed builds (`list`, `use`, `prune`).

use std::fmt;
use std::path::PathBuf;

use anyhow::{Context, Result, anyhow, bail, ensure};

use crate::domain::build::{Build, BuildId, CommitId, cmake_flags, to_prune};
use crate::domain::diagnostics::{Check, Status};
use crate::domain::recipe::{RECIPES, Recipe, cuda_devices, recipe};
use crate::domain::timestamp::Timestamp;

use super::doctor::gpu_checks;
use super::ports::{
    BuildStore, Cmake, CompileJob, GpuProbe, HostProbe, ProgramOutput, Programs, Progress, Sources,
    Unreadable,
};
use super::toolchain;

/// The reference that names a project's newest release.
pub const LATEST: &str = "latest";

/// What to build.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Request<'a> {
    pub project: &'a str,
    /// A tag, a branch or a commit; `None` or [`LATEST`] for the newest
    /// release.
    pub reference: Option<&'a str>,
    /// Rebuild a build that is already installed.
    pub force: bool,
    /// Parallel compile jobs; `None` for one per processor.
    pub jobs: Option<usize>,
}

/// A step of a build, for the user to follow.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Event {
    /// Looking up the newest release tag of the project's repository.
    Resolving,
    /// Cloning or fetching the sources, to check out `reference`.
    Fetching {
        reference: String,
    },
    CheckedOut {
        id: BuildId,
        commit: CommitId,
    },
    Compile(Progress),
    /// Running the new binary.
    Testing,
    /// Something went wrong that does not fail the build.
    Warning(String),
}

/// A build installed and made current.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Built {
    pub build: Build,
    pub binary: PathBuf,
    /// The build that was current before, if another one.
    pub previous: Option<BuildId>,
}

/// The checks that refused a build, with their remedies.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Refused(pub Vec<Check>);

impl fmt::Display for Refused {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "the build is refused; `ai-ctl doctor` shows every check")?;
        for check in &self.0 {
            write!(f, "\n  {}: {}", check.name, check.detail)?;
            for (i, line) in check.remedy.iter().flat_map(|r| r.lines()).enumerate() {
                let label = if i == 0 { "fix:" } else { "    " };
                write!(f, "\n    {label} {line}")?;
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for Refused {}

/// The build use case, with the ports it drives.
pub struct Builder<'a> {
    pub gpu: &'a dyn GpuProbe,
    pub host: &'a dyn HostProbe,
    pub sources: &'a dyn Sources,
    pub cmake: &'a dyn Cmake,
    pub store: &'a dyn BuildStore,
    pub programs: &'a dyn Programs,
    pub clock: &'a dyn Fn() -> Timestamp,
}

impl Builder<'_> {
    /// Builds `request.project` at `request.reference`, smoke-tests the
    /// binary, installs it and makes it current. Nothing is installed when
    /// any step fails.
    pub fn build(&self, request: &Request, events: &mut dyn FnMut(Event)) -> Result<Built> {
        let recipe = known_recipe(request.project)?;
        let _lock = self.store.lock()?;

        // The gate: `doctor`'s GPU and Toolchain checks.
        let gpu = self.gpu.gpu();
        let mut refused: Vec<Check> = gpu_checks(&gpu, &[]).into_iter().take(1).collect();
        let inspection = toolchain::inspect(self.host, gpu.found());
        refused.extend(inspection.checks);
        refused.retain(|check| check.status == Status::Fail);
        let toolchain = match inspection.toolchain {
            Some(toolchain) if refused.is_empty() => toolchain,
            _ => return Err(Refused(refused).into()),
        };

        let reference = match request.reference {
            None | Some(LATEST) => {
                events(Event::Resolving);
                self.latest_release(recipe)?
            }
            Some(reference) => reference.to_owned(),
        };
        events(Event::Fetching {
            reference: reference.clone(),
        });
        let checkout = self.sources.checkout(recipe, &reference)?;
        let id = match recipe.tags.newest(checkout.tags.iter().map(String::as_str)) {
            Some(tag) => BuildId::parse(tag)?,
            None => BuildId::from_commit(&checkout.commit),
        };
        events(Event::CheckedOut {
            id: id.clone(),
            commit: checkout.commit.clone(),
        });

        let (installed, unreadable) = self.store.builds(recipe.project)?;
        let taken = installed.iter().any(|build| build.id == id)
            || unreadable.iter().any(|entry| {
                entry
                    .dir
                    .file_name()
                    .is_some_and(|name| name == id.as_str())
            });
        if taken && !request.force {
            let project = recipe.project;
            if self.store.current(project)?.as_ref() == Some(&id) {
                bail!("{project} {id} is already built and current; `--force` builds it again");
            }
            bail!(
                "{project} {id} is already built; `ai-ctl build use {project} {id}` makes it current, `--force` builds it again"
            );
        }

        let flags = cmake_flags(recipe, &toolchain);
        let job = CompileJob {
            project: recipe.project,
            source: &checkout.dir,
            flags: &flags,
            target: recipe.target,
            artifact: recipe.artifact,
            jobs: request.jobs,
        };
        let compiled = self
            .cmake
            .compile(&job, &mut |progress| events(Event::Compile(progress)))?;

        let build = Build {
            project: recipe.project.to_owned(),
            id: id.clone(),
            commit: checkout.commit,
            toolkit: toolchain.toolkit(),
            host_compiler: toolchain.compiler().clone(),
            cmake_flags: flags,
            built_at: (self.clock)(),
        };
        let staged = self
            .store
            .stage(&build, &compiled.artifact, &compiled.log)?;
        events(Event::Testing);
        let installed = self
            .smoke_test(recipe, &staged.binary)
            .and_then(|()| self.store.publish(&staged, request.force));
        if let Err(err) = installed {
            if let Err(discard) = self.store.discard(&staged) {
                events(Event::Warning(format!("{discard:#}")));
            }
            return Err(err);
        }
        if let Err(err) = self.cmake.clean(recipe.project) {
            events(Event::Warning(format!("{err:#}")));
        }

        let previous = self.store.current(recipe.project)?;
        self.store
            .set_current(recipe.project, &id)
            .with_context(|| format!("{} {id} is installed but not current", recipe.project))?;
        Ok(Built {
            binary: self.store.binary(recipe.project, &id, recipe.binary()),
            build,
            previous: previous.filter(|previous| *previous != id),
        })
    }

    /// The newest release tag of the project's repository.
    fn latest_release(&self, recipe: &Recipe) -> Result<String> {
        let tags = self.sources.release_tags(recipe)?;
        recipe
            .tags
            .newest(tags.iter().map(String::as_str))
            .map(str::to_owned)
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                anyhow!(
                    "no release tag of {} matches {}",
                    recipe.repository,
                    recipe.tags.ls_remote_pattern()
                )
            })
    }

    /// The binary runs, and lists a CUDA device when the recipe says how.
    fn smoke_test(&self, recipe: &Recipe, binary: &std::path::Path) -> Result<()> {
        let name = recipe.binary();
        let version = self.programs.run(binary, &["--version"])?;
        ensure!(
            version.success,
            "the new {name} does not run: `{name} --version` failed: {}",
            summary(&version)
        );
        if let Some(listing) = recipe.device_listing {
            let command = format!("{name} {}", listing.args.join(" "));
            let output = self.programs.run(binary, listing.args)?;
            ensure!(
                output.success,
                "the new {name} failed `{command}`: {}",
                summary(&output)
            );
            let devices = format!("{}\n{}", output.stdout, output.stderr);
            ensure!(
                !cuda_devices(&devices).is_empty(),
                "the new {name} lists no CUDA device (`{command}`): {}",
                summary(&output)
            );
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

/// The last non-empty lines a program printed, stderr first.
pub(super) fn summary(output: &ProgramOutput) -> String {
    let lines: Vec<&str> = output
        .stderr
        .lines()
        .chain(output.stdout.lines())
        .map(str::trim)
        .filter(|line| !line.is_empty())
        .collect();
    if lines.is_empty() {
        return "it printed nothing".to_owned();
    }
    lines[lines.len().saturating_sub(5)..].join(" | ")
}

fn known_recipe(project: &str) -> Result<&'static Recipe> {
    recipe(project).ok_or_else(|| {
        let known: Vec<&str> = RECIPES.iter().map(|recipe| recipe.project).collect();
        anyhow!(
            "no recipe for `{project}`; ai-ctl builds {}",
            known.join(", ")
        )
    })
}

/// One project's installed builds.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Listing {
    pub project: &'static str,
    /// Newest first.
    pub builds: Vec<Build>,
    pub current: Option<BuildId>,
    pub unreadable: Vec<Unreadable>,
}

/// The installed builds of `project`, or of every project with a recipe.
pub fn list(store: &dyn BuildStore, project: Option<&str>) -> Result<Vec<Listing>> {
    let recipes: Vec<&Recipe> = match project {
        Some(project) => vec![known_recipe(project)?],
        None => RECIPES.iter().collect(),
    };
    recipes
        .into_iter()
        .map(|recipe| {
            let (mut builds, unreadable) = store.builds(recipe.project)?;
            builds.sort_by(|a, b| b.built_at.cmp(&a.built_at).then_with(|| b.id.cmp(&a.id)));
            Ok(Listing {
                project: recipe.project,
                builds,
                current: store.current(recipe.project)?,
                unreadable,
            })
        })
        .collect()
}

/// Makes an installed build current; returns the build that was.
pub fn use_build(store: &dyn BuildStore, project: &str, id: &str) -> Result<Option<BuildId>> {
    let recipe = known_recipe(project)?;
    let id = BuildId::parse(id)?;
    let _lock = store.lock()?;
    let (builds, _) = store.builds(recipe.project)?;
    ensure!(
        builds.iter().any(|build| build.id == id),
        "{} has no build {id}; `ai-ctl build list {}` lists them",
        recipe.project,
        recipe.project
    );
    let previous = store.current(recipe.project)?;
    store.set_current(recipe.project, &id)?;
    Ok(previous.filter(|previous| *previous != id))
}

/// What `prune` did to one project.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Pruned {
    pub project: &'static str,
    pub removed: Vec<BuildId>,
    pub kept: usize,
    /// Directories prune leaves alone, since they are not readable builds.
    pub unreadable: Vec<Unreadable>,
}

/// Removes every build but the `keep` newest and the current one, for
/// `project` or for every project with a recipe.
pub fn prune(store: &dyn BuildStore, project: Option<&str>, keep: usize) -> Result<Vec<Pruned>> {
    let recipes: Vec<&Recipe> = match project {
        Some(project) => vec![known_recipe(project)?],
        None => RECIPES.iter().collect(),
    };
    let _lock = store.lock()?;
    let mut pruned = Vec::new();
    for recipe in recipes {
        let (builds, unreadable) = store.builds(recipe.project)?;
        let current = store.current(recipe.project)?;
        let doomed: Vec<BuildId> = to_prune(&builds, current.as_ref(), keep)
            .into_iter()
            .map(|build| build.id.clone())
            .collect();
        for id in &doomed {
            store
                .remove(recipe.project, id)
                .with_context(|| format!("cannot remove {} {id}", recipe.project))?;
        }
        pruned.push(Pruned {
            project: recipe.project,
            kept: builds.len() - doomed.len(),
            removed: doomed,
            unreadable,
        });
    }
    Ok(pruned)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use std::any::Any;
    use std::cell::{Cell, RefCell};
    use std::collections::BTreeMap;
    use std::path::Path;

    use super::*;
    use crate::application::fakes::{FakeGpu, FakeHost, FakePrograms, gpu, printed};
    use crate::application::ports::{Checkout, Compiled, Staged};
    use crate::domain::machine::{Probed, Version};

    const B11538: &str = "79e2e74eb11022c1ba2e438df7f0ca2d4c10f8b6";
    const FEATURE: &str = "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567";
    const NOW: i64 = 1_791_568_931;

    /// Release tags `b11537` and `b11538`, and a `feature` branch.
    struct FakeSources {
        checkouts: RefCell<Vec<String>>,
    }

    impl Sources for FakeSources {
        fn release_tags(&self, _: &Recipe) -> Result<Vec<String>> {
            Ok(vec![
                "b11537".into(),
                "b10964-b10964-b29c606".into(),
                "b11538".into(),
            ])
        }
        fn checkout(&self, _: &Recipe, reference: &str) -> Result<Checkout> {
            self.checkouts.borrow_mut().push(reference.to_owned());
            let (commit, tags) = match reference {
                "b11538" | "79e2e74" => (B11538, vec!["b11538".to_owned()]),
                "feature" => (FEATURE, vec![]),
                other => bail!("`{other}` is not a tag, a branch or a commit"),
            };
            Ok(Checkout {
                dir: "/data/sources/llama.cpp".into(),
                commit: CommitId::parse(commit)?,
                tags,
            })
        }
    }

    struct FakeCmake {
        fail: bool,
        flags: RefCell<Option<BTreeMap<String, String>>>,
        cleaned: Cell<bool>,
    }

    impl Cmake for FakeCmake {
        fn compile(
            &self,
            job: &CompileJob,
            progress: &mut dyn FnMut(Progress),
        ) -> Result<Compiled> {
            *self.flags.borrow_mut() = Some(job.flags.clone());
            progress(Progress::Configuring);
            progress(Progress::Building { done: 1, total: 2 });
            if self.fail {
                bail!("cmake --build failed; the last lines of /state/build-llama.cpp.log: error");
            }
            progress(Progress::Building { done: 2, total: 2 });
            Ok(Compiled {
                artifact: Path::new("/data/work/llama.cpp").join(job.artifact),
                log: "/state/logs/build-llama.cpp.log".into(),
            })
        }
        fn clean(&self, _: &str) -> Result<()> {
            self.cleaned.set(true);
            Ok(())
        }
    }

    /// Builds in memory; `locked` stands for another ai-ctl holding the lock.
    #[derive(Default)]
    struct FakeStore {
        locked: bool,
        builds: RefCell<Vec<Build>>,
        unreadable: Vec<Unreadable>,
        current: RefCell<Option<BuildId>>,
        staged: RefCell<Vec<Build>>,
        discarded: Cell<usize>,
        replaced: Cell<bool>,
    }

    impl BuildStore for FakeStore {
        fn lock(&self) -> Result<Box<dyn Any>> {
            ensure!(!self.locked, "another ai-ctl is changing the builds");
            Ok(Box::new(()))
        }
        fn builds(&self, _: &str) -> Result<(Vec<Build>, Vec<Unreadable>)> {
            Ok((self.builds.borrow().clone(), self.unreadable.clone()))
        }
        fn current(&self, _: &str) -> Result<Option<BuildId>> {
            Ok(self.current.borrow().clone())
        }
        fn binary(&self, project: &str, id: &BuildId, name: &str) -> PathBuf {
            Path::new("/data/builds")
                .join(project)
                .join(id.as_str())
                .join(name)
        }
        fn stage(&self, build: &Build, _: &Path, _: &Path) -> Result<Staged> {
            self.staged.borrow_mut().push(build.clone());
            Ok(Staged {
                dir: "/data/builds/llama.cpp/.staging".into(),
                binary: "/data/builds/llama.cpp/.staging/llama-server".into(),
                build: build.clone(),
            })
        }
        fn publish(&self, staged: &Staged, replace: bool) -> Result<()> {
            self.replaced.set(replace);
            let mut builds = self.builds.borrow_mut();
            builds.retain(|build| build.id != staged.build.id);
            builds.push(staged.build.clone());
            Ok(())
        }
        fn discard(&self, _: &Staged) -> Result<()> {
            self.discarded.set(self.discarded.get() + 1);
            Ok(())
        }
        fn set_current(&self, _: &str, id: &BuildId) -> Result<()> {
            *self.current.borrow_mut() = Some(id.clone());
            Ok(())
        }
        fn remove(&self, _: &str, id: &BuildId) -> Result<()> {
            self.builds.borrow_mut().retain(|build| &build.id != id);
            Ok(())
        }
        fn contains(&self, _: &Path) -> bool {
            unreachable!("builds never look at processes")
        }
    }

    struct World {
        gpu: FakeGpu,
        host: FakeHost,
        sources: FakeSources,
        cmake: FakeCmake,
        store: FakeStore,
        programs: FakePrograms,
    }

    impl World {
        fn new() -> Self {
            Self {
                gpu: FakeGpu(Probed::Found(gpu())),
                host: FakeHost::healthy(),
                sources: FakeSources {
                    checkouts: RefCell::new(vec![]),
                },
                cmake: FakeCmake {
                    fail: false,
                    flags: RefCell::new(None),
                    cleaned: Cell::new(false),
                },
                store: FakeStore::default(),
                programs: FakePrograms::default(),
            }
        }

        fn build(&self, reference: Option<&str>, force: bool) -> (Result<Built>, Vec<Event>) {
            let clock = || Timestamp::from_unix(NOW).unwrap();
            let builder = Builder {
                gpu: &self.gpu,
                host: &self.host,
                sources: &self.sources,
                cmake: &self.cmake,
                store: &self.store,
                programs: &self.programs,
                clock: &clock,
            };
            let mut events = Vec::new();
            let request = Request {
                project: "llama.cpp",
                reference,
                force,
                jobs: None,
            };
            let result = builder.build(&request, &mut |event| events.push(event));
            (result, events)
        }
    }

    fn installed(id: &str, at: i64) -> Build {
        Build {
            project: "llama.cpp".into(),
            id: BuildId::parse(id).unwrap(),
            commit: CommitId::parse(B11538).unwrap(),
            toolkit: Version::new(13, 4, 92),
            host_compiler: crate::application::fakes::gxx("g++", Version::new(16, 2, 1)),
            cmake_flags: BTreeMap::new(),
            built_at: Timestamp::from_unix(at).unwrap(),
        }
    }

    fn id(text: &str) -> BuildId {
        BuildId::parse(text).unwrap()
    }

    fn message(result: Result<impl fmt::Debug>) -> String {
        format!("{:#}", result.unwrap_err())
    }

    #[test]
    fn latest_builds_the_newest_release_and_makes_it_current() {
        let world = World::new();
        *world.store.current.borrow_mut() = Some(id("b11400"));
        let (result, events) = world.build(None, false);
        let built = result.unwrap();

        assert_eq!(*world.sources.checkouts.borrow(), ["b11538"]);
        assert_eq!(built.build.id, id("b11538"));
        assert_eq!(built.build.commit.as_str(), B11538);
        assert_eq!(built.build.toolkit, Version::new(13, 4, 92));
        assert_eq!(built.build.host_compiler.command, "g++-15");
        assert_eq!(built.build.built_at.unix(), NOW);
        assert_eq!(built.build.cuda_architectures(), ["120a-real"]);
        assert_eq!(
            built.binary,
            Path::new("/data/builds/llama.cpp/b11538/llama-server")
        );
        assert_eq!(built.previous, Some(id("b11400")));
        assert_eq!(*world.store.current.borrow(), Some(id("b11538")));
        assert!(world.cmake.cleaned.get());
        assert_eq!(
            world.cmake.flags.borrow().as_ref().unwrap()["CMAKE_CUDA_HOST_COMPILER"],
            "/usr/bin/g++-15"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            events,
            [
                Event::Resolving,
                Event::Fetching {
                    reference: "b11538".into()
                },
                Event::CheckedOut {
                    id: id("b11538"),
                    commit: CommitId::parse(B11538).unwrap()
                },
                Event::Compile(Progress::Configuring),
                Event::Compile(Progress::Building { done: 1, total: 2 }),
                Event::Compile(Progress::Building { done: 2, total: 2 }),
                Event::Testing,
            ]
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn latest_can_be_named() {
        let world = World::new();
        world.build(Some(LATEST), false).0.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(*world.sources.checkouts.borrow(), ["b11538"]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_commit_of_a_release_is_named_after_the_release() {
        let world = World::new();
        let built = world.build(Some("79e2e74"), false).0.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(built.build.id, id("b11538"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_commit_without_a_release_is_named_after_its_hash() {
        let world = World::new();
        let (result, events) = world.build(Some("feature"), false);
        assert_eq!(result.unwrap().build.id, id("0123456789"));
        assert!(!events.contains(&Event::Resolving));
    }

    #[test]
    fn cuda_13_2_refuses_the_build_with_doctors_remedy() {
        let mut world = World::new();
        world.host = FakeHost::before_the_upgrade();
        let (result, events) = world.build(None, false);
        let error = result.unwrap_err();
        let refused = error.downcast_ref::<Refused>().expect("a refusal");
        assert_eq!(refused.0.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(refused.0[0].name, "CUDA toolkit");
        let text = error.to_string();
        assert!(text.contains("CUDA 13.2 is reported"), "{text}");
        assert!(
            text.contains("fix: 1. sudo dnf config-manager addrepo"),
            "{text}"
        );
        assert!(
            text.contains("3. sudo dnf install cuda-toolkit-13-4"),
            "{text}"
        );
        assert!(
            !text.contains("Toolkit packages"),
            "warnings do not refuse: {text}"
        );
        assert!(events.is_empty());
        assert!(world.sources.checkouts.borrow().is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_machine_without_the_driver_refuses_the_build() {
        let mut world = World::new();
        world.gpu = FakeGpu(Probed::Missing);
        let error = world.build(None, false).0.unwrap_err();
        let refused = error.downcast_ref::<Refused>().expect("a refusal");
        assert_eq!(refused.0[0].name, "GPU");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_build_waits_for_no_one() {
        let mut world = World::new();
        world.store.locked = true;
        let text = message(world.build(None, false).0);
        assert!(text.contains("another ai-ctl"), "{text}");
        assert!(world.sources.checkouts.borrow().is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_installed_build_is_not_rebuilt_without_force() {
        let world = World::new();
        world.store.builds.borrow_mut().push(installed("b11538", 1));
        let text = message(world.build(None, false).0);
        assert!(text.contains("already built"), "{text}");
        assert!(text.contains("ai-ctl build use llama.cpp b11538"), "{text}");
        assert!(world.cmake.flags.borrow().is_none(), "nothing compiled");

        *world.store.current.borrow_mut() = Some(id("b11538"));
        let text = message(world.build(None, false).0);
        assert!(text.contains("is already built and current"), "{text}");

        let rebuilt = world.build(None, true).0.unwrap();
        assert!(world.store.replaced.get());
        assert_eq!(rebuilt.build.built_at.unix(), NOW);
        assert_eq!(rebuilt.previous, None);
        assert_eq!(world.store.builds.borrow().len(), 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_unreadable_directory_of_the_same_name_is_in_the_way() {
        let mut world = World::new();
        world.store.unreadable.push(Unreadable {
            dir: "/data/builds/llama.cpp/b11538".into(),
            reason: "no build.json".into(),
        });
        assert!(message(world.build(None, false).0).contains("already built"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_failed_compilation_installs_nothing() {
        let mut world = World::new();
        world.cmake.fail = true;
        let text = message(world.build(None, false).0);
        assert!(text.contains("build-llama.cpp.log"), "{text}");
        assert!(world.store.staged.borrow().is_empty());
        assert_eq!(*world.store.current.borrow(), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_build_without_a_cuda_device_is_discarded() {
        let mut world = World::new();
        *world.store.current.borrow_mut() = Some(id("b11400"));
        world.programs.devices = printed(true, "Available devices:\n", "");
        let text = message(world.build(None, false).0);
        assert!(text.contains("lists no CUDA device"), "{text}");
        assert_eq!(world.store.discarded.get(), 1);
        assert!(world.store.builds.borrow().is_empty());
        assert_eq!(*world.store.current.borrow(), Some(id("b11400")));
        assert!(
            !world.cmake.cleaned.get(),
            "the build tree stays for inspection"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_binary_that_does_not_start_is_discarded() {
        let mut world = World::new();
        world.programs.version = printed(
            false,
            "",
            "llama-server: error while loading shared libraries: libcudart.so.13: cannot open shared object file\n",
        );
        let text = message(world.build(None, false).0);
        assert!(text.contains("does not run"), "{text}");
        assert!(text.contains("libcudart.so.13"), "{text}");
        assert_eq!(world.store.discarded.get(), 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_unknown_project_or_reference_is_refused() {
        let world = World::new();
        let clock = || Timestamp::from_unix(NOW).unwrap();
        let builder = Builder {
            gpu: &world.gpu,
            host: &world.host,
            sources: &world.sources,
            cmake: &world.cmake,
            store: &world.store,
            programs: &world.programs,
            clock: &clock,
        };
        let request = Request {
            project: "whisper.cpp",
            reference: None,
            force: false,
            jobs: None,
        };
        let text = message(builder.build(&request, &mut |_| {}));
        assert_eq!(text, "no recipe for `whisper.cpp`; ai-ctl builds llama.cpp");

        assert!(message(world.build(Some("b1"), false).0).contains("is not a tag"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_refusal_lists_each_check_with_its_remedy() {
        let refused = Refused(vec![
            Check::fail(
                "CMake",
                "cmake not found",
                Some("sudo dnf install cmake".into()),
            ),
            Check::fail("Git", "git: broken", None),
        ]);
        assert_eq!(
            refused.to_string(),
            "the build is refused; `ai-ctl doctor` shows every check\n  CMake: cmake not found\n    fix: sudo dnf install cmake\n  Git: git: broken"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn list_shows_the_newest_first_with_the_current_one() {
        let store = FakeStore::default();
        store.builds.borrow_mut().extend([
            installed("b1", 10),
            installed("b3", 30),
            installed("b2", 20),
        ]);
        *store.current.borrow_mut() = Some(id("b2"));
        let listings = list(&store, None).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(listings.len(), 1);
        let ids: Vec<&str> = listings[0].builds.iter().map(|b| b.id.as_str()).collect();
        assert_eq!(ids, ["b3", "b2", "b1"]);
        assert_eq!(listings[0].current, Some(id("b2")));
        assert!(list(&store, Some("nope")).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn use_switches_to_an_installed_build_only() {
        let store = FakeStore::default();
        store
            .builds
            .borrow_mut()
            .extend([installed("b1", 10), installed("b2", 20)]);
        *store.current.borrow_mut() = Some(id("b2"));
        assert_eq!(
            use_build(&store, "llama.cpp", "b1").unwrap(),
            Some(id("b2"))
        );
        assert_eq!(*store.current.borrow(), Some(id("b1")));
        assert_eq!(use_build(&store, "llama.cpp", "b1").unwrap(), None);

        let text = message(use_build(&store, "llama.cpp", "b9"));
        assert!(text.contains("has no build b9"), "{text}");
        assert!(message(use_build(&store, "llama.cpp", "../b1")).contains("not a build id"));
        assert_eq!(*store.current.borrow(), Some(id("b1")));
    }

    #[test]
    fn prune_keeps_the_newest_and_the_current_build() {
        let store = FakeStore::default();
        store.builds.borrow_mut().extend([
            installed("b1", 10),
            installed("b2", 20),
            installed("b3", 30),
            installed("b4", 40),
        ]);
        *store.current.borrow_mut() = Some(id("b1"));
        let pruned = prune(&store, Some("llama.cpp"), 2).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(pruned[0].removed, [id("b2")]);
        assert_eq!(pruned[0].kept, 3);
        let left: Vec<String> = store
            .builds
            .borrow()
            .iter()
            .map(|b| b.id.to_string())
            .collect();
        assert_eq!(left, ["b1", "b3", "b4"]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn prune_waits_for_no_one() {
        let store = FakeStore {
            locked: true,
            ..FakeStore::default()
        };
        store.builds.borrow_mut().push(installed("b1", 10));
        assert!(message(prune(&store, None, 0)).contains("another ai-ctl"));
        assert_eq!(store.builds.borrow().len(), 1);
    }
}
</file>

<file path="src/application/doctor.rs">
//! `doctor`: a read-only diagnosis of everything the stack needs, with a
//! remedy for each problem. It never installs anything.

use std::borrow::Cow;
use std::fmt::Write as _;
use std::path::Path;

use std::collections::BTreeSet;

use anyhow::Result;

use crate::domain::diagnostics::{Check, Report, Section, Status};
use crate::domain::gateway::PINNED;
use crate::domain::gateway_config::GPU_DEVICE;
use crate::domain::inventory::{BackendSource, Inventory, Model};
use crate::domain::machine::{Gpu, GpuProcess, Probed, Tool};
use crate::domain::recipe::{RECIPES, Recipe, recipe};

use super::build::summary;
use super::gateway_config::Configurator;
use super::lifecycle::{Inspection, Lifecycle, Serving};
use super::ports::{
    BuildStore, Containers, GatewayApi, GatewayProcesses, GatewayStore, GpuProbe, HostProbe,
    InventoryStore, Liveness, Programs,
};
use super::toolchain;

/// The architecture native builds target.
const BLACKWELL: u32 = 12;
/// The device the gateway gives a model's container.
const CDI_ALL: &str = GPU_DEVICE;
/// Below this, a process holds a background context: in hybrid graphics the
/// compositor keeps about 4 MiB on the NVIDIA GPU, while a display running on
/// it holds hundreds of MiB.
const IDLE_VRAM_TOLERANCE: u64 = 64 << 20;
const POWER_PROFILE: &str = "performance";

const DRIVER_REMEDY: &str =
    "sudo dnf install akmod-nvidia xorg-x11-drv-nvidia-cuda   # from RPM Fusion, then reboot";
const DRIVER_FAILED_REMEDY: &str = "run `nvidia-smi`; after a driver update, reboot (with Secure Boot, enroll the akmod's MOK key)";
const VRAM_REMEDY: &str = "run the display on the integrated GPU (hybrid graphics) and close other GPU programs, so the models get the whole VRAM";
const CDI_REMEDY: &str =
    "sudo nvidia-ctk cdi generate --output=/etc/cdi/nvidia.yaml   # again after each driver update";
const PROFILE_REMEDY: &str = "busctl set-property org.freedesktop.UPower.PowerProfiles /org/freedesktop/UPower/PowerProfiles org.freedesktop.UPower.PowerProfiles ActiveProfile s performance   # or choose Performance in the system menu";
const INIT_REMEDY: &str = "ai-ctl init --models-dir <dir>";
const SWAP_REMEDY: &str = "ai-ctl gateway install";

/// The doctor use case, with the probes it reads.
pub struct Doctor<'a> {
    pub gpu: &'a dyn GpuProbe,
    pub host: &'a dyn HostProbe,
    pub inventory: &'a dyn InventoryStore,
    pub builds: &'a dyn BuildStore,
    pub programs: &'a dyn Programs,
    pub releases: &'a dyn GatewayStore,
    pub processes: &'a dyn GatewayProcesses,
    pub api: &'a dyn GatewayApi,
    pub containers: &'a dyn Containers,
}

impl Doctor<'_> {
    pub fn run(&self) -> Report {
        let gpu = self.gpu.gpu();
        let inventory = self.inventory.load();
        // Its warnings are left out: each is reported by a check of its own
        // (the GPU, the gateway's API, the inventory, podman), or comes from
        // /proc, which Linux always gives.
        let inspection = self.lifecycle().inspect(&mut |_| {});
        let accounted: Vec<GpuProcess> = match &inspection {
            Ok(inspection) => {
                let status = &inspection.status;
                let held = status.gateway.iter().flat_map(|gateway| &gateway.held);
                held.chain(&status.leftovers).cloned().collect()
            }
            Err(_) => Vec::new(),
        };
        let gateway = self.gateway_checks(&inventory, &inspection);
        Report {
            sections: vec![
                Section::new("GPU and driver", gpu_checks(&gpu, &accounted)),
                Section::new(
                    "Toolchain",
                    toolchain::inspect(self.host, gpu.found()).checks,
                ),
                Section::new("Containers (image backends only)", self.container_checks()),
                Section::new("Power", self.power_checks()),
                Section::new("Builds", self.build_checks(&inventory)),
                Section::new("Inventory", self.inventory_checks(inventory)),
                Section::new("Gateway", gateway),
            ],
        }
    }

    /// The lifecycle use cases on the doctor's ports, to inspect the
    /// gateway.
    fn lifecycle(&self) -> Lifecycle<'_> {
        Lifecycle {
            configurator: Configurator {
                inventory: self.inventory,
                builds: self.builds,
                gpu: self.gpu,
            },
            releases: self.releases,
            processes: self.processes,
            api: self.api,
            containers: self.containers,
            // Inspecting never waits.
            pause: std::thread::sleep,
        }
    }

    /// llama-swap, the gateway's process, its address, and what earlier
    /// gateways left on the GPU.
    fn gateway_checks(
        &self,
        inventory: &Result<Option<Inventory>>,
        inspection: &Result<Inspection>,
    ) -> Vec<Check> {
        let mut checks = vec![self.llama_swap_check()];
        match inspection {
            Ok(inspection) => checks.extend([
                gateway_check(inspection),
                self.address_check(inventory, inspection.status.gateway.as_ref()),
                leftovers_check(inspection),
            ]),
            Err(err) => checks.push(Check::fail("Gateway", format!("{err:#}"), None)),
        }
        checks
    }

    fn llama_swap_check(&self) -> Check {
        const NAME: &str = "llama-swap";
        let pinned = PINNED.release;
        let release = match self.releases.current() {
            Ok(Some(release)) => release,
            Ok(None) => {
                return Check::fail(
                    NAME,
                    "not installed; `ai-ctl up` needs it",
                    fix(SWAP_REMEDY),
                );
            }
            Err(err) => return Check::fail(NAME, format!("{err:#}"), fix(SWAP_REMEDY)),
        };
        match self.releases.is_installed(release) {
            Ok(true) if release == pinned => {
                Check::ok(NAME, format!("{release}, the release this ai-ctl pins"))
            }
            Ok(true) => Check::warn(
                NAME,
                format!("{release} is current; this ai-ctl pins {pinned}"),
                fix(SWAP_REMEDY),
            ),
            Ok(false) => Check::fail(
                NAME,
                format!("{release} is current, but its binary is missing"),
                fix(format!("{SWAP_REMEDY} --version {release}")),
            ),
            Err(err) => Check::fail(NAME, format!("{err:#}"), fix(SWAP_REMEDY)),
        }
    }

    /// Whether `up` could listen on the inventory's address.
    fn address_check(
        &self,
        inventory: &Result<Option<Inventory>>,
        gateway: Option<&Serving>,
    ) -> Check {
        const NAME: &str = "Gateway address";
        let Ok(Some(inventory)) = inventory else {
            return Check::skip(NAME, "needs the inventory");
        };
        let listen = inventory.gateway().listen;
        if gateway.is_some_and(|gateway| gateway.listen == listen) {
            return Check::ok(NAME, format!("{listen}, where the gateway listens"));
        }
        match self.processes.is_free(listen) {
            Ok(true) => Check::ok(NAME, format!("{listen} free")),
            Ok(false) => Check::fail(
                NAME,
                format!("{listen} is taken by another program; `ai-ctl up` would refuse to start"),
                fix("stop it, or give the gateway another `listen` address in the inventory"),
            ),
            Err(err) => Check::fail(
                NAME,
                format!("{err:#}"),
                fix("give the gateway another `listen` address in the inventory"),
            ),
        }
    }

    fn container_checks(&self) -> Vec<Check> {
        let podman = match self.host.tool_version(Tool::Podman) {
            Probed::Found(version) => Check::ok("Podman", version.to_string()),
            Probed::Missing => {
                Check::warn("Podman", "podman not found", fix("sudo dnf install podman"))
            }
            Probed::Failed(err) => Check::warn("Podman", format!("podman: {err}"), None),
        };

        const CTK: &str = "NVIDIA Container Toolkit";
        let ctk = self.host.tool_version(Tool::NvidiaCtk);
        let ctk_check = match &ctk {
            Probed::Found(version) => Check::ok(CTK, version.to_string()),
            Probed::Missing => Check::warn(
                CTK,
                "nvidia-ctk not found",
                fix("sudo dnf install nvidia-container-toolkit   # from NVIDIA's repository"),
            ),
            Probed::Failed(err) => Check::warn(CTK, format!("nvidia-ctk: {err}"), None),
        };

        const CDI: &str = "CDI specification";
        let cdi = match ctk {
            Probed::Found(_) => match self.host.cdi_devices() {
                Probed::Found(devices) if devices.iter().any(|d| d == CDI_ALL) => {
                    Check::ok(CDI, format!("{CDI_ALL} listed"))
                }
                Probed::Found(devices) if !devices.is_empty() => Check::warn(
                    CDI,
                    format!("{CDI_ALL} not listed (found {})", devices.join(", ")),
                    fix(CDI_REMEDY),
                ),
                Probed::Found(_) | Probed::Missing => {
                    Check::warn(CDI, "no CDI device listed", fix(CDI_REMEDY))
                }
                Probed::Failed(err) => {
                    Check::warn(CDI, format!("nvidia-ctk cdi list: {err}"), fix(CDI_REMEDY))
                }
            },
            _ => Check::skip(CDI, "needs nvidia-ctk"),
        };

        vec![podman, ctk_check, cdi]
    }

    fn power_checks(&self) -> Vec<Check> {
        const AC: &str = "AC power";
        let ac = match self.host.ac_power() {
            Probed::Found(true) => Check::ok(AC, "adapter online"),
            Probed::Found(false) => Check::warn(
                AC,
                "on battery: the GPU runs at a reduced power limit",
                fix("plug in the AC adapter"),
            ),
            Probed::Missing => Check::skip(AC, "no external power supply reported"),
            Probed::Failed(err) => Check::warn(AC, err, None),
        };

        const PROFILE: &str = "Power profile";
        let profile = match self.host.power_profile() {
            Probed::Found(profile) if profile == POWER_PROFILE => Check::ok(PROFILE, profile),
            Probed::Found(profile) => Check::warn(
                PROFILE,
                format!("`{profile}`; generation and benchmarks need `{POWER_PROFILE}`"),
                fix(PROFILE_REMEDY),
            ),
            Probed::Missing => Check::skip(
                PROFILE,
                "no power profile service (power-profiles-daemon or tuned-ppd)",
            ),
            Probed::Failed(err) => Check::warn(PROFILE, err, None),
        };

        vec![ac, profile]
    }

    /// Each project's current build runs. A project the inventory runs needs
    /// one; the others are reported when they have one.
    fn build_checks(&self, inventory: &Result<Option<Inventory>>) -> Vec<Check> {
        let used: BTreeSet<&str> = match inventory {
            Ok(Some(inventory)) => inventory
                .backends()
                .iter()
                .filter_map(|backend| match &backend.source {
                    BackendSource::Build(project) => Some(project.as_str()),
                    BackendSource::Image(_) => None,
                })
                .collect(),
            _ => BTreeSet::new(),
        };
        let mut checks: Vec<Check> = RECIPES
            .iter()
            .map(|recipe| self.build_check(recipe, used.contains(recipe.project)))
            .collect();
        checks.extend(used.iter().filter(|project| recipe(project).is_none()).map(|project| {
            Check::fail(
                *project,
                format!("the inventory runs a build of {project}, which ai-ctl has no recipe for"),
                None,
            )
        }));
        checks
    }

    fn build_check(&self, recipe: &Recipe, used: bool) -> Check {
        let project = recipe.project;
        let status = if used { Status::Fail } else { Status::Warn };
        let problem = |detail: String, remedy: String| {
            let remedy = Some(remedy);
            match status {
                Status::Fail => Check::fail(project, detail, remedy),
                _ => Check::warn(project, detail, remedy),
            }
        };
        let build_remedy = format!("ai-ctl build {project}");
        let current = match self.builds.current(project) {
            Ok(Some(current)) => current,
            Ok(None) if used => {
                return problem(
                    "no build, and the inventory runs one".to_owned(),
                    build_remedy,
                );
            }
            Ok(None) => return Check::skip(project, "no build; the inventory does not run one"),
            Err(err) => return problem(format!("{err:#}"), build_remedy),
        };
        let build = match self.builds.builds(project) {
            Ok((builds, _)) => builds.into_iter().find(|build| build.id == current),
            Err(err) => return problem(format!("{err:#}"), build_remedy),
        };
        let Some(build) = build else {
            return problem(
                format!("the current build, {current}, is not installed"),
                format!("ai-ctl build use {project} <id>   # or: {build_remedy}"),
            );
        };
        let rebuild = format!("ai-ctl build {project} --ref {current} --force");
        let binary = self.builds.binary(project, &current, recipe.binary());
        match self.programs.run(&binary, &["--version"]) {
            Ok(output) if output.success => Check::ok(
                project,
                format!(
                    "{current}, built {} with CUDA {}",
                    build.built_at.minutes(),
                    build.toolkit
                ),
            ),
            Ok(output) => problem(
                format!("{current} does not run: {}", summary(&output)),
                rebuild,
            ),
            Err(err) => problem(format!("{current}: {err:#}"), rebuild),
        }
    }

    fn inventory_checks(&self, inventory: Result<Option<Inventory>>) -> Vec<Check> {
        const NAME: &str = "Inventory";
        let location = self.inventory.location().display();
        match inventory {
            Ok(Some(inventory)) => {
                let summary = format!(
                    "{location}: {} models, {} backends",
                    inventory.models().len(),
                    inventory.backends().len()
                );
                std::iter::once(Check::ok(NAME, summary))
                    .chain(
                        inventory
                            .models()
                            .iter()
                            .map(|model| self.model_check(&inventory, model)),
                    )
                    .collect()
            }
            Ok(None) => vec![Check::fail(
                NAME,
                format!("{location} not found"),
                fix(INIT_REMEDY),
            )],
            Err(err) => vec![Check::fail(
                NAME,
                format!("{err:#}"),
                fix(format!(
                    "fix {location}, or move it away and run `{INIT_REMEDY}`"
                )),
            )],
        }
    }

    fn model_check(&self, inventory: &Inventory, model: &Model) -> Check {
        let name = format!("Model {}", model.id);
        let dir = inventory.model_dir(model);
        let missing: Vec<&Path> = model
            .files
            .values()
            .map(|file| file.as_path())
            .filter(|file| !self.host.is_file(&dir.join(file)))
            .collect();
        if missing.is_empty() {
            return Check::ok(
                name,
                format!("{} files in {}", model.files.len(), dir.display()),
            );
        }

        let names: Vec<String> = missing
            .iter()
            .map(|file| file.display().to_string())
            .collect();
        let remedy = match &model.repo {
            Some(repo) => format!(
                "hf download {} {} --local-dir {}",
                shell_quote(repo),
                names
                    .iter()
                    .map(|name| shell_quote(name))
                    .collect::<Vec<_>>()
                    .join(" "),
                shell_quote(&dir.display().to_string())
            ),
            None => format!("copy {} into {}", names.join(", "), dir.display()),
        };
        Check::fail(
            name,
            format!("missing in {}: {}", dir.display(), names.join(", ")),
            fix(remedy),
        )
    }
}

/// Whether the gateway runs, and what it serves.
fn gateway_check(inspection: &Inspection) -> Check {
    const NAME: &str = "Gateway";
    let Some(gateway) = &inspection.status.gateway else {
        return match &inspection.stale {
            Some((pid, liveness)) => {
                let now = match liveness {
                    Liveness::Exited => "has exited".to_owned(),
                    Liveness::Reused(Some(program)) => {
                        format!("is gone; PID {pid} now runs {}", program.display())
                    }
                    Liveness::Reused(None) | Liveness::Running => {
                        format!("is gone; PID {pid} now runs another user's program")
                    }
                };
                Check::warn(
                    NAME,
                    format!("stopped; the gateway recorded with PID {pid} {now}"),
                    fix("ai-ctl status   # removes the record"),
                )
            }
            None => Check::ok(NAME, "stopped; `ai-ctl up` starts it"),
        };
    };
    let at = format!("running on http://{} (PID {})", gateway.listen, gateway.pid);
    if gateway.current == Some(false) {
        return Check::warn(
            NAME,
            format!(
                "{at}, on another configuration, address or release than the inventory, \
                 the builds and llama-swap give now"
            ),
            fix("ai-ctl down && ai-ctl up"),
        );
    }
    match gateway.models.as_deref() {
        None => Check::warn(
            NAME,
            format!("{at}, but its API does not answer"),
            fix("read its log: ai-ctl logs"),
        ),
        Some([]) => Check::ok(NAME, format!("{at}, no model loaded")),
        Some(models) => {
            let models: Vec<String> = models
                .iter()
                .map(|model| format!("{} ({})", model.id, model.state))
                .collect();
            Check::ok(NAME, format!("{at}: {}", models.join(", ")))
        }
    }
}

/// The processes earlier gateways left on the GPU, which `up` refuses to
/// start beside.
fn leftovers_check(inspection: &Inspection) -> Check {
    const NAME: &str = "GPU leftovers";
    let status = &inspection.status;
    if status.gpu.is_none() {
        return Check::skip(NAME, "the GPU could not be read");
    }
    if status.leftovers.is_empty() {
        return Check::ok(NAME, "none");
    }
    let processes: Vec<&GpuProcess> = status.leftovers.iter().collect();
    let pids: Vec<String> = processes
        .iter()
        .map(|process| process.pid.to_string())
        .collect();
    Check::fail(
        NAME,
        format!(
            "left by an earlier gateway: {}; `ai-ctl up` refuses to start until they are gone",
            process_list(&processes)
        ),
        fix(format!(
            "kill {}   # ai-ctl never stops a backend itself",
            pids.join(" ")
        )),
    )
}

/// The GPU and driver checks; the first one, "GPU", also gates a build.
/// `accounted` are the processes the Gateway section reports, which the
/// idle VRAM leaves out.
pub(super) fn gpu_checks(gpu: &Probed<Gpu>, accounted: &[GpuProcess]) -> Vec<Check> {
    let unavailable = |reason: &str| {
        [
            Check::skip("Driver", reason),
            Check::skip("Idle VRAM", reason),
        ]
    };
    match gpu {
        Probed::Found(gpu) => vec![
            gpu_check(gpu),
            driver_check(gpu),
            vram_check(gpu, accounted),
        ],
        Probed::Missing => {
            let mut checks = vec![Check::fail(
                "GPU",
                "NVML not found: the NVIDIA driver is not installed",
                fix(DRIVER_REMEDY),
            )];
            checks.extend(unavailable("no NVIDIA driver"));
            checks
        }
        Probed::Failed(err) => {
            let mut checks = vec![Check::fail(
                "GPU",
                format!("NVML: {err}"),
                fix(DRIVER_FAILED_REMEDY),
            )];
            checks.extend(unavailable("the GPU could not be read"));
            checks
        }
    }
}

fn gpu_check(gpu: &Gpu) -> Check {
    let (major, minor) = gpu.compute_capability;
    let detail = format!(
        "{}, compute {major}.{minor}, {}",
        gpu.name,
        gib(gpu.memory_total)
    );
    if major == BLACKWELL {
        Check::ok("GPU", detail)
    } else {
        Check::warn(
            "GPU",
            format!("{detail}; native builds target sm_120a (Blackwell)"),
            None,
        )
    }
}

fn driver_check(gpu: &Gpu) -> Check {
    Check::ok(
        "Driver",
        format!(
            "{}, CUDA {}.{}",
            gpu.driver_version, gpu.cuda_version.major, gpu.cuda_version.minor
        ),
    )
}

fn vram_check(gpu: &Gpu, accounted: &[GpuProcess]) -> Check {
    let (holders, background): (Vec<&GpuProcess>, Vec<&GpuProcess>) = gpu
        .processes
        .iter()
        .filter(|process| !accounted.iter().any(|other| other.pid == process.pid))
        .partition(|process| {
            process
                .memory
                .is_none_or(|bytes| bytes >= IDLE_VRAM_TOLERANCE)
        });
    if holders.is_empty() {
        let mut detail = format!("{} in use", mib(gpu.memory_used));
        let gateway: u64 = accounted.iter().filter_map(|process| process.memory).sum();
        if !accounted.is_empty() {
            let _ = write!(
                detail,
                ", {} of it by the processes the Gateway section reports",
                mib(gateway)
            );
        }
        if !background.is_empty() {
            let _ = write!(
                detail,
                "; under {} each: {}",
                mib(IDLE_VRAM_TOLERANCE),
                process_list(&background)
            );
        }
        return Check::ok("Idle VRAM", detail);
    }
    Check::warn(
        "Idle VRAM",
        format!("held by {}", process_list(&holders)),
        fix(VRAM_REMEDY),
    )
}

fn process_list(processes: &[&GpuProcess]) -> String {
    processes
        .iter()
        .map(|process| match process.memory {
            Some(bytes) => format!("{} (pid {}, {})", process.name, process.pid, mib(bytes)),
            None => format!("{} (pid {})", process.name, process.pid),
        })
        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        .join(", ")
}

fn fix(remedy: impl Into<String>) -> Option<String> {
    Some(remedy.into())
}

fn gib(bytes: u64) -> String {
    format!("{:.1} GiB", bytes as f64 / f64::from(1u32 << 30))
}

fn mib(bytes: u64) -> String {
    format!("{} MiB", bytes >> 20)
}

/// Quotes `word` for a POSIX shell when it holds anything but safe characters.
fn shell_quote(word: &str) -> Cow<'_, str> {
    let safe = !word.is_empty()
        && word
            .chars()
            .all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || "_-./:=@%+,".contains(c));
    if safe {
        Cow::Borrowed(word)
    } else {
        Cow::Owned(format!("'{}'", word.replace('\'', r"'\''")))
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use std::collections::BTreeMap;

    use super::*;
    use crate::application::fakes::{
        FakeApi, FakeBuilds, FakeContainers, FakeGpu, FakeHost, FakeInventory as FakeStore,
        FakeProcesses, FakePrograms, FakeReleases, gpu, gxx, inventory, model, printed, record,
        toolkit,
    };
    use crate::application::ports::RunningModel;
    use crate::domain::diagnostics::Status;
    use crate::domain::inventory::{Backend, BackendKind, BackendSource, Gateway, InventoryParts};
    use crate::domain::machine::Version;

    /// A machine where every check passes.
    fn healthy_host() -> FakeHost {
        let dir = Path::new("/models/qwen3.8-27b");
        let mut host = FakeHost::healthy();
        host.files
            .extend(model().files.values().map(|file| dir.join(file)));
        host
    }

    fn diagnose(gpu: Probed<Gpu>, host: &FakeHost, store: FakeStore) -> Report {
        diagnose_builds(
            gpu,
            host,
            store,
            &FakeBuilds::current_b11538(),
            &FakePrograms::default(),
        )
    }

    fn diagnose_builds(
        gpu: Probed<Gpu>,
        host: &FakeHost,
        store: FakeStore,
        builds: &FakeBuilds,
        programs: &FakePrograms,
    ) -> Report {
        Doctor {
            gpu: &FakeGpu(gpu),
            host,
            inventory: &store,
            builds,
            programs,
            releases: &FakeReleases::current("263"),
            processes: &FakeProcesses::default(),
            api: &FakeApi::answering_after(0),
            containers: &FakeContainers::default(),
        }
        .run()
    }

    /// The gateway's side of the machine: llama-swap v263 installed, and no
    /// gateway recorded until a test records one.
    struct GatewaySide {
        gpu: Probed<Gpu>,
        releases: FakeReleases,
        processes: FakeProcesses,
        api: FakeApi,
        containers: FakeContainers,
    }

    impl GatewaySide {
        fn new() -> Self {
            Self {
                gpu: Probed::Found(gpu()),
                releases: FakeReleases::current("263"),
                processes: FakeProcesses::default(),
                api: FakeApi::answering_after(0),
                containers: FakeContainers::default(),
            }
        }

        /// The gateway PID 1234 running the model PID 5000, on the
        /// configuration the inventory gives.
        fn serving() -> Self {
            let mut side = Self::new();
            *side.processes.record.borrow_mut() = Some(record(1234));
            side.processes.descendants = vec![5000];
            side.gpu = with(&[(5000, "llama-server", 21094)]);
            let config = Configurator {
                inventory: &installed(),
                builds: &FakeBuilds::current_b11538(),
                gpu: &FakeGpu(side.gpu.clone()),
            }
            .generate()
            .unwrap()
            .to_yaml();
            *side.processes.config.borrow_mut() = Some(config);
            side
        }

        fn report(&self) -> Report {
            Doctor {
                gpu: &FakeGpu(self.gpu.clone()),
                host: &healthy_host(),
                inventory: &installed(),
                builds: &FakeBuilds::current_b11538(),
                programs: &FakePrograms::default(),
                releases: &self.releases,
                processes: &self.processes,
                api: &self.api,
                containers: &self.containers,
            }
            .run()
        }

        fn check(&self, name: &str) -> Check {
            check(&self.report(), name).clone()
        }
    }

    /// The idle GPU, with these processes on it.
    fn with(processes: &[(u32, &str, u64)]) -> Probed<Gpu> {
        let mut gpu = gpu();
        gpu.processes = processes
            .iter()
            .map(|&(pid, name, mib)| GpuProcess {
                pid,
                name: name.into(),
                memory: Some(mib << 20),
            })
            .collect();
        Probed::Found(gpu)
    }

    fn builds_check(builds: &FakeBuilds, programs: &FakePrograms, store: FakeStore) -> Check {
        let report = diagnose_builds(
            Probed::Found(gpu()),
            &healthy_host(),
            store,
            builds,
            programs,
        );
        check(&report, "llama.cpp").clone()
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_current_build_runs() {
        let check = builds_check(
            &FakeBuilds::current_b11538(),
            &FakePrograms::default(),
            installed(),
        );
        assert_eq!(check.status, Status::Ok);
        assert_eq!(
            check.detail,
            "b11538, built 2026-10-09 20:48 UTC with CUDA 13.4.92"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_current_build_that_no_longer_runs_fails_with_a_rebuild() {
        let programs = FakePrograms {
            version: printed(
                false,
                "",
                "llama-server: error while loading shared libraries: libcudart.so.13: cannot open shared object file: No such file or directory\n",
            ),
            ..FakePrograms::default()
        };
        let check = builds_check(&FakeBuilds::current_b11538(), &programs, installed());
        assert_eq!(check.status, Status::Fail);
        assert!(
            check.detail.starts_with("b11538 does not run: "),
            "{}",
            check.detail
        );
        assert!(check.detail.contains("libcudart.so.13"), "{}", check.detail);
        assert_eq!(
            check.remedy.as_deref(),
            Some("ai-ctl build llama.cpp --ref b11538 --force")
        );
        // Without the inventory running it, the same build only warns.
        let check = builds_check(
            &FakeBuilds::current_b11538(),
            &programs,
            FakeStore(Ok(None)),
        );
        assert_eq!(check.status, Status::Warn);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_project_the_inventory_runs_needs_a_build() {
        let none = FakeBuilds {
            builds: vec![],
            current: None,
        };
        let check = builds_check(&none, &FakePrograms::default(), installed());
        assert_eq!(check.status, Status::Fail);
        assert_eq!(check.remedy.as_deref(), Some("ai-ctl build llama.cpp"));
        let check = builds_check(&none, &FakePrograms::default(), FakeStore(Ok(None)));
        assert_eq!(check.status, Status::Skip);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_current_link_to_a_missing_build_fails() {
        let dangling = FakeBuilds {
            builds: vec![],
            current: Some(crate::domain::build::BuildId::parse("b9").unwrap()),
        };
        let check = builds_check(&dangling, &FakePrograms::default(), installed());
        assert_eq!(check.status, Status::Fail);
        assert_eq!(check.detail, "the current build, b9, is not installed");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_project_without_a_recipe_fails() {
        let inventory = Inventory::new(InventoryParts {
            models_dir: "/models".into(),
            gateway: Gateway::default(),
            defaults: BTreeMap::new(),
            backends: vec![Backend {
                id: "llama-native".into(),
                kind: BackendKind::LlamaServer,
                source: BackendSource::Build("whisper.cpp".into()),
            }],
            models: vec![model()],
        })
        .unwrap();
        let report = diagnose(
            Probed::Found(gpu()),
            &healthy_host(),
            FakeStore(Ok(Some(inventory))),
        );
        let whisper = check(&report, "whisper.cpp");
        assert_eq!(whisper.status, Status::Fail);
        assert!(whisper.detail.contains("no recipe"), "{}", whisper.detail);
        assert_eq!(check(&report, "llama.cpp").status, Status::Ok);
    }

    fn installed() -> FakeStore {
        FakeStore(Ok(Some(inventory("/models"))))
    }

    fn check<'a>(report: &'a Report, name: &str) -> &'a Check {
        report
            .checks()
            .find(|check| check.name == name)
            .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("no check named {name}"))
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_healthy_machine_passes_every_check() {
        let report = diagnose(Probed::Found(gpu()), &healthy_host(), installed());
        let not_ok: Vec<_> = report
            .checks()
            .filter(|check| check.status != Status::Ok)
            .collect();
        assert!(not_ok.is_empty(), "{not_ok:#?}");
        assert_eq!(
            report
                .sections
                .iter()
                .map(|s| s.name.as_str())
                .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            [
                "GPU and driver",
                "Toolchain",
                "Containers (image backends only)",
                "Power",
                "Builds",
                "Inventory",
                "Gateway"
            ]
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_machine_without_nvml_fails_the_gpu_and_goes_on() {
        let report = diagnose(Probed::Missing, &healthy_host(), installed());
        let gpu = check(&report, "GPU");
        assert_eq!(gpu.status, Status::Fail);
        assert!(gpu.remedy.as_deref().unwrap().contains("akmod-nvidia"));
        assert_eq!(check(&report, "Driver").status, Status::Skip);
        assert_eq!(check(&report, "Idle VRAM").status, Status::Skip);
        assert_eq!(check(&report, "CUDA toolkit").status, Status::Ok);
        assert_eq!(check(&report, "Model qwen3.8-27b").status, Status::Ok);
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_unreadable_gpu_fails_with_the_nvml_error() {
        let report = diagnose(
            Probed::Failed("Driver Not Loaded".into()),
            &healthy_host(),
            installed(),
        );
        let gpu = check(&report, "GPU");
        assert_eq!(gpu.status, Status::Fail);
        assert!(gpu.detail.contains("Driver Not Loaded"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_gpu_other_than_blackwell_is_a_warning() {
        let mut ada = gpu();
        ada.compute_capability = (8, 9);
        let report = diagnose(Probed::Found(ada), &healthy_host(), installed());
        assert_eq!(check(&report, "GPU").status, Status::Warn);
    }

    #[test]
    fn processes_holding_vram_are_named() {
        let mut busy = gpu();
        busy.processes = vec![
            GpuProcess {
                pid: 2210,
                name: "gnome-shell".into(),
                memory: Some(312 << 20),
            },
            GpuProcess {
                pid: 2390,
                name: "Xwayland".into(),
                memory: None,
            },
        ];
        let report = diagnose(Probed::Found(busy), &healthy_host(), installed());
        let vram = check(&report, "Idle VRAM");
        assert_eq!(vram.status, Status::Warn);
        assert_eq!(
            vram.detail,
            "held by gnome-shell (pid 2210, 312 MiB), Xwayland (pid 2390)"
        );
        assert!(vram.remedy.as_deref().unwrap().contains("integrated GPU"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_background_context_is_tolerated() {
        let mut hybrid = gpu();
        hybrid.processes = vec![GpuProcess {
            pid: 5153,
            name: "gnome-shell".into(),
            memory: Some(4 << 20),
        }];
        let report = diagnose(Probed::Found(hybrid), &healthy_host(), installed());
        let vram = check(&report, "Idle VRAM");
        assert_eq!(vram.status, Status::Ok);
        assert_eq!(
            vram.detail,
            "16 MiB in use; under 64 MiB each: gnome-shell (pid 5153, 4 MiB)"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn cuda_13_2_fails_with_the_upgrade_matching_the_driver() {
        let mut host = FakeHost::before_the_upgrade();
        host.files = healthy_host().files;
        let report = diagnose(Probed::Found(gpu()), &host, installed());
        let cuda = check(&report, "CUDA toolkit");
        assert_eq!(cuda.status, Status::Fail);
        assert!(cuda.detail.contains("#21255"), "{}", cuda.detail);
        let remedy = cuda.remedy.as_deref().unwrap();
        assert!(
            remedy.starts_with("1. sudo dnf config-manager addrepo"),
            "{remedy}"
        );
        assert!(remedy.contains("cuda-fedora44.repo"), "{remedy}");
        assert!(
            remedy.ends_with("3. sudo dnf install cuda-toolkit-13-4"),
            "{remedy}"
        );
        assert_eq!(check(&report, "Toolkit packages").status, Status::Warn);
        assert!(report.has_failures());
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_toolkit_newer_than_the_driver_fails() {
        let mut host = healthy_host();
        host.toolkit = Probed::Found(toolkit(Version::new(13, 5, 10)));
        let report = diagnose(Probed::Found(gpu()), &host, installed());
        let cuda = check(&report, "CUDA toolkit");
        assert_eq!(cuda.status, Status::Fail);
        assert!(
            cuda.detail.contains("newer than CUDA 13.4"),
            "{}",
            cuda.detail
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_toolkit_without_blackwell_support_fails() {

        let report = diagnose(Probed::Found(gpu()), &host, installed());
        assert_eq!(check(&report, "CUDA toolkit").status, Status::Fail);
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_toolkit_is_not_compared_with_an_unknown_driver() {
        let mut host = healthy_host();
        host.toolkit = Probed::Found(toolkit(Version::new(13, 5, 10)));
        let report = diagnose(Probed::Missing, &host, installed());
        assert_eq!(check(&report, "CUDA toolkit").status, Status::Ok);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_missing_toolkit_fails_and_skips_the_compiler_limit() {
        let mut host = healthy_host();

        let report = diagnose(Probed::Found(gpu()), &host, installed());
        let cuda = check(&report, "CUDA toolkit");

            cuda.remedy
                .as_deref()
                .unwrap()
                .contains("cuda-toolkit-13-4")
        );
        assert_eq!(check(&report, "Host compiler").status, Status::Skip);
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_compiler_check_names_the_compiler_a_build_uses() {

        let compiler = check(&report, "Host compiler");
        assert_eq!(compiler.status, Status::Ok);
        assert!(
            compiler
                .detail
                .starts_with("g++-15 15.3.1; nvcc 13.4.92 accepts GCC 15"),
            "{}",

        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn compilers_all_too_new_fail_with_the_compat_package() {

        let report = diagnose(Probed::Found(gpu()), &host, installed());

        assert_eq!(compiler.status, Status::Fail);
        assert_eq!(
            compiler.remedy.as_deref(),

        );

    #[test]
    fn no_compiler_at_all_fails() {
