        let report = diagnose(Probed::Found(gpu()), &host, installed());
        assert_eq!(
            check(&report, "Host compiler").remedy.as_deref(),
            Some("sudo dnf install gcc-c++")
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_unknown_gcc_limit_is_a_warning() {
        let mut host = healthy_host();
        let mut kit = toolkit(Version::new(13, 4, 92));
        kit.max_gcc_major = Probed::Missing;
        host.toolkit = Probed::Found(kit);
        let report = diagnose(Probed::Found(gpu()), &host, installed());
        assert_eq!(check(&report, "Host compiler").status, Status::Warn);
    }

    #[test]
    fn build_tools_below_their_minimum_fail() {
        let mut host = healthy_host();
        host.cmake = Probed::Found(Version::new(3, 16, 3));
        host.git = Probed::Missing;
        let report = diagnose(Probed::Found(gpu()), &host, installed());
        let cmake = check(&report, "CMake");
        assert_eq!(cmake.status, Status::Fail);
        assert_eq!(cmake.detail, "3.16.3 is older than the 3.18.0 required");
        let git = check(&report, "Git");
        assert_eq!(git.status, Status::Fail);
        assert_eq!(git.remedy.as_deref(), Some("sudo dnf install git"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn missing_container_tooling_only_warns() {
        let mut host = healthy_host();
        host.podman = Probed::Missing;
        host.nvidia_ctk = Probed::Missing;
        let report = diagnose(Probed::Found(gpu()), &host, installed());
        assert_eq!(check(&report, "Podman").status, Status::Warn);
        assert_eq!(
            check(&report, "NVIDIA Container Toolkit").status,
            Status::Warn
        );
        assert_eq!(check(&report, "CDI specification").status, Status::Skip);
        assert!(
            !host.cdi_listed.get(),
            "CDI is not listed without nvidia-ctk"
        );
        assert!(!report.has_failures());
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_cdi_specification_without_all_gpus_warns_with_the_generate_command() {
        let mut host = healthy_host();
        host.cdi = Probed::Found(vec!["nvidia.com/gpu=0".into()]);
        let report = diagnose(Probed::Found(gpu()), &host, installed());
        let cdi = check(&report, "CDI specification");
        assert_eq!(cdi.status, Status::Warn);
        assert!(
            cdi.remedy
                .as_deref()
                .unwrap()
                .contains("nvidia-ctk cdi generate")
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn battery_and_a_slow_profile_warn() {
        let mut host = healthy_host();
        host.ac = Probed::Found(false);
        host.profile = Probed::Found("balanced".into());
        let report = diagnose(Probed::Found(gpu()), &host, installed());
        assert_eq!(check(&report, "AC power").status, Status::Warn);
        let profile = check(&report, "Power profile");
        assert_eq!(profile.status, Status::Warn);
        assert!(
            profile
                .remedy
                .as_deref()
                .unwrap()
                .contains("ActiveProfile s performance")
        );
        assert!(!report.has_failures());
    }

    #[test]
    fn power_checks_skip_what_the_machine_does_not_report() {
        let mut host = healthy_host();
        host.ac = Probed::Missing;
        host.profile = Probed::Missing;
        let report = diagnose(Probed::Found(gpu()), &host, installed());
        assert_eq!(check(&report, "AC power").status, Status::Skip);
        assert_eq!(check(&report, "Power profile").status, Status::Skip);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_missing_inventory_fails_with_the_init_command() {
        let report = diagnose(Probed::Found(gpu()), &healthy_host(), FakeStore(Ok(None)));
        let inventory = check(&report, "Inventory");
        assert_eq!(inventory.status, Status::Fail);
        assert_eq!(inventory.remedy.as_deref(), Some(INIT_REMEDY));
        assert!(
            report
                .checks()
                .all(|check| !check.name.starts_with("Model "))
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_invalid_inventory_fails_with_its_error() {
        let store = FakeStore(Err("model `x` is declared more than once".into()));
        let report = diagnose(Probed::Found(gpu()), &healthy_host(), store);
        let inventory = check(&report, "Inventory");
        assert_eq!(inventory.status, Status::Fail);
        assert!(inventory.detail.contains("more than once"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn missing_model_files_fail_with_the_download_command() {
        let mut host = healthy_host();
        host.files
            .remove(Path::new("/models/qwen3.8-27b/Qwen3.8-27B-UD-Q4_K_M.gguf"));
        host.files.remove(Path::new(
            "/models/qwen3.8-27b/MTP/mtp-Qwen3.8-27B-Q4_0.gguf",
        ));
        let report = diagnose(Probed::Found(gpu()), &host, installed());
        let model = check(&report, "Model qwen3.8-27b");
        assert_eq!(model.status, Status::Fail);
        assert_eq!(
            model.remedy.as_deref(),
            Some(
                "hf download unsloth/Qwen3.8-27B-GGUF Qwen3.8-27B-UD-Q4_K_M.gguf \
                 MTP/mtp-Qwen3.8-27B-Q4_0.gguf --local-dir /models/qwen3.8-27b"
            )
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_download_command_quotes_unusual_paths() {
        let mut host = healthy_host();
        host.files.clear();
        let store = FakeStore(Ok(Some(inventory("/data/my models"))));
        let report = diagnose(Probed::Found(gpu()), &host, store);
        let remedy = check(&report, "Model qwen3.8-27b").remedy.clone().unwrap();
        assert!(
            remedy.ends_with("--local-dir '/data/my models/qwen3.8-27b'"),
            "{remedy}"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn shell_quote_escapes_single_quotes() {
        assert_eq!(shell_quote("plain/path-1.gguf"), "plain/path-1.gguf");
        assert_eq!(shell_quote("it's"), r"'it'\''s'");
        assert_eq!(shell_quote(""), "''");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_stopped_gateway_with_the_pinned_llama_swap_passes() {
        let side = GatewaySide::new();
        let report = side.report();
        let gateway: Vec<(&str, Status, &str)> = report
            .sections
            .iter()
            .find(|section| section.name == "Gateway")
            .unwrap()
            .checks
            .iter()
            .map(|check| (check.name.as_str(), check.status, check.detail.as_str()))
            .collect();
        assert_eq!(
            gateway,
            [
                (
                    "llama-swap",
                    Status::Ok,
                    "v263, the release this ai-ctl pins"
                ),
                ("Gateway", Status::Ok, "stopped; `ai-ctl up` starts it"),
                ("Gateway address", Status::Ok, "127.0.0.1:8080 free"),
                ("GPU leftovers", Status::Ok, "none"),
            ]
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn llama_swap_missing_fails_and_another_release_warns() {
        let mut side = GatewaySide::new();
        side.releases = FakeReleases::none();
        let swap = side.check("llama-swap");
        assert_eq!(
            (swap.status, swap.detail.as_str(), swap.remedy.as_deref()),
            (
                Status::Fail,
                "not installed; `ai-ctl up` needs it",
                Some("ai-ctl gateway install")
            )
        );
        side.releases = FakeReleases::current("262");
        let swap = side.check("llama-swap");
        assert_eq!(
            (swap.status, swap.detail.as_str()),
            (Status::Warn, "v262 is current; this ai-ctl pins v263")
        );
        side.releases.missing = true;
        let swap = side.check("llama-swap");
        assert_eq!(
            (swap.status, swap.remedy.as_deref()),
            (Status::Fail, Some("ai-ctl gateway install --version v262"))
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_running_gateway_names_its_models_and_leaves_the_idle_vram_alone() {
        let mut side = GatewaySide::serving();
        side.api.models = Some(vec![RunningModel {
            id: "qwen3.6-35b-a3b".into(),
            state: "ready".into(),
        }]);
        let report = side.report();
        let gateway = check(&report, "Gateway");
        assert_eq!(
            (gateway.status, gateway.detail.as_str()),
            (
                Status::Ok,
                "running on http://127.0.0.1:8080 (PID 1234): qwen3.6-35b-a3b (ready)"
            )
        );
        assert_eq!(
            check(&report, "Gateway address").detail,
            "127.0.0.1:8080, where the gateway listens"
        );
        let vram = check(&report, "Idle VRAM");
        assert_eq!(
            (vram.status, vram.detail.as_str()),
            (
                Status::Ok,
                "16 MiB in use, 21094 MiB of it by the processes the Gateway section reports"
            )
        );
        // Read-only: nothing was signalled or removed.
        assert!(side.processes.signals.borrow().is_empty());
        assert_eq!(side.processes.record.borrow().as_ref(), Some(&record(1234)));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_running_gateway_out_of_date_or_without_an_api_warns() {
        let side = GatewaySide::serving();
        *side.processes.config.borrow_mut() = Some("models: {}\n".into());
        let gateway = side.check("Gateway");
        assert_eq!(gateway.status, Status::Warn);
        assert!(gateway.detail.ends_with("on another configuration, address or release than the inventory, the builds and llama-swap give now"));
        assert_eq!(gateway.remedy.as_deref(), Some("ai-ctl down && ai-ctl up"));

        let mut side = GatewaySide::serving();
        side.api.models = None;
        let gateway = side.check("Gateway");
        assert_eq!(
            (gateway.status, gateway.detail.as_str()),
            (
                Status::Warn,
                "running on http://127.0.0.1:8080 (PID 1234), but its API does not answer"
            )
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_stale_record_is_reported_and_left_for_status_to_remove() {
        let mut side = GatewaySide::new();
        *side.processes.record.borrow_mut() = Some(record(1234));
        side.processes.liveness = Some(Liveness::Exited);
        let gateway = side.check("Gateway");
        assert_eq!(
            (
                gateway.status,
                gateway.detail.as_str(),
                gateway.remedy.as_deref()
            ),
            (
                Status::Warn,
                "stopped; the gateway recorded with PID 1234 has exited",
                Some("ai-ctl status   # removes the record")
            )
        );
        assert_eq!(side.processes.record.borrow().as_ref(), Some(&record(1234)));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_taken_address_fails() {
        let mut side = GatewaySide::new();
        side.processes.taken = true;
        let address = side.check("Gateway address");
        assert_eq!(
            (address.status, address.detail.as_str()),
            (
                Status::Fail,
                "127.0.0.1:8080 is taken by another program; `ai-ctl up` would refuse to start"
            )
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn leftovers_fail_with_kill_and_leave_the_idle_vram_alone() {
        let mut side = GatewaySide::new();
        side.processes
            .programs
            .insert(5000, "/data/builds/llama.cpp/b11538/llama-server".into());
        side.gpu = with(&[(5000, "llama-server", 21094)]);
        let report = side.report();
        let leftovers = check(&report, "GPU leftovers");
        assert_eq!(
            (
                leftovers.status,
                leftovers.detail.as_str(),
                leftovers.remedy.as_deref()
            ),
            (
                Status::Fail,
                "left by an earlier gateway: llama-server (pid 5000, 21094 MiB); \
                 `ai-ctl up` refuses to start until they are gone",
                Some("kill 5000   # ai-ctl never stops a backend itself")
            )
        );
        assert_eq!(check(&report, "Idle VRAM").status, Status::Ok);
    }

    #[test]
    fn without_a_gpu_or_an_inventory_the_gateway_checks_that_need_them_are_skipped() {
        let mut side = GatewaySide::new();
        side.gpu = Probed::Missing;
        assert_eq!(side.check("GPU leftovers").status, Status::Skip);
        let report = diagnose(Probed::Found(gpu()), &healthy_host(), FakeStore(Ok(None)));
        assert_eq!(check(&report, "Gateway address").status, Status::Skip);
        assert_eq!(check(&report, "Gateway").status, Status::Ok);
    }
}
</file>

<file path="src/application/fakes.rs">
//! Test doubles of the ports, shared by the use cases' tests.

use std::any::Any;
use std::cell::{Cell, RefCell};
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, HashSet, VecDeque};
use std::io::Write;
use std::net::SocketAddr;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use anyhow::{Result, anyhow, ensure};

use crate::domain::build::{Build, BuildId, CommitId};
use crate::domain::gateway::{Release, Sha256};
use crate::domain::gateway_config::CONTAINER_PREFIX;
use crate::domain::inventory::{
    Backend, BackendKind, BackendSource, Gateway, Inventory, InventoryParts, Model,
};
use crate::domain::machine::{
    CudaRepository, CudaToolkit, Gpu, GpuProcess, HostCompiler, Probed, Tool, Version,
};

use crate::domain::timestamp::Timestamp;

use super::ports::{
    BuildStore, Containers, GatewayApi, GatewayProcesses, GatewayRecord, GatewayStore, GpuProbe,
    HostProbe, Installed, InventoryStore, Liveness, ProgramOutput, Programs, RunningModel, Signal,
    Staged, StagedRelease, StartedGateway, Unreadable,
};

pub struct FakeGpu(pub Probed<Gpu>);

impl GpuProbe for FakeGpu {
    fn gpu(&self) -> Probed<Gpu> {
        self.0.clone()
    }
}

pub struct FakeHost {
    pub toolkit: Probed<CudaToolkit>,
    pub compilers: Probed<Vec<HostCompiler>>,
    pub cmake: Probed<Version>,
    pub git: Probed<Version>,
    pub podman: Probed<Version>,
    pub nvidia_ctk: Probed<Version>,
    pub cdi: Probed<Vec<String>>,
    pub cdi_listed: Cell<bool>,
    pub ac: Probed<bool>,
    pub profile: Probed<String>,
    pub files: HashSet<PathBuf>,
    pub fedora: Probed<u32>,
    pub repositories: Probed<Vec<CudaRepository>>,
    pub toolkit_packages: Probed<Vec<String>>,
}

impl FakeHost {
    /// This machine, but with a toolkit accepting GCC 15 at most: every
    /// check passes, and a build compiles with `g++-15`.
    pub fn healthy() -> Self {
        Self {
            toolkit: Probed::Found(toolkit(Version::new(13, 4, 92))),
            compilers: Probed::Found(vec![
                gxx("g++", Version::new(16, 2, 1)),
                gxx("g++-15", Version::new(15, 3, 1)),
            ]),
            cmake: Probed::Found(Version::new(4, 3, 0)),
            git: Probed::Found(Version::new(2, 56, 0)),
            podman: Probed::Found(Version::new(5, 8, 7)),
            nvidia_ctk: Probed::Found(Version::new(1, 20, 1)),
            cdi: Probed::Found(vec!["nvidia.com/gpu=0".into(), "nvidia.com/gpu=all".into()]),
            cdi_listed: Cell::new(false),
            ac: Probed::Found(true),
            profile: Probed::Found("performance".into()),
            files: ["/usr/bin/gcc", "/usr/bin/gcc-15"]
                .into_iter()
                .map(PathBuf::from)
                .collect(),
            fedora: Probed::Found(44),
            repositories: Probed::Found(vec![CudaRepository {
                id: "cuda-fedora44-x86_64".into(),
                driver_packages: vec![],
            }]),
            toolkit_packages: Probed::Found(vec![
                "cuda-toolkit-13-4".into(),
                "cuda-toolkit-13-4-config-common".into(),
                "cuda-toolkit-13-config-common".into(),
                "cuda-toolkit-config-common".into(),
            ]),
        }
    }
}

impl HostProbe for FakeHost {
    fn cuda_toolkit(&self) -> Probed<CudaToolkit> {
        self.toolkit.clone()
    }
    fn host_compilers(&self) -> Probed<Vec<HostCompiler>> {
        self.compilers.clone()
    }
    fn tool_version(&self, tool: Tool) -> Probed<Version> {
        match tool {
            Tool::Cmake => self.cmake.clone(),
            Tool::Git => self.git.clone(),
            Tool::Podman => self.podman.clone(),
            Tool::NvidiaCtk => self.nvidia_ctk.clone(),
        }
    }
    fn cdi_devices(&self) -> Probed<Vec<String>> {
        self.cdi_listed.set(true);
        self.cdi.clone()
    }
    fn ac_power(&self) -> Probed<bool> {
        self.ac.clone()
    }
    fn power_profile(&self) -> Probed<String> {
        self.profile.clone()
    }
    fn is_file(&self, path: &Path) -> bool {
        self.files.contains(path)
    }
    fn fedora_release(&self) -> Probed<u32> {
        self.fedora.clone()
    }
    fn cuda_repositories(&self) -> Probed<Vec<CudaRepository>> {
        self.repositories.clone()
    }
    fn toolkit_packages(&self) -> Probed<Vec<String>> {
        self.toolkit_packages.clone()
    }
}

impl FakeHost {
    /// This machine on 2026-10-09, before the upgrade: CUDA 13.2 from the
    /// `cuda-fedora43` repository, the only CUDA repository configured.
    pub fn before_the_upgrade() -> Self {
        Self {
            toolkit: Probed::Found(toolkit(Version::new(13, 2, 86))),
            repositories: Probed::Found(vec![CudaRepository {
                id: "cuda-fedora43-x86_64".into(),
                driver_packages: vec![],
            }]),
            toolkit_packages: Probed::Found(vec![
                "cuda-toolkit".into(),
                "cuda-toolkit-13-2".into(),
                "cuda-toolkit-config-common".into(),
            ]),
            ..Self::healthy()
        }
    }
}

/// The RTX 5090 Laptop GPU with driver 615.71.09 (CUDA 13.4), idle.
pub fn gpu() -> Gpu {
    Gpu {
        name: "NVIDIA GeForce RTX 5090 Laptop GPU".into(),
        compute_capability: (12, 0),
        memory_total: 24463 << 20,
        memory_reserved: 388 << 20,
        memory_used: 16 << 20,
        driver_version: "615.71.09".into(),
        cuda_version: Version::new(13, 4, 0),
        processes: vec![],
    }
}

/// A toolkit under `/usr/local/cuda-<major>.<minor>` that accepts GCC 15.
pub fn toolkit(version: Version) -> CudaToolkit {
    CudaToolkit {
        version,
        nvcc: format!(
            "/usr/local/cuda-{}.{}/bin/nvcc",
            version.major, version.minor
        )
        .into(),
        max_gcc_major: Probed::Found(15),
    }
}

pub fn gxx(command: &str, version: Version) -> HostCompiler {
    HostCompiler {
        command: command.into(),
        path: Path::new("/usr/bin").join(command),
        version,
    }
}

pub fn printed(success: bool, stdout: &str, stderr: &str) -> ProgramOutput {
    ProgramOutput {
        success,
        stdout: stdout.into(),
        stderr: stderr.into(),
    }
}

/// A llama-server build answering `--version` and `--list-devices` as
/// b11538 does on this machine.
pub struct FakePrograms {
    pub version: ProgramOutput,
    pub devices: ProgramOutput,
}

impl Default for FakePrograms {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            version: printed(
                true,
                "",
                include_str!("../../tests/fixtures/llama-server-version.txt"),
            ),
            devices: printed(
                true,
                include_str!("../../tests/fixtures/llama-server-list-devices.txt"),
                "",
            ),
        }
    }
}

impl Programs for FakePrograms {
    fn run(&self, _: &Path, args: &[&str]) -> Result<ProgramOutput> {
        Ok(match args {
            ["--version"] => self.version.clone(),
            ["--list-devices"] => self.devices.clone(),
            other => panic!("unexpected arguments {other:?}"),
        })
    }
}

/// llama.cpp build b11538 as installed on 2026-10-09.
pub fn b11538() -> Build {
    Build {
        project: "llama.cpp".into(),
        id: BuildId::parse("b11538").unwrap(),
        commit: CommitId::parse("79e2e74eb11022c1ba2e438df7f0ca2d4c10f8b6").unwrap(),
        toolkit: Version::new(13, 4, 92),
        host_compiler: gxx("g++", Version::new(16, 2, 1)),
        cmake_flags: BTreeMap::new(),
        built_at: Timestamp::parse("2026-10-09T20:48:47Z").unwrap(),
    }
}

/// Installed builds, read-only: `doctor` never changes them.
pub struct FakeBuilds {
    pub builds: Vec<Build>,
    pub current: Option<BuildId>,
}

impl FakeBuilds {
    /// b11538, current.
    pub fn current_b11538() -> Self {
        Self {
            builds: vec![b11538()],
            current: Some(BuildId::parse("b11538").unwrap()),
        }
    }
}

impl BuildStore for FakeBuilds {
    fn lock(&self) -> Result<Box<dyn Any>> {
        unreachable!("read-only")
    }
    fn builds(&self, _: &str) -> Result<(Vec<Build>, Vec<Unreadable>)> {
        Ok((self.builds.clone(), vec![]))
    }
    fn current(&self, _: &str) -> Result<Option<BuildId>> {
        Ok(self.current.clone())
    }
    fn binary(&self, project: &str, id: &BuildId, name: &str) -> PathBuf {
        Path::new("/data/builds")
            .join(project)
            .join(id.as_str())
            .join(name)
    }
    fn stage(&self, _: &Build, _: &Path, _: &Path) -> Result<Staged> {
        unreachable!("read-only")
    }
    fn publish(&self, _: &Staged, _: bool) -> Result<()> {
        unreachable!("read-only")
    }
    fn discard(&self, _: &Staged) -> Result<()> {
        unreachable!("read-only")
    }
    fn set_current(&self, _: &str, _: &BuildId) -> Result<()> {
        unreachable!("read-only")
    }
    fn remove(&self, _: &str, _: &BuildId) -> Result<()> {
        unreachable!("read-only")
    }
    fn contains(&self, program: &Path) -> bool {
        program.starts_with("/data/builds")
    }
}

/// The installed inventory, read-only, or the error reading it gives.
pub struct FakeInventory(pub Result<Option<Inventory>, String>);

impl InventoryStore for FakeInventory {
    fn location(&self) -> &Path {
        Path::new("/home/user/.config/ai-ctl/inventory.toml")
    }
    fn load(&self) -> Result<Option<Inventory>> {
        self.0.clone().map_err(|message| anyhow!(message))
    }
    fn install(&self, _: &Path) -> Result<Installed> {
        unreachable!("read-only")
    }
}

/// The main model, served by the native build.
pub fn model() -> Model {
    Model {
        id: "qwen3.8-27b".into(),
        backend: "llama-native".into(),
        repo: Some("unsloth/Qwen3.8-27B-GGUF".into()),
        dir: "qwen3.8-27b".into(),
        files: BTreeMap::from([
            ("model".into(), "Qwen3.8-27B-UD-Q4_K_M.gguf".into()),
            ("mmproj".into(), "mmproj-F16.gguf".into()),
            ("model-draft".into(), "MTP/mtp-Qwen3.8-27B-Q4_0.gguf".into()),
        ]),
        flags: BTreeMap::new(),
        ctx_size: Some(98304),
        vram_mib: 22733,
        ttl: Some(1800),
        preload: true,
    }
}

/// An inventory of [`model`] alone, on the native build of llama.cpp.
pub fn inventory_parts(models_dir: &str) -> InventoryParts {
    InventoryParts {
        models_dir: models_dir.into(),
        gateway: Gateway::default(),
        defaults: BTreeMap::new(),
        backends: vec![Backend {
            id: "llama-native".into(),
            kind: BackendKind::LlamaServer,
            source: BackendSource::Build("llama.cpp".into()),
        }],
        models: vec![model()],
    }
}

pub fn inventory(models_dir: &str) -> Inventory {
    Inventory::new(inventory_parts(models_dir)).unwrap()
}

/// The installed llama-swap releases, read-only: the current one, if any,
/// whose binary is `missing` when its link names a removed release.
pub struct FakeReleases {
    pub current: Option<Release>,
    pub missing: bool,
}

impl FakeReleases {
    pub fn current(release: &str) -> Self {
        Self {
            current: Some(Release::parse(release).unwrap()),
            missing: false,
        }
    }

    pub fn none() -> Self {
        Self {
            current: None,
            missing: false,
        }
    }
}

impl GatewayStore for FakeReleases {
    fn lock(&self) -> Result<Box<dyn Any>> {
        unreachable!("read-only")
    }
    fn is_installed(&self, release: Release) -> Result<bool> {
        Ok(self.current == Some(release) && !self.missing)
    }
    fn current(&self) -> Result<Option<Release>> {
        Ok(self.current)
    }
    fn binary(&self, release: Release) -> PathBuf {
        PathBuf::from(format!("/data/llama-swap/{release}/llama-swap"))
    }
    fn stage(&self, _: Release) -> Result<StagedRelease> {
        unreachable!("read-only")
    }
    fn sha256(&self, _: &Path) -> Result<Sha256> {
        unreachable!("read-only")
    }
    fn unpack(&self, _: &StagedRelease) -> Result<PathBuf> {
        unreachable!("read-only")
    }
    fn publish(&self, _: &StagedRelease) -> Result<()> {
        unreachable!("read-only")
    }
    fn discard(&self, _: &StagedRelease) -> Result<()> {
        unreachable!("read-only")
    }
    fn set_current(&self, _: Release) -> Result<()> {
        unreachable!("read-only")
    }
}

/// The address of the recorded gateway.
pub const LISTEN: &str = "127.0.0.1:8080";
/// The binary of [`FakeReleases::current`]`("263")`.
pub const SWAP_BINARY: &str = "/data/llama-swap/v263/llama-swap";

pub fn listen() -> SocketAddr {
    LISTEN.parse().unwrap()
}

pub fn record(pid: u32) -> GatewayRecord {
    GatewayRecord {
        pid,
        binary: SWAP_BINARY.into(),
        listen: listen(),
    }
}

/// A change a running gateway makes to its log.
pub enum Logged {
    Appends(&'static str),
    /// A new log takes the place of the old one.
    Replaces(&'static str),
}

/// The process side in memory. `exits_after` is how many checks a
/// started gateway survives; `None` keeps it running, `stubborn` through
/// SIGTERM, `immortal` through SIGKILL too. With `writing`, each check
/// of the gateway changes its log, and it exits with the last change.
#[derive(Default)]
pub struct FakeProcesses {
    pub record: RefCell<Option<GatewayRecord>>,
    pub liveness: Option<Liveness>,
    pub config: RefCell<Option<String>>,
    pub taken: bool,
    pub exits_after: Option<u32>,
    pub stubborn: bool,
    pub immortal: bool,
    pub started: Cell<u32>,
    pub signals: RefCell<Vec<Signal>>,
    pub descendants: Vec<u32>,
    pub programs: BTreeMap<u32, PathBuf>,
    pub tail: String,
    pub log: RefCell<Option<Vec<u8>>>,
    pub writing: RefCell<VecDeque<Logged>>,
    pub writes: bool,
}

impl FakeProcesses {
    /// Whether the signals sent so far ended the gateway.
    pub fn stopped(&self) -> bool {
        let signals = self.signals.borrow();
        !self.immortal
            && (signals.contains(&Signal::Kill)
                || (signals.contains(&Signal::Terminate) && !self.stubborn))
    }
}

pub struct FakeStarted<'a> {
    pub processes: &'a FakeProcesses,
    pub checks: u32,
}

impl StartedGateway for FakeStarted<'_> {
    fn pid(&self) -> u32 {
        4242
    }
    fn exited(&mut self) -> Result<Option<String>> {
        self.checks += 1;
        let killed = self.processes.stopped();
        let crashed = self
            .processes
            .exits_after
            .is_some_and(|checks| self.checks > checks);
        Ok((killed || crashed).then(|| "exit status: 1".to_owned()))
    }
}

impl GatewayProcesses for FakeProcesses {
    fn lock(&self) -> Result<Box<dyn Any>> {
        Ok(Box::new(()))
    }
    fn record(&self) -> Result<Option<GatewayRecord>> {
        Ok(self.record.borrow().clone())
    }
    fn liveness(&self, _: &GatewayRecord) -> Result<Liveness> {
        if self.stopped() {
            return Ok(Liveness::Exited);
        }
        if self.writes {
            let mut writing = self.writing.borrow_mut();
            let mut log = self.log.borrow_mut();
            match writing.pop_front() {
                Some(Logged::Appends(text)) => log
                    .get_or_insert_default()
                    .extend_from_slice(text.as_bytes()),
                Some(Logged::Replaces(text)) => *log = Some(text.as_bytes().to_vec()),
                None => {}
            }
            if writing.is_empty() {
                return Ok(Liveness::Exited);
            }
        }
        Ok(self.liveness.clone().unwrap_or(Liveness::Running))
    }
    fn forget(&self) -> Result<()> {
        *self.record.borrow_mut() = None;
        Ok(())
    }
    fn config(&self) -> Result<Option<String>> {
        Ok(self.config.borrow().clone())
    }
    fn is_free(&self, _: SocketAddr) -> Result<bool> {
        Ok(!self.taken)
    }
    fn start(
        &self,
        binary: &Path,
        config: &str,
        listen: SocketAddr,
    ) -> Result<Box<dyn StartedGateway + '_>> {
        self.started.set(self.started.get() + 1);
        *self.config.borrow_mut() = Some(config.to_owned());
        *self.record.borrow_mut() = Some(GatewayRecord {
            pid: 4242,
            binary: binary.to_owned(),
            listen,
        });
        Ok(Box::new(FakeStarted {
            processes: self,
            checks: 0,
        }))
    }
    fn signal(&self, _: u32, signal: Signal) -> Result<()> {
        self.signals.borrow_mut().push(signal);
        Ok(())
    }
    fn descendants(&self, pid: u32) -> Result<Vec<u32>> {
        Ok(if pid == 1234 {
            self.descendants.clone()
        } else {
            vec![]
        })
    }
    fn program(&self, pid: u32) -> Result<Option<PathBuf>> {
        Ok(self.programs.get(&pid).cloned())
    }
    fn log(&self) -> PathBuf {
        "/state/logs/gateway.log".into()
    }
    fn log_tail(&self, _: usize) -> Result<String> {
        Ok(self.tail.clone())
    }
    fn read_log(&self, offset: u64, max: usize) -> Result<Option<Vec<u8>>> {
        let log = self.log.borrow();
        let Some(log) = log.as_ref() else {
            return Ok(None);
        };
        let offset = usize::try_from(offset).unwrap();
        Ok(log
            .get(offset..)
            .map(|rest| rest[..rest.len().min(max)].to_vec()))
    }
}

/// Answers the health check from the `answers_after`-th call on; lists
/// `models`, or fails to when `None`.
pub struct FakeApi {
    pub answers_after: Option<u32>,
    pub calls: Cell<u32>,
    pub unload_fails: bool,
    pub unloads: Cell<u32>,
    pub models: Option<Vec<RunningModel>>,
    pub streamed: RefCell<Vec<(SocketAddr, String)>>,
}

impl FakeApi {
    pub fn answering_after(calls: u32) -> Self {
        Self {
            answers_after: Some(calls),
            calls: Cell::new(0),
            unload_fails: false,
            unloads: Cell::new(0),
            models: Some(vec![]),
            streamed: RefCell::new(vec![]),
        }
    }
    pub fn silent() -> Self {
        Self {
            answers_after: None,
            ..Self::answering_after(0)
        }
    }
}

impl GatewayApi for FakeApi {
    fn healthy(&self, _: SocketAddr) -> Result<bool> {
        self.calls.set(self.calls.get() + 1);
        Ok(self.answers_after.is_some_and(|n| self.calls.get() > n))
    }
    fn unload_all(&self, _: SocketAddr) -> Result<()> {
        self.unloads.set(self.unloads.get() + 1);
        ensure!(!self.unload_fails, "connection reset");
        Ok(())
    }
    fn running(&self, _: SocketAddr) -> Result<Vec<RunningModel>> {
        self.models
            .clone()
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("GET http://127.0.0.1:8080/running: timed out"))
    }
    fn stream_log(&self, listen: SocketAddr, model: &str, out: &mut dyn Write) -> Result<()> {
        self.streamed.borrow_mut().push((listen, model.to_owned()));
        out.write_all(b"srv  load_model: loading model\n")?;
        Ok(())
    }
}

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct FakeContainers {
    pub local: Vec<String>,
    pub pulled: RefCell<Vec<String>>,
    pub running: Vec<u32>,
}

impl Containers for FakeContainers {
    fn image_exists(&self, image: &str) -> Result<bool> {
        Ok(self.local.iter().any(|local| local == image))
    }
    fn pull(&self, image: &str) -> Result<()> {
        self.pulled.borrow_mut().push(image.to_owned());
        Ok(())
    }
    fn pids(&self, prefix: &str) -> Result<Vec<u32>> {
        assert_eq!(prefix, CONTAINER_PREFIX);
        Ok(self.running.clone())
    }
}

/// The GPU read again on each call: each reading is given once, the last
/// one for good.
pub struct ScriptedGpu {
    pub readings: RefCell<Vec<Probed<Gpu>>>,
    pub reads: Cell<u32>,
}

impl ScriptedGpu {
    pub fn new(readings: Vec<Probed<Gpu>>) -> Self {
        Self {
            readings: RefCell::new(readings),
            reads: Cell::new(0),
        }
    }
}

impl GpuProbe for ScriptedGpu {
    fn gpu(&self) -> Probed<Gpu> {
        self.reads.set(self.reads.get() + 1);
        let mut readings = self.readings.borrow_mut();
        if readings.len() > 1 {
            readings.remove(0)
        } else {
            readings[0].clone()
        }
    }
}

/// The GPU with these processes on it.
pub fn gpu_running(processes: &[(u32, &str, u64)]) -> Probed<Gpu> {
    let mut gpu = gpu();
    gpu.processes = processes
        .iter()
        .map(|&(pid, name, mib)| GpuProcess {
            pid,
            name: name.into(),
            memory: Some(mib << 20),
        })
        .collect();
    Probed::Found(gpu)
}
</file>

<file path="src/application/gateway_config.rs">
//! `gateway config`: the gateway's configuration, generated from the
//! installed inventory, the current builds and the GPU's memory.

use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
use std::net::SocketAddr;

use anyhow::{Context, Result, anyhow, bail, ensure};

use crate::domain::gateway_config::{self, GatewayConfig, Runtime};
use crate::domain::inventory::{Backend, BackendSource, Inventory};
use crate::domain::machine::Probed;
use crate::domain::recipe::recipe;

use super::ports::{BuildStore, GpuProbe, InventoryStore};

/// A configuration, with what starting a gateway on it takes besides.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Prepared {
    pub config: GatewayConfig,
    /// The inventory's address for the gateway.
    pub listen: SocketAddr,
    /// The images the models run, which must be in local storage: the
    /// configuration never pulls one.
    pub images: Vec<String>,
}

/// The generation use case, with the ports it reads.
pub struct Configurator<'a> {
    pub inventory: &'a dyn InventoryStore,
    pub builds: &'a dyn BuildStore,
    pub gpu: &'a dyn GpuProbe,
}

impl Configurator<'_> {
    /// The configuration of the installed inventory, each backend a model
    /// uses running from where it is installed now, the models sharing the
    /// GPU's usable memory.
    pub fn generate(&self) -> Result<GatewayConfig> {
        Ok(self.prepare()?.config)
    }

    /// The configuration, with the address and the images it needs.
    pub fn prepare(&self) -> Result<Prepared> {
        let inventory = self.inventory.load()?.ok_or_else(|| {
            anyhow!(
                "no inventory at {}; install it with `ai-ctl init --models-dir <dir>`",
                self.inventory.location().display()
            )
        })?;
        let runtimes = self.runtimes(&inventory)?;
        let gpu_mib = match self.gpu.gpu() {
            Probed::Found(gpu) => gpu.usable_mib(),
            Probed::Missing => {
                bail!("no NVIDIA GPU found, whose memory the models share; run `ai-ctl doctor`")
            }
            Probed::Failed(err) => bail!("cannot read the GPU's memory: {err}"),
        };
        let config = gateway_config::generate(&inventory, &runtimes, gpu_mib)
            .context("cannot configure the gateway")?;
        let images: BTreeSet<&String> = runtimes
            .values()
            .filter_map(|runtime| match runtime {
                Runtime::Image(image) => Some(image),
                Runtime::Native(_) => None,
            })
            .collect();
        Ok(Prepared {
            config,
            listen: inventory.gateway().listen,
            images: images.into_iter().cloned().collect(),
        })
    }

    /// The runtime of every backend a model uses.
    fn runtimes(&self, inventory: &Inventory) -> Result<BTreeMap<String, Runtime>> {
        let mut runtimes = BTreeMap::new();
        for model in inventory.models() {
            if runtimes.contains_key(&model.backend) {
                continue;
            }
            let backend = inventory
                .backend(&model.backend)
                .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("backend `{}` is not declared", model.backend))?;
            let runtime = self
                .runtime(backend)
                .with_context(|| format!("backend `{}`, which `{}` uses", backend.id, model.id))?;
            runtimes.insert(backend.id.clone(), runtime);
        }
        Ok(runtimes)
    }

    fn runtime(&self, backend: &Backend) -> Result<Runtime> {
        match &backend.source {
            BackendSource::Build(project) => {
                let recipe = recipe(project)
                    .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("ai-ctl has no recipe to build {project}"))?;
                let current = self.builds.current(project)?.ok_or_else(|| {
                    anyhow!("{project} has no build; run `ai-ctl build {project}`")
                })?;
                let (builds, _) = self.builds.builds(project)?;
                ensure!(
                    builds.iter().any(|build| build.id == current),
                    "the current build of {project}, {current}, is not installed; run `ai-ctl build {project}`"
                );
                Ok(Runtime::Native(self.builds.binary(
                    project,
                    &current,
                    recipe.binary(),
                )))
            }
            BackendSource::Image(reference) => Ok(Runtime::Image(reference.clone())),
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::application::fakes::{
        FakeBuilds, FakeGpu, FakeInventory, gpu, inventory, inventory_parts,
    };
    use crate::domain::build::BuildId;
    use crate::domain::inventory::BackendKind;

    fn generate(inventory: FakeInventory, builds: &FakeBuilds) -> Result<GatewayConfig> {
        on_gpu(inventory, builds, Probed::Found(gpu()))
    }

    fn on_gpu(
        inventory: FakeInventory,
        builds: &FakeBuilds,
        gpu: Probed<crate::domain::machine::Gpu>,
    ) -> Result<GatewayConfig> {
        Configurator {
            inventory: &inventory,
            builds,
            gpu: &FakeGpu(gpu),
        }
        .generate()
    }

    fn message(result: Result<GatewayConfig>) -> String {
        format!("{:#}", result.unwrap_err())
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_model_runs_the_current_build() {
        let config = generate(
            FakeInventory(Ok(Some(inventory("/models")))),
            &FakeBuilds::current_b11538(),
        )
        .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            config.models[0].cmd.words().next(),
            Some("/data/builds/llama.cpp/b11538/llama-server")
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_models_share_the_memory_the_driver_does_not_reserve() {
        let config = generate(
            FakeInventory(Ok(Some(inventory("/models")))),
            &FakeBuilds::current_b11538(),
        )
        .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(config.matrix.gpu_mib, 24463 - 388);
    }

    #[test]
    fn without_a_readable_gpu_there_is_no_matrix_to_compute() {
        let missing = message(on_gpu(
            FakeInventory(Ok(Some(inventory("/models")))),
            &FakeBuilds::current_b11538(),
            Probed::Missing,
        ));
        assert_eq!(
            missing,
            "no NVIDIA GPU found, whose memory the models share; run `ai-ctl doctor`"
        );
        let failed = message(on_gpu(
            FakeInventory(Ok(Some(inventory("/models")))),
            &FakeBuilds::current_b11538(),
            Probed::Failed("NVML: driver/library version mismatch".into()),
        ));
        assert_eq!(
            failed,
            "cannot read the GPU's memory: NVML: driver/library version mismatch"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn without_an_inventory_init_is_the_remedy() {
        let message = message(generate(
            FakeInventory(Ok(None)),
            &FakeBuilds::current_b11538(),
        ));
        assert_eq!(
            message,
            "no inventory at /home/user/.config/ai-ctl/inventory.toml; install it with `ai-ctl init --models-dir <dir>`"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_unreadable_inventory_is_an_error() {
        let message = message(generate(
            FakeInventory(Err("invalid TOML".into())),
            &FakeBuilds::current_b11538(),
        ));
        assert_eq!(message, "invalid TOML");
    }

    #[test]
    fn without_a_current_build_the_remedy_is_to_build() {
        let builds = FakeBuilds {
            builds: vec![],
            current: None,
        };
        let message = message(generate(
            FakeInventory(Ok(Some(inventory("/models")))),
            &builds,
        ));
        assert_eq!(
            message,
            "backend `llama-native`, which `qwen3.8-27b` uses: llama.cpp has no build; run `ai-ctl build llama.cpp`"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_current_link_to_a_missing_build_is_an_error() {
        let builds = FakeBuilds {
            builds: vec![],
            current: Some(BuildId::parse("b9").unwrap()),
        };
        let message = message(generate(
            FakeInventory(Ok(Some(inventory("/models")))),
            &builds,
        ));
        assert!(
            message.ends_with(
                "the current build of llama.cpp, b9, is not installed; run `ai-ctl build llama.cpp`"
            ),
            "{message}"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_model_on_an_image_needs_no_build() {
        let mut parts = inventory_parts("/models");
        parts.backends.push(Backend {
            id: "llama-cuda13".into(),
            kind: BackendKind::LlamaServer,
            source: BackendSource::Image("ghcr.io/ggml-org/llama.cpp:server-cuda13".into()),
        });
        parts.models[0].backend = "llama-cuda13".into();
        let installed = Inventory::new(parts).unwrap();
        let builds = FakeBuilds {
            builds: vec![],
            current: None,
        };
        let config = generate(FakeInventory(Ok(Some(installed))), &builds).unwrap();
        let words: Vec<&str> = config.models[0].cmd.words().collect();
        assert_eq!(words[..2], ["podman", "run"]);
        assert!(words.contains(&"ghcr.io/ggml-org/llama.cpp:server-cuda13"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_images_to_pull_are_those_the_models_run() {
        let mut parts = inventory_parts("/models");
        for (id, image) in [
            ("llama-cuda12", "ghcr.io/ggml-org/llama.cpp:server-cuda"),
            ("llama-cuda13", "ghcr.io/ggml-org/llama.cpp:server-cuda13"),
            (
                "llama-cuda13-bis",
                "ghcr.io/ggml-org/llama.cpp:server-cuda13",
            ),
        ] {
            parts.backends.push(Backend {
                id: id.into(),
                kind: BackendKind::LlamaServer,
                source: BackendSource::Image(image.into()),
            });
        }
        let mut second = crate::application::fakes::model();
        second.id = "second".into();
        second.backend = "llama-cuda13-bis".into();
        parts.models[0].backend = "llama-cuda13".into();
        parts.models.push(second);
        parts.gateway.listen = "127.0.0.1:9090".parse().unwrap();
        let prepared = Configurator {
            inventory: &FakeInventory(Ok(Some(Inventory::new(parts).unwrap()))),
            builds: &FakeBuilds::current_b11538(),
            gpu: &FakeGpu(Probed::Found(gpu())),
        }
        .prepare()
        .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            prepared.images,
            ["ghcr.io/ggml-org/llama.cpp:server-cuda13"]
        );
        assert_eq!(prepared.listen, "127.0.0.1:9090".parse().unwrap());
    }

    #[test]
    fn only_the_backends_a_model_uses_are_resolved() {
        let mut parts = inventory_parts("/models");
        parts.backends.push(Backend {
            id: "llama-cuda13".into(),
            kind: BackendKind::LlamaServer,
            source: BackendSource::Image("ghcr.io/ggml-org/llama.cpp:server-cuda13".into()),
        });
        let installed = Inventory::new(parts.clone()).unwrap();
        assert!(
            generate(
                FakeInventory(Ok(Some(installed))),
                &FakeBuilds::current_b11538()
            )
            .is_ok()
        );

        parts.backends.push(Backend {
            id: "whisper".into(),
            kind: BackendKind::LlamaServer,
            source: BackendSource::Build("whisper.cpp".into()),
        });
        parts.models[0].backend = "whisper".into();
        let installed = Inventory::new(parts).unwrap();
        let message = message(generate(
            FakeInventory(Ok(Some(installed))),
            &FakeBuilds::current_b11538(),
        ));
        assert_eq!(
            message,
            "backend `whisper`, which `qwen3.8-27b` uses: ai-ctl has no recipe to build whisper.cpp"
        );
    }
}

<file path="src/application/gateway.rs">
//! `gateway install`: installs a llama-swap release, verified, and makes it
//! the current one.

use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use anyhow::{Context, Result, anyhow, ensure};

use crate::domain::gateway::{BINARY, PINNED, Release, Sha256, checksum, reports};

use super::build::summary;
use super::ports::{Downloads, GatewayStore, Programs, StagedRelease};

/// A step of an installation, for the user to follow.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Event {
    /// Reading the checksums file published with the release.
    Checksums {
        url: String,
    },
    Downloading {
        url: String,
    },
    /// The archive's SHA-256 matches the expected one.
    Verified {
        sha256: Sha256,
    },
    /// Running the new binary.
    Testing,
    /// Something went wrong that does not fail the installation.
    Warning(String),
}

/// What an installation did.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Outcome {
    /// Downloaded, verified and installed.
    Downloaded,
    /// Installed already; made current again.
    Reused,
    /// Installed already, and current.
    Unchanged,
}

/// A release installed and current.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Installation {
    pub release: Release,
    pub binary: PathBuf,
    pub outcome: Outcome,
    /// The release that was current before, if another one.
    pub previous: Option<Release>,
}

/// The installation use case, with the ports it drives.
pub struct Installer<'a> {
    pub downloads: &'a dyn Downloads,
    pub store: &'a dyn GatewayStore,
    pub programs: &'a dyn Programs,
}

impl Installer<'_> {
    /// Installs `release`, or the pinned one, and makes it current. A
    /// release not installed yet is downloaded, its archive's SHA-256
    /// checked, and its binary run; nothing is installed when a step fails.
    pub fn install(
        &self,
        release: Option<Release>,
        events: &mut dyn FnMut(Event),
    ) -> Result<Installation> {
        ensure!(
            cfg!(all(target_os = "linux", target_arch = "x86_64")),
            "ai-ctl installs llama-swap for Linux on x86_64 only"
        );
        let release = release.unwrap_or(PINNED.release);
        let _lock = self.store.lock()?;
        let previous = self.store.current()?;

        let outcome = if !self.store.is_installed(release)? {
            let expected = self.expected_sha256(release, events)?;
            let staged = self.store.stage(release)?;
            let installed = self
                .fetch(&staged, &expected, events)
                .and_then(|()| self.store.publish(&staged));
            if let Err(err) = installed {
                if let Err(discard) = self.store.discard(&staged) {
                    events(Event::Warning(format!("{discard:#}")));
                }
                return Err(err);
            }
            Outcome::Downloaded
        } else if previous == Some(release) {
            Outcome::Unchanged
        } else {
            Outcome::Reused
        };
        if outcome != Outcome::Unchanged {
            self.store
                .set_current(release)
                .with_context(|| format!("llama-swap {release} is installed but not current"))?;
        }
        Ok(Installation {
            release,
            binary: self.store.binary(release),
            outcome,
            previous: previous.filter(|previous| *previous != release),
        })
    }

    /// The pinned release's digest, or the one its checksums file gives.
    fn expected_sha256(&self, release: Release, events: &mut dyn FnMut(Event)) -> Result<Sha256> {
        if release == PINNED.release {
            return Ok(Sha256::parse(PINNED.sha256)?);
        }
        let url = release.url(&release.checksums());
        events(Event::Checksums { url: url.clone() });
        let checksums = self
            .downloads
            .text(&url)
            .with_context(|| format!("cannot read the checksums of llama-swap {release}"))?;
        let archive = release.archive();
        checksum(&checksums, &archive)
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("{url} gives no SHA-256 for {archive}"))
    }

    /// Downloads the archive into the staged directory, checks it, unpacks
    /// the binary and runs it.
    fn fetch(
        &self,
        staged: &StagedRelease,
        expected: &Sha256,
        events: &mut dyn FnMut(Event),
    ) -> Result<()> {
        let release = staged.release;
        let archive = release.archive();
        let url = release.url(&archive);
        events(Event::Downloading { url: url.clone() });
        self.downloads.download(&url, &staged.archive)?;
        let actual = self.store.sha256(&staged.archive)?;
        ensure!(
            actual == *expected,
            "{archive} has the SHA-256 {actual}, not the expected {expected}: the download is corrupt or was tampered with"
        );
        events(Event::Verified { sha256: actual });
        let binary = self.store.unpack(staged)?;
        events(Event::Testing);
        self.smoke_test(&binary, release)
    }

    /// The binary runs, and is the release it was downloaded as.
    fn smoke_test(&self, binary: &Path, release: Release) -> Result<()> {
        let output = self.programs.run(binary, &["-version"])?;
        ensure!(
            output.success,
            "the new {BINARY} does not run: `{BINARY} -version` failed: {}",
            summary(&output)
        );
        ensure!(
            reports(&output.stdout, release),
            "the new {BINARY} is not {release}: `{BINARY} -version` printed {}",
            summary(&output)
        );
        Ok(())
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use std::any::Any;
    use std::cell::{Cell, RefCell};

    use anyhow::bail;

    use super::*;
    use crate::application::fakes::printed;
    use crate::application::ports::ProgramOutput;

    const V262_SHA256: &str = "871b3ed7891f8ce057428f5a34d38298e06c36c6848c1136e05308eb4303af50";

    /// The release server: v262 and v263 publish their checksums; every
    /// download succeeds, and the store gives the digest of `content`.
    #[derive(Default)]
    struct FakeDownloads {
        texts: RefCell<Vec<String>>,
        downloads: RefCell<Vec<(String, PathBuf)>>,
        fail: bool,
    }

    impl Downloads for FakeDownloads {
        fn download(&self, url: &str, to: &Path) -> Result<()> {
            if self.fail {
                bail!("cannot download {url}: curl: (6) Could not resolve host: github.com");
            }
            self.downloads
                .borrow_mut()
                .push((url.to_owned(), to.to_owned()));
            Ok(())
        }
        fn text(&self, url: &str) -> Result<String> {
            self.texts.borrow_mut().push(url.to_owned());
            Ok(match url.rsplit('/').next() {
                Some("llama-swap_263_checksums.txt") => {
                    include_str!("../../tests/fixtures/llama-swap-263-checksums.txt").to_owned()
                }
                Some("llama-swap_262_checksums.txt") => {
                    format!("{V262_SHA256}  llama-swap_262_linux_amd64.tar.gz\n")
                }
                Some("llama-swap_261_checksums.txt") => {
                    format!("{V262_SHA256}  llama-swap_261_linux_arm64.tar.gz\n")
                }
                _ => {
                    bail!("cannot download {url}: curl: (22) The requested URL returned error: 404")
                }
            })
        }
    }

    /// Releases in memory; `locked` stands for another ai-ctl holding the
    /// lock, and `digest` is what any downloaded archive hashes to.
    struct FakeStore {
        locked: bool,
        installed: RefCell<Vec<Release>>,
        current: RefCell<Option<Release>>,
        digest: Sha256,
        staged: RefCell<Vec<Release>>,
        discarded: Cell<usize>,
    }

    impl FakeStore {
        fn new() -> Self {
            Self {
                locked: false,
                installed: RefCell::new(vec![]),
                current: RefCell::new(None),
                digest: Sha256::parse(PINNED.sha256).unwrap(),
                staged: RefCell::new(vec![]),
                discarded: Cell::new(0),
            }
        }
    }

    impl GatewayStore for FakeStore {
        fn lock(&self) -> Result<Box<dyn Any>> {
            ensure!(!self.locked, "another ai-ctl is installing llama-swap");
            Ok(Box::new(()))
        }
        fn is_installed(&self, release: Release) -> Result<bool> {
            Ok(self.installed.borrow().contains(&release))
        }
        fn current(&self) -> Result<Option<Release>> {
            Ok(*self.current.borrow())
        }
        fn binary(&self, release: Release) -> PathBuf {
            PathBuf::from(format!("/data/llama-swap/{release}/llama-swap"))
        }
        fn stage(&self, release: Release) -> Result<StagedRelease> {
            self.staged.borrow_mut().push(release);
            Ok(StagedRelease {
                release,
                dir: "/data/llama-swap/.staging".into(),
                archive: format!("/data/llama-swap/.staging/{}", release.archive()).into(),
            })
        }
        fn sha256(&self, _: &Path) -> Result<Sha256> {
            Ok(self.digest.clone())
        }
        fn unpack(&self, _: &StagedRelease) -> Result<PathBuf> {
            Ok("/data/llama-swap/.staging/llama-swap".into())
        }
        fn publish(&self, staged: &StagedRelease) -> Result<()> {
            self.installed.borrow_mut().push(staged.release);
            Ok(())
        }
        fn discard(&self, _: &StagedRelease) -> Result<()> {
            self.discarded.set(self.discarded.get() + 1);
            Ok(())
        }
        fn set_current(&self, release: Release) -> Result<()> {
            *self.current.borrow_mut() = Some(release);
            Ok(())
        }
    }

    /// A llama-swap binary answering `-version`.
    struct FakeLlamaSwap(ProgramOutput);

    impl FakeLlamaSwap {
        fn of(release: Release) -> Self {
            Self(printed(
                true,
                &format!("version: {release} (e33e97b), built at 2026-10-10T06:17:54Z\n"),
                "",
            ))
        }
    }

    impl Programs for FakeLlamaSwap {
        fn run(&self, _: &Path, args: &[&str]) -> Result<ProgramOutput> {
            assert_eq!(args, ["-version"]);
            Ok(self.0.clone())
        }
    }

    struct World {
        downloads: FakeDownloads,
        store: FakeStore,
        programs: FakeLlamaSwap,
    }

    impl World {
        fn new() -> Self {
            Self {
                downloads: FakeDownloads::default(),
                store: FakeStore::new(),
                programs: FakeLlamaSwap::of(PINNED.release),
            }
        }

        fn install(&self, release: Option<u32>) -> (Result<Installation>, Vec<Event>) {
            let installer = Installer {
                downloads: &self.downloads,
                store: &self.store,
                programs: &self.programs,
            };
            let mut events = Vec::new();
            let release = release.map(|number| release_of(&number.to_string()));
            let result = installer.install(release, &mut |event| events.push(event));
            (result, events)
        }
    }

    fn release_of(text: &str) -> Release {
        Release::parse(text).unwrap()
    }

    fn message(result: Result<Installation>) -> String {
        format!("{:#}", result.unwrap_err())
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_pinned_release_is_installed_against_its_own_digest() {
        let world = World::new();
        let (result, events) = world.install(None);
        let installation = result.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            installation,
            Installation {
                release: PINNED.release,
                binary: "/data/llama-swap/v263/llama-swap".into(),
                outcome: Outcome::Downloaded,
                previous: None,
            }
        );
        let url = "https://github.com/mostlygeek/llama-swap/releases/download/v263/llama-swap_263_linux_amd64.tar.gz";
        assert_eq!(
            events,
            [
                Event::Downloading { url: url.into() },
                Event::Verified {
                    sha256: Sha256::parse(PINNED.sha256).unwrap()
                },
                Event::Testing,
            ]
        );
        assert!(world.downloads.texts.borrow().is_empty());
        assert_eq!(
            *world.downloads.downloads.borrow(),
            [(
                url.to_owned(),
                "/data/llama-swap/.staging/llama-swap_263_linux_amd64.tar.gz".into()
            )]
        );
        assert_eq!(*world.store.current.borrow(), Some(PINNED.release));
    }

    #[test]
    fn another_release_is_checked_against_its_published_checksums() {
        let mut world = World::new();
        world.store.digest = Sha256::parse(V262_SHA256).unwrap();
        world.programs = FakeLlamaSwap::of(release_of("262"));
        *world.store.installed.borrow_mut() = vec![PINNED.release];
        *world.store.current.borrow_mut() = Some(PINNED.release);
        let (result, events) = world.install(Some(262));
        let installation = result.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(installation.outcome, Outcome::Downloaded);
        assert_eq!(installation.previous, Some(PINNED.release));
        assert_eq!(
            events[0],
            Event::Checksums {
                url: "https://github.com/mostlygeek/llama-swap/releases/download/v262/llama-swap_262_checksums.txt".into()
            }
        );
        assert_eq!(*world.store.current.borrow(), Some(release_of("262")));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_digest_mismatch_installs_nothing() {
        let mut world = World::new();
        world.store.digest = Sha256::parse(V262_SHA256).unwrap();
        let (result, events) = world.install(None);
        let message = message(result);
        assert!(
            message.contains(&format!(
                "has the SHA-256 {V262_SHA256}, not the expected {}",
                PINNED.sha256
            )),
            "{message}"
        );
        assert!(message.contains("tampered with"), "{message}");
        assert!(!events.contains(&Event::Testing));
        assert!(world.store.installed.borrow().is_empty());
        assert_eq!(world.store.discarded.get(), 1);
        assert_eq!(*world.store.current.borrow(), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_release_whose_checksums_omit_the_archive_is_refused() {
        let world = World::new();
        let message = message(world.install(Some(261)).0);
        assert!(
            message.ends_with("llama-swap_261_checksums.txt gives no SHA-256 for llama-swap_261_linux_amd64.tar.gz"),
            "{message}"
        );
        assert!(world.store.staged.borrow().is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_release_that_does_not_exist_is_refused_before_any_download() {
        let world = World::new();
        let message = message(world.install(Some(9999)).0);
        assert!(
            message.starts_with("cannot read the checksums of llama-swap v9999: cannot download"),
            "{message}"
        );
        assert!(message.contains("404"), "{message}");
        assert!(world.downloads.downloads.borrow().is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_failed_download_installs_nothing() {
        let mut world = World::new();
        world.downloads.fail = true;
        let message = message(world.install(None).0);
        assert!(message.contains("Could not resolve host"), "{message}");
        assert_eq!(world.store.discarded.get(), 1);
        assert!(world.store.installed.borrow().is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_binary_that_does_not_run_is_discarded() {
        let mut world = World::new();
        world.programs = FakeLlamaSwap(printed(false, "", "exec format error\n"));
        let message = message(world.install(None).0);
        assert_eq!(
            message,
            "the new llama-swap does not run: `llama-swap -version` failed: exec format error"
        );
        assert_eq!(world.store.discarded.get(), 1);
        assert_eq!(*world.store.current.borrow(), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_binary_of_another_release_is_discarded() {
        let mut world = World::new();
        world.programs = FakeLlamaSwap::of(release_of("262"));
        let message = message(world.install(None).0);
        assert!(
            message.starts_with(
                "the new llama-swap is not v263: `llama-swap -version` printed version: v262"
            ),
            "{message}"
        );
        assert_eq!(world.store.discarded.get(), 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_installed_release_is_made_current_without_a_download() {
        let world = World::new();
        *world.store.installed.borrow_mut() = vec![release_of("262"), PINNED.release];
        *world.store.current.borrow_mut() = Some(release_of("262"));
        let (result, events) = world.install(None);
        let installation = result.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(installation.outcome, Outcome::Reused);
        assert_eq!(installation.previous, Some(release_of("262")));
        assert!(events.is_empty());
        assert!(world.store.staged.borrow().is_empty());
        assert_eq!(*world.store.current.borrow(), Some(PINNED.release));
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_current_release_is_left_as_it_is() {
        let world = World::new();
        *world.store.installed.borrow_mut() = vec![PINNED.release];
        *world.store.current.borrow_mut() = Some(PINNED.release);
        let installation = world.install(None).0.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(installation.outcome, Outcome::Unchanged);
        assert_eq!(installation.previous, None);
        assert!(world.store.staged.borrow().is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_installation_waits_for_no_one() {
        let mut world = World::new();
        world.store.locked = true;
        let message = message(world.install(None).0);
        assert_eq!(message, "another ai-ctl is installing llama-swap");
        assert!(world.store.staged.borrow().is_empty());
    }
}
</file>

<file path="src/application/init.rs">
//! `init`: installs the shipped inventory.

use std::path::Path;

use anyhow::{Result, bail};

use super::ports::{Installed, InventoryStore};

/// Installs the inventory with `models_dir` substituted; refuses to replace an
/// installed one.
pub fn init(store: &dyn InventoryStore, models_dir: &Path) -> Result<()> {
    match store.install(models_dir)? {
        Installed::Written => Ok(()),
        Installed::AlreadyExists => bail!(
            "{} already exists; edit it, or move it away to install the shipped inventory again",
            store.location().display()
        ),
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use std::cell::RefCell;
    use std::path::PathBuf;

    use anyhow::anyhow;

    use super::*;
    use crate::domain::inventory::Inventory;

    struct Store {
        outcome: Result<Installed, String>,
        asked_for: RefCell<Option<PathBuf>>,
    }

    impl Store {
        fn new(outcome: Result<Installed, String>) -> Self {
            Self {
                outcome,
                asked_for: RefCell::new(None),
            }
        }
    }

    impl InventoryStore for Store {
        fn location(&self) -> &Path {
            Path::new("/home/user/.config/ai-ctl/inventory.toml")
        }

        fn load(&self) -> Result<Option<Inventory>> {
            unreachable!("init never loads the inventory")
        }

        fn install(&self, models_dir: &Path) -> Result<Installed> {
            *self.asked_for.borrow_mut() = Some(models_dir.to_owned());
            self.outcome.clone().map_err(|message| anyhow!(message))
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn installs_with_the_given_models_directory() {
        let store = Store::new(Ok(Installed::Written));
        init(&store, Path::new("/data/models")).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            store.asked_for.borrow().as_deref(),
            Some(Path::new("/data/models"))
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn refuses_to_replace_an_installed_inventory() {
        let store = Store::new(Ok(Installed::AlreadyExists));
        let message = init(&store, Path::new("/data/models"))
            .unwrap_err()
            .to_string();
        assert!(
            message.contains("inventory.toml already exists"),
            "{message}"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn passes_store_errors_through() {
        let store = Store::new(Err("/data/models is not a directory".into()));
        let message = init(&store, Path::new("/data/models"))
            .unwrap_err()
            .to_string();
        assert_eq!(message, "/data/models is not a directory");
    }
}
</file>

<file path="src/application/mod.rs">
//! Application layer: the use cases, and the ports they need.
//!
//! A use case orchestrates calls to ports — traits for what lies outside the
//! process, such as the GPU, the file system or the gateway's API. Depends
//! only on `domain`; the adapters behind the ports live in `infrastructure`.

pub mod build;
pub mod doctor;
#[cfg(test)]
mod fakes;
pub mod gateway;
pub mod gateway_config;
pub mod init;
pub mod lifecycle;
pub mod ports;
pub mod toolchain;
</file>

<file path="src/application/ports.rs">
//! Ports: what the use cases need from outside the process.

use std::any::Any;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::io::Write;
use std::net::SocketAddr;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use anyhow::Result;

use crate::domain::bench::{Message, Sample};
use crate::domain::build::{Build, BuildId, CommitId};
use crate::domain::gateway::{Release, Sha256};
use crate::domain::inventory::Inventory;
use crate::domain::machine::{
    CudaRepository, CudaToolkit, Gpu, HostCompiler, Probed, Tool, Version,
};
use crate::domain::recipe::Recipe;

/// Reads the NVIDIA GPU and its driver.
pub trait GpuProbe {
    fn gpu(&self) -> Probed<Gpu>;
}

/// Reads the host, read-only: installed tools, power state, files.
pub trait HostProbe {
    /// The toolkit of the `nvcc` a build would use.
    fn cuda_toolkit(&self) -> Probed<CudaToolkit>;
    /// The GNU C++ compilers on the `PATH`, the default `g++` first.
    fn host_compilers(&self) -> Probed<Vec<HostCompiler>>;
    fn tool_version(&self, tool: Tool) -> Probed<Version>;
    /// The CDI device names `nvidia-ctk` lists, such as `nvidia.com/gpu=all`.
    fn cdi_devices(&self) -> Probed<Vec<String>>;
    /// Whether an external power supply is online; `Missing` when the machine
    /// reports none.
    fn ac_power(&self) -> Probed<bool>;
    /// The active power profile, such as `performance`.
    fn power_profile(&self) -> Probed<String>;
    fn is_file(&self, path: &Path) -> bool;
    /// The Fedora release, from `/etc/os-release`; `Missing` on another
    /// distribution.
    fn fedora_release(&self) -> Probed<u32>;
    /// The enabled NVIDIA CUDA repositories, read from dnf's cache.
    fn cuda_repositories(&self) -> Probed<Vec<CudaRepository>>;
    /// The installed packages whose name starts with `cuda-toolkit`.
    fn toolkit_packages(&self) -> Probed<Vec<String>>;
}

/// What [`InventoryStore::install`] did.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Installed {
    Written,
    /// An inventory was already there; it was left untouched.
    AlreadyExists,
}

/// The installed inventory file.
pub trait InventoryStore {
    /// Where the inventory lives.
    fn location(&self) -> &Path;
    /// The installed inventory, or `None` when none is installed.
    fn load(&self) -> Result<Option<Inventory>>;
    /// Installs the shipped inventory with `models_dir` substituted, unless
    /// one is already installed.
    fn install(&self, models_dir: &Path) -> Result<Installed>;
}

/// What [`Sources::checkout`] checked out.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Checkout {
    /// The working tree, at the commit.
    pub dir: PathBuf,
    pub commit: CommitId,
    /// The tags pointing at the commit.
    pub tags: Vec<String>,
}

/// A project's sources, from its git repository.
pub trait Sources {
    /// The tags of the project's repository that may be releases, read from
    /// the remote without cloning; the recipe's pattern sorts them out.
    fn release_tags(&self, recipe: &Recipe) -> Result<Vec<String>>;
    /// Clones the project's repository, or fetches it when it is already
    /// cloned, and checks out `reference`: a tag, a branch or a commit.
    fn checkout(&self, recipe: &Recipe, reference: &str) -> Result<Checkout>;
}

/// One CMake build: a fresh build tree for `source`, configured with
/// `flags`, building `target` only.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct CompileJob<'a> {
    pub project: &'a str,
    pub source: &'a Path,
    pub flags: &'a BTreeMap<String, String>,
    pub target: &'a str,
    /// Where the target's binary lands, relative to the build tree.
    pub artifact: &'a str,
    /// Parallel compile jobs; `None` for one per processor.
    pub jobs: Option<usize>,
}

/// What a successful [`Cmake::compile`] produced.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Compiled {
    /// The target's binary, in the build tree.
    pub artifact: PathBuf,
    /// The complete output of CMake and the compilers.
    pub log: PathBuf,
}

/// How far a compilation is.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Progress {
    Configuring,
    /// `done` of `total` build steps finished.
    Building {
        done: u32,
        total: u32,
    },
}

/// Compiles a CMake project.
pub trait Cmake {
    /// Configures a fresh build tree and builds the job's target, writing
    /// every line of output to the build log. A failure names the log and
    /// quotes its last lines.
    fn compile(&self, job: &CompileJob, progress: &mut dyn FnMut(Progress)) -> Result<Compiled>;
    /// Removes the project's build tree.
    fn clean(&self, project: &str) -> Result<()>;
}

/// A build made but not yet installed: its directory, out of sight of the
/// installed builds, holds the binary and the metadata.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Staged {
    pub dir: PathBuf,
    pub binary: PathBuf,
    pub build: Build,
}

/// A directory of the store that is not a readable build.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Unreadable {
    pub dir: PathBuf,
    pub reason: String,
}

/// The installed builds of every project, with each project's current one.
pub trait BuildStore {
    /// Takes the lock every change to the builds holds, released when the
    /// returned guard is dropped; fails at once when another ai-ctl holds it.
    fn lock(&self) -> Result<Box<dyn Any>>;
    /// The project's installed builds, and the directories that are not.
    fn builds(&self, project: &str) -> Result<(Vec<Build>, Vec<Unreadable>)>;
    /// The build the project's `current` link names, if any.
    fn current(&self, project: &str) -> Result<Option<BuildId>>;
    /// The installed binary of a build.
    fn binary(&self, project: &str, id: &BuildId, name: &str) -> PathBuf;
    /// Copies `artifact` and `log` beside the build's metadata, out of sight.
    fn stage(&self, build: &Build, artifact: &Path, log: &Path) -> Result<Staged>;
    /// Installs a staged build under its id; with `replace`, an installed
    /// build of the same id gives way to it.
    fn publish(&self, staged: &Staged, replace: bool) -> Result<()>;
    /// Removes a staged build.
    fn discard(&self, staged: &Staged) -> Result<()>;
    /// Points the project's `current` link at an installed build.
    fn set_current(&self, project: &str, id: &BuildId) -> Result<()>;
    /// Removes an installed build.
    fn remove(&self, project: &str, id: &BuildId) -> Result<()>;
    /// Whether `program` lies in the store: the binary of a build, installed
    /// or removed since.
    fn contains(&self, program: &Path) -> bool;
}

/// What a program printed, and whether it succeeded.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct ProgramOutput {
    pub success: bool,
    pub stdout: String,
    pub stderr: String,
}

/// Runs a program briefly, such as a new build's smoke test.
pub trait Programs {
    fn run(&self, program: &Path, args: &[&str]) -> Result<ProgramOutput>;
}

/// Files fetched over HTTPS.
pub trait Downloads {
    /// Saves the file at `url` as `to`.
    fn download(&self, url: &str, to: &Path) -> Result<()>;
    /// The content of the text file at `url`.
    fn text(&self, url: &str) -> Result<String>;
}

/// A gateway release being installed: a directory out of sight of the
/// installed releases, where its archive is downloaded and unpacked.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct StagedRelease {
    pub release: Release,
    pub dir: PathBuf,
    pub archive: PathBuf,
}

/// The installed releases of the gateway, with the current one.
pub trait GatewayStore {
    /// Takes the lock every change to the releases holds, released when the
    /// returned guard is dropped; fails at once when another ai-ctl holds it.
    fn lock(&self) -> Result<Box<dyn Any>>;
    /// Whether the release's binary is installed.
    fn is_installed(&self, release: Release) -> Result<bool>;
    /// The release the `current` link names, if any.
    fn current(&self) -> Result<Option<Release>>;
    /// The installed binary of a release.
    fn binary(&self, release: Release) -> PathBuf;
    /// An empty directory for the release, out of sight.
    fn stage(&self, release: Release) -> Result<StagedRelease>;
    /// The SHA-256 of a file.
    fn sha256(&self, file: &Path) -> Result<Sha256>;
    /// Extracts the binary from the staged archive and removes the archive;
    /// returns the binary.
    fn unpack(&self, staged: &StagedRelease) -> Result<PathBuf>;
    /// Installs a staged release; fails when the release is there already.
    fn publish(&self, staged: &StagedRelease) -> Result<()>;
    /// Removes a staged release.
    fn discard(&self, staged: &StagedRelease) -> Result<()>;
    /// Points the `current` link at an installed release.
    fn set_current(&self, release: Release) -> Result<()>;
}

/// The gateway ai-ctl started, as its state directory records it.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct GatewayRecord {
    pub pid: u32,
    /// The llama-swap binary it runs.
    pub binary: PathBuf,
    pub listen: SocketAddr,
}

/// What runs under a recorded gateway's PID now.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Liveness {
    /// The recorded binary: the gateway runs.
    Running,
    /// Nothing: the gateway has exited.
    Exited,
    /// Another program, named when it can be read: the PID was reused.
    Reused(Option<PathBuf>),
}

/// A signal for a gateway to stop.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Signal {
    /// SIGTERM: llama-swap stops its models, then exits.
    Terminate,
    /// SIGKILL: the process ends at once, its models left running.
    Kill,
}

/// A gateway just started, still a child of this process.
pub trait StartedGateway {
    fn pid(&self) -> u32;
    /// Its exit status, once it has exited.
    fn exited(&mut self) -> Result<Option<String>>;
}

/// The gateway as a process of this machine, and the files it keeps: its
/// record, its configuration and its log.
pub trait GatewayProcesses {
    /// Takes the lock that starting and stopping the gateway hold, released
    /// when the returned guard is dropped; fails at once when another ai-ctl
    /// holds it.
    fn lock(&self) -> Result<Box<dyn Any>>;
    /// The recorded gateway, if any.
    fn record(&self) -> Result<Option<GatewayRecord>>;
    fn liveness(&self, record: &GatewayRecord) -> Result<Liveness>;
    /// Removes the record.
    fn forget(&self) -> Result<()>;
    /// The configuration the gateway was last started with.
    fn config(&self) -> Result<Option<String>>;
    /// Whether a server could listen on `listen` now.
    fn is_free(&self, listen: SocketAddr) -> Result<bool>;
    /// Writes `config`, then starts `binary` on it, listening on `listen`,
    /// detached from the terminal, its output in a new log; and records it.
    fn start(
        &self,
        binary: &Path,
        config: &str,
        listen: SocketAddr,
    ) -> Result<Box<dyn StartedGateway + '_>>;
    /// Sends `signal` to the process `pid`; a process already gone is not
    /// an error.
    fn signal(&self, pid: u32, signal: Signal) -> Result<()>;
    /// The processes descending from `pid`, its children first.
    fn descendants(&self, pid: u32) -> Result<Vec<u32>>;
    /// The program the process `pid` runs, as `/proc/<pid>/exe` names it;
    /// `None` when the process is gone or belongs to another user.
    fn program(&self, pid: u32) -> Result<Option<PathBuf>>;
    /// The gateway's log.
    fn log(&self) -> PathBuf;
    /// The last `lines` lines of the log.
    fn log_tail(&self, lines: usize) -> Result<String>;
    /// At most `max` bytes of the log from `offset` on, empty at its end;
    /// `None` when there is no log, or one shorter than `offset`: a new log
    /// replaced it.
    fn read_log(&self, offset: u64, max: usize) -> Result<Option<Vec<u8>>>;
}

/// A model the gateway runs, as llama-swap reports it.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct RunningModel {
    pub id: String,
    /// `starting`, `ready` or `stopping`.
    pub state: String,
}

/// llama-swap's HTTP API.
pub trait GatewayApi {
    /// Whether a gateway listening on `listen` answers its health check;
    /// `false` while nothing listens there.
    fn healthy(&self, listen: SocketAddr) -> Result<bool>;
    /// Stops every model the gateway runs, and returns once they are
    /// stopped.
    fn unload_all(&self, listen: SocketAddr) -> Result<()>;
    /// The models the gateway runs, loading or stopping included.
    fn running(&self, listen: SocketAddr) -> Result<Vec<RunningModel>>;
    /// Copies to `out` the output of `model` the gateway kept, then its live
    /// output as it comes, until the gateway ends the stream.
    fn stream_log(&self, listen: SocketAddr, model: &str, out: &mut dyn Write) -> Result<()>;
}

/// A chat completion request, streamed.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct ChatRequest {
    /// The model's ID: llama-swap routes on it, and llama-server answers to
    /// it.
    pub model: String,
    pub messages: Vec<Message>,
    /// Tokens to generate at most.
    pub max_tokens: u32,
    /// Whether to generate `max_tokens` exactly, the end of sequence ignored.
    pub ignore_eos: bool,
    /// The sampling seed, so two runs generate the same tokens.
    pub seed: u32,
}

/// A streamed reply, timed.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct Reply {
    pub sample: Sample,
    /// The reply's text and reasoning, which a session sends back.
    pub content: String,
