        Ok(entries) => entries,
        Err(err) if err.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(()),
        Err(err) => return Err(err).with_context(|| format!("cannot read {}", dir.display())),
    };
    for entry in entries {
        let entry = entry.with_context(|| format!("cannot read {}", dir.display()))?;
        let name = entry.file_name();
        if !name.to_string_lossy().starts_with('.') || name == ".lock" {
            continue;
        }
        let path = entry.path();
        if path.is_symlink() || path.is_file() {
            fs::remove_file(&path).with_context(|| format!("cannot remove {}", path.display()))?;
        } else {
            remove_dir_if_present(&path)?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn a_missing_directory_is_already_removed() {
        let root = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        remove_dir_if_present(&root.path().join("absent")).unwrap();
        let dir = root.path().join("tree/leaf");
        fs::create_dir_all(&dir).unwrap();
        fs::write(dir.join("file"), "x").unwrap();
        remove_dir_if_present(&root.path().join("tree")).unwrap();
        assert!(!root.path().join("tree").exists());
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_second_lock_is_refused_until_the_first_is_dropped() {
        let root = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let dir = root.path().join("store");
        let _exclusive = crate::infrastructure::command::exclusive();
        let first = lock(&dir, "changing the store").unwrap();
        let message = lock(&dir, "changing the store").unwrap_err().to_string();
        assert!(
            message.starts_with("another ai-ctl is changing the store ("),
            "{message}"
        );
        drop(first);
        lock(&dir, "changing the store").unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_link_is_replaced_in_place() {
        let root = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let link = root.path().join("current");
        let temporary = root.path().join(".current-1");
        replace_link(&link, "a", &temporary).unwrap();
        replace_link(&link, "b", &temporary).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(fs::read_link(&link).unwrap(), Path::new("b"));
        assert!(temporary.symlink_metadata().is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn hidden_entries_go_but_the_lock_and_the_rest_stay() {
        let root = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let dir = root.path();
        fs::create_dir_all(dir.join(".staging-1/sub")).unwrap();
        fs::write(dir.join(".partial"), "x").unwrap();
        std::os::unix::fs::symlink("v1", dir.join(".current-1")).unwrap();
        fs::write(dir.join(".lock"), "").unwrap();
        fs::create_dir(dir.join("v1")).unwrap();
        remove_hidden(dir).unwrap();
        let mut names: Vec<String> = fs::read_dir(dir)
            .unwrap()
            .map(|entry| entry.unwrap().file_name().to_string_lossy().into_owned())
            .collect();
        names.sort();
        assert_eq!(names, [".lock", "v1"]);
        remove_hidden(&dir.join("absent")).unwrap();
    }
}
</file>

<file path="src/infrastructure/gateway_api.rs">
//! llama-swap's HTTP API, over the HTTP/1.0 client of [`super::http`].

use std::io::Write;
use std::net::SocketAddr;
use std::time::Duration;

use anyhow::{Context, Result, anyhow, bail};
use serde::Deserialize;

use super::http::{self, Response, copy_stream, request};
use crate::application::ports::{GatewayApi, RunningModel};

const HEALTH_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(2);
/// llama-swap lists its models from memory.
const RUNNING_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(5);
/// llama-swap answers an unload once every model is stopped: SIGTERM, then
/// SIGKILL after each model's `unloadTimeout`, 10 s by default.
const UNLOAD_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(120);
/// How much of an error's body a log stream reads, to quote it.
const MAX_ERROR_BODY: usize = 64 * 1024;

/// The body of `/running`, of which ai-ctl reads each model's ID and state.
#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct Running {
    running: Vec<RunningEntry>,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct RunningEntry {
    model: String,
    state: String,
}

/// The models a `/running` body lists.
pub fn parse_running(body: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<RunningModel>> {
    let running: Running =
        serde_json::from_slice(body).context("the list of running models is malformed")?;
    Ok(running
        .running
        .into_iter()
        .map(|entry| RunningModel {
            id: entry.model,
            state: entry.state,
        })
        .collect())
}

/// The error a response other than 200 gives `method path`.
fn unexpected(listen: SocketAddr, method: &str, path: &str, response: &Response) -> anyhow::Error {
    anyhow!(
        "{method} http://{listen}{path} answered {}: {}",
        response.status,
        String::from_utf8_lossy(&response.body).trim()
    )
}

/// llama-swap's API on the loopback.
pub struct LlamaSwapApi;

impl GatewayApi for LlamaSwapApi {
    fn healthy(&self, listen: SocketAddr) -> Result<bool> {
        Ok(request(listen, "GET", "/health", HEALTH_TIMEOUT)?
            .is_some_and(|response| response.status == 200))
    }

    fn unload_all(&self, listen: SocketAddr) -> Result<()> {
        let path = "/api/models/unload";
        let response = request(listen, "POST", path, UNLOAD_TIMEOUT)?
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("nothing listens on {listen}"))?;
        if response.status != 200 {
            return Err(unexpected(listen, "POST", path, &response));
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    fn running(&self, listen: SocketAddr) -> Result<Vec<RunningModel>> {
        let path = "/running";
        let response = request(listen, "GET", path, RUNNING_TIMEOUT)?
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("nothing listens on {listen}"))?;
        if response.status != 200 {
            return Err(unexpected(listen, "GET", path, &response));
        }
        parse_running(&response.body).with_context(|| format!("GET http://{listen}{path}"))
    }

    fn stream_log(&self, listen: SocketAddr, model: &str, out: &mut dyn Write) -> Result<()> {
        let path = format!("/logs/stream/{model}");
        // A stream waits as long as the model is silent.
        let mut stream = http::send(listen, "GET", &path, None, None)?
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("nothing listens on {listen}"))?;
        match copy_stream(&mut stream, out, MAX_ERROR_BODY)
            .with_context(|| format!("GET http://{listen}{path}"))?
        {
            None => Ok(()),
            // llama-swap knows no log of that name.
            Some(response) if response.status == 400 => {
                bail!("the gateway serves no model {model:?}")
            }
            Some(response) => Err(unexpected(listen, "GET", &path, &response)),
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use std::net::TcpListener;

    use super::*;
    use crate::infrastructure::http::{parse, serve};

    const HEALTH: &[u8] = include_bytes!("../../tests/fixtures/llama-swap-263-health.http");
    const RUNNING: &[u8] = include_bytes!("../../tests/fixtures/llama-swap-263-running-empty.http");
    const LOADED: &[u8] = include_bytes!("../../tests/fixtures/llama-swap-263-running.http");
    /// `/logs/stream/fake` of a gateway whose model `fake` answered one
    /// request, read for a second, then disconnected.
    const STREAM: &[u8] = include_bytes!("../../tests/fixtures/llama-swap-263-logs-stream.http");
    /// `/logs/stream/nope`, for a model the gateway does not have.
    const UNKNOWN: &[u8] =
        include_bytes!("../../tests/fixtures/llama-swap-263-logs-stream-unknown.http");
    const STREAMED: &str = "fake: loading model\n\
         fake: \"GET /health HTTP/1.1\" 200 -\n\
         fake: \"POST /v1/chat/completions HTTP/1.1\" 200 -\n\
         fake: \"GET /v1/models HTTP/1.1\" 200 -\n";

    #[test]
    fn the_running_models_are_read_with_their_state() {
        assert_eq!(
            parse_running(&parse(LOADED).unwrap().body).unwrap(),
            [RunningModel {
                id: "qwen3.6-35b-a3b".into(),
                state: "ready".into(),
            }]
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse_running(&parse(RUNNING).unwrap().body).unwrap(),
            Vec::new()
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse_running(b"{\"models\":[]}").unwrap_err().to_string(),
            "the list of running models is malformed"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_models_log_is_streamed_from_the_gateway() {
        let (addr, server) = serve(STREAM);
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        LlamaSwapApi.stream_log(addr, "fake", &mut out).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(String::from_utf8(out).unwrap(), STREAMED);
        assert!(
            server
                .join()
                .unwrap()
                .starts_with("GET /logs/stream/fake HTTP/1.0\r\n")
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_unknown_model_has_no_log_stream() {
        let (addr, server) = serve(UNKNOWN);
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        assert_eq!(
            LlamaSwapApi
                .stream_log(addr, "nope", &mut out)
                .unwrap_err()
                .to_string(),
            "the gateway serves no model \"nope\""
        );
        server.join().unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn nothing_listening_is_unhealthy() {
        // A child forked while the port is bound would keep it listening.
        let _releasing = crate::infrastructure::command::exclusive();
        let addr = TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0")
            .unwrap()
            .local_addr()
            .unwrap();
        assert!(!LlamaSwapApi.healthy(addr).unwrap());
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_healthy_gateway_answers_200() {
        let (addr, server) = serve(HEALTH);
        assert!(LlamaSwapApi.healthy(addr).unwrap());
        assert!(
            server
                .join()
                .unwrap()
                .starts_with("GET /health HTTP/1.0\r\n")
        );
    }
}
</file>

<file path="src/infrastructure/gateway_process.rs">
//! The gateway as a process: llama-swap started detached from the terminal,
//! and the files it leaves in the state directory — its record, the
//! configuration it runs, and its log.
//!
//! The record and the configuration are written to a hidden file first and
//! renamed into place, so a reader never finds half of one. Whether a
//! recorded gateway still runs is read from `/proc/<pid>/exe`: a PID the
//! kernel gave to another program since is not the gateway.

use std::any::Any;
use std::fs::{self, File};
use std::io::{self, Read, Seek, SeekFrom};
use std::net::{SocketAddr, TcpListener};
use std::os::unix::process::CommandExt;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::process::{Child, Command, Stdio};

use anyhow::{Context, Result, anyhow, bail};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

use crate::application::ports::{
    GatewayProcesses, GatewayRecord, Liveness, Signal, StartedGateway,
};

use super::command::{self, RunError};
use super::files::lock;

const RECORD: &str = "gateway.json";
const CONFIG: &str = "llama-swap.yaml";
const LOG: &str = "gateway.log";
/// The log of the previous start, kept beside the new one.
const PREVIOUS_LOG: &str = "gateway.log.1";

/// The gateway's files: its record and configuration in `dir`, its log in
/// `logs_dir`.
pub struct LocalGateway {
    dir: PathBuf,
    logs_dir: PathBuf,
}

impl LocalGateway {
    pub fn new(dir: PathBuf, logs_dir: PathBuf) -> Self {
        Self { dir, logs_dir }
    }

    fn record_file(&self) -> PathBuf {
        self.dir.join(RECORD)
    }

    fn config_file(&self) -> PathBuf {
        self.dir.join(CONFIG)
    }

    /// Writes `text` to `name` in the gateway's directory, by a rename.
    fn write(&self, name: &str, text: &str) -> Result<()> {
        fs::create_dir_all(&self.dir)
            .with_context(|| format!("cannot create {}", self.dir.display()))?;
        let path = self.dir.join(name);
        let temporary = self.dir.join(format!(".{name}.tmp"));
        fs::write(&temporary, text)
            .and_then(|()| fs::rename(&temporary, &path))
            .with_context(|| format!("cannot write {}", path.display()))
    }

    /// Opens a new log, keeping the previous one as `gateway.log.1`.
    fn new_log(&self) -> Result<File> {
        fs::create_dir_all(&self.logs_dir)
            .with_context(|| format!("cannot create {}", self.logs_dir.display()))?;
        let log = self.log();
        match fs::rename(&log, self.logs_dir.join(PREVIOUS_LOG)) {
            Err(err) if err.kind() != io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {
                return Err(err).with_context(|| format!("cannot rotate {}", log.display()));
            }
            _ => {}
        }
        File::create(&log).with_context(|| format!("cannot create {}", log.display()))
    }
}

/// The record as `gateway.json` holds it.
#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize)]
struct RecordFile {
    pid: u32,
    binary: PathBuf,
    listen: SocketAddr,
}

/// Reads the program a process runs from its `/proc/<pid>/exe` link, which
/// names `binary`, or `binary (deleted)` once the file is removed.
fn liveness_of(pid: u32, binary: &Path) -> Result<Liveness> {
    let link = PathBuf::from(format!("/proc/{pid}/exe"));
    match fs::read_link(&link) {
        Ok(target) => {
            let deleted = format!("{} (deleted)", binary.display());
            if target == binary || target.as_os_str() == deleted.as_str() {
                Ok(Liveness::Running)
            } else {
                Ok(Liveness::Reused(Some(target)))
            }
        }
        // A process of another user hides its program.
        Err(err) if err.kind() == io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied => Ok(Liveness::Reused(None)),
        // No process, or a zombie, which has no program any more.
        Err(err) if err.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(Liveness::Exited),
        Err(err) => Err(err).with_context(|| format!("cannot read {}", link.display())),
    }
}

impl GatewayProcesses for LocalGateway {
    fn lock(&self) -> Result<Box<dyn Any>> {
        lock(&self.dir, "starting or stopping the gateway")
    }

    fn record(&self) -> Result<Option<GatewayRecord>> {
        let path = self.record_file();
        let text = match fs::read_to_string(&path) {
            Ok(text) => text,
            Err(err) if err.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(None),
            Err(err) => return Err(err).with_context(|| format!("cannot read {}", path.display())),
        };
        let record: RecordFile = serde_json::from_str(&text).with_context(|| {
            format!(
                "{} is malformed; remove it once no llama-swap runs",
                path.display()
            )
        })?;
        Ok(Some(GatewayRecord {
            pid: record.pid,
            binary: record.binary,
            listen: record.listen,
        }))
    }

    fn liveness(&self, record: &GatewayRecord) -> Result<Liveness> {
        liveness_of(record.pid, &record.binary)
    }

    fn forget(&self) -> Result<()> {
        let path = self.record_file();
        match fs::remove_file(&path) {
            Err(err) if err.kind() != io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {
                Err(err).with_context(|| format!("cannot remove {}", path.display()))
            }
            _ => Ok(()),
        }
    }

    fn config(&self) -> Result<Option<String>> {
        let path = self.config_file();
        match fs::read_to_string(&path) {
            Ok(text) => Ok(Some(text)),
            Err(err) if err.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(None),
            Err(err) => Err(err).with_context(|| format!("cannot read {}", path.display())),
        }
    }

    fn is_free(&self, listen: SocketAddr) -> Result<bool> {
        match TcpListener::bind(listen) {
            Ok(_) => Ok(true),
            Err(err) if err.kind() == io::ErrorKind::AddrInUse => Ok(false),
            Err(err) => Err(err).with_context(|| format!("cannot listen on {listen}")),
        }
    }

    fn start(
        &self,
        binary: &Path,
        config: &str,
        listen: SocketAddr,
    ) -> Result<Box<dyn StartedGateway + '_>> {
        self.write(CONFIG, config)?;
        let log = self.new_log()?;
        let output = log
            .try_clone()
            .with_context(|| format!("cannot open {}", self.log().display()))?;
        let mut command = Command::new(binary);
        command
            .arg("-config")
            .arg(self.config_file())
            .arg("-listen")
            .arg(listen.to_string())
            .current_dir(&self.dir)
            .stdin(Stdio::null())
            .stdout(output)
            .stderr(log)
            // Out of the terminal's process group: Ctrl-C and the end of the
            // session do not reach it.
            .process_group(0);
        let mut child = command::spawn(&mut command)
            .with_context(|| format!("cannot run {}", binary.display()))?;
        let record = RecordFile {
            pid: child.id(),
            binary: binary.to_owned(),
            listen,
        };
        let recorded = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&record)
            .context("cannot serialize the gateway's record")
            .and_then(|text| self.write(RECORD, &format!("{text}\n")));
        if let Err(err) = recorded {
            // Unrecorded, the gateway could not be stopped by ai-ctl.
            let _ = child.kill();
            let _ = child.wait();
            return Err(err);
        }
        Ok(Box::new(Started(child)))
    }

    fn signal(&self, pid: u32, signal: Signal) -> Result<()> {
        let name = match signal {
            Signal::Terminate => "TERM",
            Signal::Kill => "KILL",
        };
        let pid = pid.to_string();
        match command::run("kill", &["-s", name, &pid]) {
            Ok(output) if output.success => Ok(()),
            Ok(output) if output.stderr.contains("No such process") => Ok(()),
            Ok(output) => bail!("kill -s {name} {pid}: {}", output.failure()),
            Err(RunError::NotFound) => bail!("kill not found; run `sudo dnf install util-linux`"),
            Err(RunError::Failed(message)) => Err(anyhow!(message)),
        }
    }

    fn descendants(&self, pid: u32) -> Result<Vec<u32>> {
        let entries = fs::read_dir("/proc").context("cannot read /proc")?;
        let mut parents = Vec::new();
        for entry in entries.flatten() {
            let Some(child) = entry
                .file_name()
                .to_str()
                .and_then(|name| name.parse().ok())
            else {
                continue;
            };
            // A process may exit while /proc is read.
            if let Ok(stat) = fs::read_to_string(entry.path().join("stat"))
                && let Some(parent) = parent_pid(&stat)
            {
                parents.push((child, parent));
            }
        }
        Ok(descendants_of(pid, &parents))
    }

    fn program(&self, pid: u32) -> Result<Option<PathBuf>> {
        let link = PathBuf::from(format!("/proc/{pid}/exe"));
        match fs::read_link(&link) {
            Ok(target) => Ok(Some(target)),
            Err(err)
                if matches!(
                    err.kind(),
                    io::ErrorKind::NotFound | io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied
                ) =>
            {
                Ok(None)
            }
            Err(err) => Err(err).with_context(|| format!("cannot read {}", link.display())),
        }
    }

    fn log(&self) -> PathBuf {
        self.logs_dir.join(LOG)
    }

    fn log_tail(&self, lines: usize) -> Result<String> {
        let log = self.log();
        let bytes = fs::read(&log).with_context(|| format!("cannot read {}", log.display()))?;
        let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&bytes);
        let all: Vec<&str> = text.lines().collect();
        Ok(all[all.len().saturating_sub(lines)..].join("\n"))
    }

    fn read_log(&self, offset: u64, max: usize) -> Result<Option<Vec<u8>>> {
        let log = self.log();
        let mut file = match File::open(&log) {
            Ok(file) => file,
            Err(err) if err.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(None),
            Err(err) => return Err(err).with_context(|| format!("cannot open {}", log.display())),
        };
        let mut read = || -> io::Result<Option<Vec<u8>>> {
            if file.metadata()?.len() < offset {
                return Ok(None);
            }
            file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(offset))?;
            let mut bytes = Vec::new();
            (&mut file).take(max as u64).read_to_end(&mut bytes)?;
            Ok(Some(bytes))
        };
        read().with_context(|| format!("cannot read {}", log.display()))
    }
}

/// The parent PID in a `/proc/<pid>/stat` line: the fourth field, counted
/// after the program name, which is in parentheses and may hold any
/// character.
fn parent_pid(stat: &str) -> Option<u32> {
    let (_, after) = stat.rsplit_once(')')?;
    after.split_whitespace().nth(1)?.parse().ok()
}

/// The processes descending from `root`, its children first, given each
/// process's parent.
fn descendants_of(root: u32, parents: &[(u32, u32)]) -> Vec<u32> {
    let mut found = Vec::new();
    let mut next = 0;
    let mut current = root;
    loop {
        found.extend(
            parents
                .iter()
                .filter(|&&(child, parent)| parent == current && child != root)
                .map(|&(child, _)| child),
        );
        let Some(&pid) = found.get(next) else {
            return found;
        };
        current = pid;
        next += 1;
    }
}

/// llama-swap, started by this process.
struct Started(Child);

impl StartedGateway for Started {
    fn pid(&self) -> u32 {
        self.0.id()
    }

    fn exited(&mut self) -> Result<Option<String>> {
        let status = self.0.try_wait().context("cannot wait for llama-swap")?;
        Ok(status.map(|status| status.to_string()))
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;

    use super::*;

    fn gateway(root: &Path) -> LocalGateway {
        LocalGateway::new(root.join("gateway"), root.join("logs"))
    }

    fn record(pid: u32, binary: &Path) -> GatewayRecord {
        GatewayRecord {
            pid,
            binary: binary.to_owned(),
            listen: "127.0.0.1:8080".parse().unwrap(),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn this_process_runs_its_own_binary() {
        let me = std::env::current_exe().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            liveness_of(std::process::id(), &me).unwrap(),
            Liveness::Running
        );
        assert_eq!(
            liveness_of(std::process::id(), Path::new("/usr/bin/llama-swap")).unwrap(),
            Liveness::Reused(Some(me))
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_exited_process_is_seen_as_such() {
        let mut child = command::spawn(&mut Command::new("true")).unwrap();
        let pid = child.id();
        child.wait().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            liveness_of(pid, Path::new("/usr/bin/true")).unwrap(),
            Liveness::Exited
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_process_names_its_program_until_it_is_gone() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let gateway = gateway(dir.path());
        assert_eq!(
            gateway.program(std::process::id()).unwrap(),
            Some(std::env::current_exe().unwrap())
        );
        let mut child = command::spawn(&mut Command::new("true")).unwrap();
        let pid = child.id();
        child.wait().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(gateway.program(pid).unwrap(), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_log_is_read_from_an_offset_in_bounded_pieces() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let gateway = gateway(dir.path());
        assert_eq!(gateway.read_log(0, 4).unwrap(), None);
        fs::create_dir_all(dir.path().join("logs")).unwrap();
        fs::write(gateway.log(), "line 1\nline 2\n").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(gateway.read_log(0, 4).unwrap().unwrap(), b"line");
        assert_eq!(gateway.read_log(7, 100).unwrap().unwrap(), b"line 2\n");
        assert_eq!(gateway.read_log(14, 100).unwrap().unwrap(), b"");
        // Shorter than the offset: another log took its place.
        assert_eq!(gateway.read_log(15, 100).unwrap(), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_stat_line_gives_its_parent_whatever_the_program_name() {
        assert_eq!(
            parent_pid("5000 (llama-server) S 4242 5000 4021 0 -1 4194560 3"),
            Some(4242)
        );
        assert_eq!(parent_pid("77 (a) b (c)) R 12 77 77 0"), Some(12));
        assert_eq!(parent_pid("77 (truncated"), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn descendants_are_found_level_by_level() {
        let parents = [(2, 1), (5000, 4242), (5001, 5000), (6000, 4242), (7000, 1)];
        assert_eq!(descendants_of(4242, &parents), [5000, 6000, 5001]);
        assert_eq!(descendants_of(7000, &parents), Vec::<u32>::new());
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_child_of_this_process_is_among_its_descendants() {
        let gateway = gateway(Path::new("/nonexistent"));
        let mut child = command::spawn(Command::new("sleep").arg("60")).unwrap();
        let descendants = gateway.descendants(std::process::id()).unwrap();
        child.kill().unwrap();
        child.wait().unwrap();
        assert!(descendants.contains(&child.id()));
    }

    #[test]
    fn no_record_reads_as_none_and_forgetting_it_is_harmless() {
        let root = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let gateway = gateway(root.path());
        assert_eq!(gateway.record().unwrap(), None);
        assert_eq!(gateway.config().unwrap(), None);
        gateway.forget().unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_malformed_record_is_an_error_naming_it() {
        let root = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let gateway = gateway(root.path());
        gateway.write(RECORD, "{\"pid\": \"x\"}").unwrap();
        let message = gateway.record().unwrap_err().to_string();
        assert!(message.ends_with("gateway.json is malformed; remove it once no llama-swap runs"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_taken_port_is_not_free() {
        let gateway = gateway(Path::new("/nonexistent"));
        let listener = TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").unwrap();
        let addr = listener.local_addr().unwrap();
        assert!(!gateway.is_free(addr).unwrap());
        // A child forked meanwhile would hold the socket open.
        let _releasing = command::exclusive();
        drop(listener);
        assert!(gateway.is_free(addr).unwrap());
    }

    /// Writes an executable script into `dir`.
    fn script(dir: &Path, body: &str) -> PathBuf {
        let _writing = command::exclusive();
        let path = dir.join("llama-swap");
        fs::write(&path, format!("#!/bin/sh\n{body}\n")).unwrap();
        fs::set_permissions(&path, fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o755)).unwrap();
        path
    }

    fn wait(started: &mut dyn StartedGateway) -> String {
        loop {
            if let Some(status) = started.exited().unwrap() {
                return status;
            }
            std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(10));
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn start_writes_the_configuration_records_the_process_and_logs_its_output() {
        let root = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let gateway = gateway(root.path());
        // The working directory, the arguments, then a line on stderr.
        let binary = script(root.path(), "pwd; echo \"$@\"; echo failing >&2; exit 3");
        fs::create_dir_all(root.path().join("logs")).unwrap();
        fs::write(gateway.log(), "previous run\n").unwrap();
        let listen = "127.0.0.1:8080".parse().unwrap();

        let mut started = gateway.start(&binary, "models: {}\n", listen).unwrap();
        let pid = started.pid();
        assert_eq!(wait(started.as_mut()), "exit status: 3");

        let dir = root.path().join("gateway");
        assert_eq!(gateway.config().unwrap().as_deref(), Some("models: {}\n"));
        assert_eq!(gateway.record().unwrap(), Some(record(pid, &binary)));
        assert_eq!(
            fs::read_to_string(gateway.log()).unwrap(),
            format!(
                "{}\n-config {} -listen 127.0.0.1:8080\nfailing\n",
                dir.display(),
                dir.join(CONFIG).display()
            )
        );
        assert_eq!(
            gateway.log_tail(2).unwrap(),
            format!(
                "-config {} -listen 127.0.0.1:8080\nfailing",
                dir.join(CONFIG).display()
            )
        );
        assert_eq!(
            fs::read_to_string(root.path().join("logs").join(PREVIOUS_LOG)).unwrap(),
            "previous run\n"
        );
        gateway.forget().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(gateway.record().unwrap(), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_missing_binary_fails_to_start_and_records_nothing() {
        let root = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let gateway = gateway(root.path());
        let missing = root.path().join("llama-swap");
        let listen = "127.0.0.1:8080".parse().unwrap();
        let message = format!("{:#}", gateway.start(&missing, "", listen).err().unwrap());
        assert!(message.starts_with(&format!("cannot run {}", missing.display())));
        assert_eq!(gateway.record().unwrap(), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_signal_to_a_process_already_gone_is_not_an_error() {
        let gateway = gateway(Path::new("/nonexistent"));
        let mut child = command::spawn(&mut Command::new("true")).unwrap();
        let pid = child.id();
        child.wait().unwrap();
        gateway.signal(pid, Signal::Terminate).unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn terminate_stops_a_process() {
        let gateway = gateway(Path::new("/nonexistent"));
        let mut child = command::spawn(Command::new("sleep").arg("60")).unwrap();
        gateway.signal(child.id(), Signal::Terminate).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(child.wait().unwrap().to_string(), "signal: 15 (SIGTERM)");
    }
}
</file>

<file path="src/infrastructure/gateway_store.rs">
//! Installed llama-swap releases on disk.
//!
//! ```text
//! llama-swap/
//!   .lock                     held by every change to the releases
//!   v263/llama-swap
//!   current -> v263
//!   .staging-v264-4242/       a release being installed, out of sight
//! ```
//!
//! A release appears under its tag by a rename, so an installed release is
//! always complete and verified; the `current` link is replaced by a rename
//! too. Hidden entries left by an interrupted run go with the next install.

use std::any::Any;
use std::fs;
use std::io;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::process::Command;
use std::time::Duration;

use anyhow::{Context, Result, bail, ensure};

use crate::application::ports::{GatewayStore, StagedRelease};
use crate::domain::build::CURRENT;
use crate::domain::gateway::{BINARY, Release, Sha256};

use super::command::{RunError, execute};
use super::files::{lock, remove_dir_if_present, remove_hidden, replace_link};

/// `sha256sum` and `tar` work on a local archive of a few dozen MB.
const TOOL_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(60);

/// The releases under `root`.
pub struct FsGatewayStore {
    root: PathBuf,
}

impl FsGatewayStore {
    pub fn new(root: PathBuf) -> Self {
        Self { root }
    }

    fn release_dir(&self, release: Release) -> PathBuf {
        self.root.join(release.to_string())
    }

    /// A hidden name in the root, unique to this process.
    fn hidden(&self, kind: &str, release: Release) -> PathBuf {
        self.root
            .join(format!(".{kind}-{release}-{}", std::process::id()))
    }
}

/// Runs a tool on a local file and returns what it printed.
fn tool(command: Command) -> Result<String> {
    let program = command.get_program().to_string_lossy().into_owned();
    match execute(command, Some(TOOL_TIMEOUT)) {
        Ok(output) if output.success => Ok(output.stdout),
        Ok(output) => bail!("{program} failed: {}", output.failure()),
        Err(RunError::NotFound) => bail!("{program} is not installed"),
        Err(RunError::Failed(message)) => bail!(message),
    }
}

impl GatewayStore for FsGatewayStore {
    fn lock(&self) -> Result<Box<dyn Any>> {
        lock(&self.root, "installing llama-swap")
    }

    fn is_installed(&self, release: Release) -> Result<bool> {
        let binary = self.binary(release);
        match fs::metadata(&binary) {
            Ok(metadata) => Ok(metadata.is_file()),
            Err(err) if err.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(false),
            Err(err) => Err(err).with_context(|| format!("cannot read {}", binary.display())),
        }
    }

    fn current(&self) -> Result<Option<Release>> {
        let link = self.root.join(CURRENT);
        let target = match fs::read_link(&link) {
            Ok(target) => target,
            Err(err) if err.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(None),
            Err(err) => return Err(err).with_context(|| format!("cannot read {}", link.display())),
        };
        let release = target
            .to_str()
            .and_then(|name| Release::parse(name).ok())
            .filter(|release| release.to_string() == target.as_os_str().to_string_lossy());
        match release {
            Some(release) => Ok(Some(release)),
            None => bail!(
                "{} points at {}, not at a release of its directory",
                link.display(),
                target.display()
            ),
        }
    }

    fn binary(&self, release: Release) -> PathBuf {
        self.release_dir(release).join(BINARY)
    }

    fn stage(&self, release: Release) -> Result<StagedRelease> {
        remove_hidden(&self.root)?;
        let dir = self.hidden("staging", release);
        fs::create_dir_all(&dir).with_context(|| format!("cannot create {}", dir.display()))?;
        Ok(StagedRelease {
            release,
            archive: dir.join(release.archive()),
            dir,
        })
    }

    fn sha256(&self, file: &Path) -> Result<Sha256> {
        let mut command = Command::new("sha256sum");
        command.arg("--").arg(file);
        let output = tool(command)?;
        let digest = output.split_whitespace().next().unwrap_or_default();
        Sha256::parse(digest)
            .with_context(|| format!("unexpected output of sha256sum for {}", file.display()))
    }

    fn unpack(&self, staged: &StagedRelease) -> Result<PathBuf> {
        let mut command = Command::new("tar");
        command
            .args(["--extract", "--gzip", "--no-same-owner", "--file"])
            .arg(&staged.archive)
            .arg("--directory")
            .arg(&staged.dir)
            .args(["--", BINARY]);
        tool(command).with_context(|| format!("cannot unpack {}", staged.archive.display()))?;
        fs::remove_file(&staged.archive)
            .with_context(|| format!("cannot remove {}", staged.archive.display()))?;
        let binary = staged.dir.join(BINARY);
        let metadata = fs::symlink_metadata(&binary)
            .with_context(|| format!("cannot read {}", binary.display()))?;
        ensure!(
            metadata.is_file(),
            "{BINARY} in {} is not a file",
            staged.archive.display()
        );
        Ok(binary)
    }

    fn publish(&self, staged: &StagedRelease) -> Result<()> {
        let target = self.release_dir(staged.release);
        ensure!(
            target.symlink_metadata().is_err(),
            "{} already exists; remove it to install {} again",
            target.display(),
            staged.release
        );
        fs::rename(&staged.dir, &target)
            .with_context(|| format!("cannot install {}", target.display()))
    }

    fn discard(&self, staged: &StagedRelease) -> Result<()> {
        remove_dir_if_present(&staged.dir)
    }

    fn set_current(&self, release: Release) -> Result<()> {
        ensure!(
            self.is_installed(release)?,
            "llama-swap {release} is not installed in {}",
            self.root.display()
        );
        replace_link(
            &self.root.join(CURRENT),
            &release.to_string(),
            &self.hidden("current", release),
        )
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;

    use super::*;
    use crate::infrastructure::command;

    struct Fixture {
        root: tempfile::TempDir,
        store: FsGatewayStore,
    }

    fn fixture() -> Fixture {
        let root = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        Fixture {
            store: FsGatewayStore::new(root.path().join("llama-swap")),
            root,
        }
    }

    fn release(text: &str) -> Release {
        Release::parse(text).unwrap()
    }

    impl Fixture {
        /// Stages `release` with an archive holding a `llama-swap` script and
        /// a README, as a release archive does.
        fn stage_archive(&self, release: Release) -> StagedRelease {
            let content = self.root.path().join("content");
            fs::create_dir_all(&content).unwrap();
            let binary = content.join(BINARY);
            fs::write(&binary, "#!/bin/sh\necho version: v263\n").unwrap();
            fs::set_permissions(&binary, fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o755)).unwrap();
            fs::write(content.join("README.md"), "readme").unwrap();
            let staged = self.store.stage(release).unwrap();
            let mut tar = Command::new("tar");
            tar.arg("--create")
                .arg("--gzip")
                .arg("--file")
                .arg(&staged.archive)
                .arg("--directory")
                .arg(&content)
                .args([BINARY, "README.md"]);
            let status = command::spawn(&mut tar).unwrap().wait().unwrap();
            assert!(status.success());
            staged
        }

        fn install(&self, release: Release) {
            let staged = self.stage_archive(release);
            self.store.unpack(&staged).unwrap();
            self.store.publish(&staged).unwrap();
        }

        fn entries(&self) -> Vec<String> {
            let mut names: Vec<String> = fs::read_dir(&self.store.root)
                .unwrap()
                .map(|entry| entry.unwrap().file_name().to_string_lossy().into_owned())
                .collect();
            names.sort();
            names
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_staged_release_is_out_of_sight_until_published() {
        let f = fixture();
        let staged = f.stage_archive(release("263"));
        let name = staged
            .dir
            .file_name()
            .unwrap()
            .to_string_lossy()
            .into_owned();
        assert!(name.starts_with(".staging-v263-"), "{name}");
        assert_eq!(
            staged.archive.file_name().unwrap(),
            "llama-swap_263_linux_amd64.tar.gz"
        );
        assert!(!f.store.is_installed(release("263")).unwrap());

        let binary = f.store.unpack(&staged).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(binary, staged.dir.join(BINARY));
        assert!(!staged.archive.exists());
        assert!(!staged.dir.join("README.md").exists());
        f.store.publish(&staged).unwrap();
        assert!(f.store.is_installed(release("263")).unwrap());
        let installed = f.store.binary(release("263"));
        assert_eq!(installed, f.store.root.join("v263/llama-swap"));
        assert_eq!(
            fs::metadata(&installed).unwrap().permissions().mode() & 0o777,
            0o755
        );
        assert_eq!(f.entries(), ["v263"]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_digest_is_the_one_sha256sum_computes() {
        let f = fixture();
        let file = f.root.path().join("abc");
        fs::write(&file, "abc").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            f.store.sha256(&file).unwrap().as_str(),
            "ba7816bf8f01cfea414140de5dae2223b00361a396177a9cb410ff61f20015ad"
        );
        let message = format!(
            "{:#}",
            f.store.sha256(&f.root.path().join("absent")).unwrap_err()
        );
        assert!(
            message.starts_with("sha256sum failed: sha256sum:"),
            "{message}"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_archive_without_the_binary_is_refused() {
        let f = fixture();
        let staged = f.store.stage(release("263")).unwrap();
        fs::write(staged.dir.join("README.md"), "readme").unwrap();
        let mut tar = Command::new("tar");
        tar.arg("--create")
            .arg("--gzip")
            .arg("--file")
            .arg(&staged.archive)
            .arg("--directory")
            .arg(&staged.dir)
            .arg("README.md");
        let status = command::spawn(&mut tar).unwrap().wait().unwrap();
        assert!(status.success());
        let message = format!("{:#}", f.store.unpack(&staged).unwrap_err());
        assert!(message.starts_with("cannot unpack"), "{message}");
        assert!(message.contains("tar failed"), "{message}");

        fs::write(&staged.archive, "not an archive").unwrap();
        assert!(f.store.unpack(&staged).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_installed_release_is_not_overwritten() {
        let f = fixture();
        f.install(release("263"));
        let staged = f.stage_archive(release("263"));
        f.store.unpack(&staged).unwrap();
        let message = f.store.publish(&staged).unwrap_err().to_string();
        assert!(message.contains("v263 already exists"), "{message}");
        f.store.discard(&staged).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(f.entries(), ["v263"]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn leftovers_of_an_interrupted_run_go_with_the_next_install() {
        let f = fixture();
        f.install(release("262"));
        fs::create_dir_all(f.store.root.join(".staging-v263-1/sub")).unwrap();
        std::os::unix::fs::symlink("v262", f.store.root.join(".current-v262-1")).unwrap();
        let _guard = f.store.lock().unwrap();
        f.install(release("263"));
        assert_eq!(f.entries(), [".lock", "v262", "v263"]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_current_link_switches_between_installed_releases() {
        let f = fixture();
        assert_eq!(f.store.current().unwrap(), None);
        f.install(release("262"));
        f.install(release("263"));
        f.store.set_current(release("263")).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(f.store.current().unwrap(), Some(release("263")));
        f.store.set_current(release("262")).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(f.store.current().unwrap(), Some(release("262")));
        let link = f.store.root.join(CURRENT);
        assert_eq!(fs::read_link(&link).unwrap(), Path::new("v262"));
        assert!(link.join(BINARY).is_file());
        let message = f.store.set_current(release("9")).unwrap_err().to_string();
        assert!(
            message.starts_with("llama-swap v9 is not installed"),
            "{message}"
        );
        assert_eq!(f.entries(), ["current", "v262", "v263"]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_link_pointing_elsewhere_is_reported() {
        let f = fixture();
        f.install(release("263"));
        for target in ["/usr/bin", "263", "v0263"] {
            let link = f.store.root.join(CURRENT);
            let _ = fs::remove_file(&link);
            std::os::unix::fs::symlink(target, &link).unwrap();
            let message = f.store.current().unwrap_err().to_string();
            assert!(
                message.contains("not at a release of its directory"),
                "{target}: {message}"
            );
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_directory_without_its_binary_is_not_installed() {
        let f = fixture();
        fs::create_dir_all(f.store.root.join("v263")).unwrap();
        assert!(!f.store.is_installed(release("263")).unwrap());
        assert!(f.store.set_current(release("263")).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_second_lock_is_refused() {
        let f = fixture();
        let _first = f.store.lock().unwrap();
        let message = f.store.lock().unwrap_err().to_string();
        assert!(
            message.starts_with("another ai-ctl is installing llama-swap"),
            "{message}"
        );
    }
}
</file>

<file path="src/infrastructure/git.rs">
//! A project's sources through the `git` command line: one clone per
//! project under the data directory, checked out at the commit a build
//! needs.

use std::ffi::OsStr;
use std::fs;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::process::Command;

use anyhow::{Context, Result, anyhow, bail, ensure};

use crate::application::ports::{Checkout, Sources};
use crate::domain::build::CommitId;
use crate::domain::recipe::Recipe;

use super::command::{self, Output, RunError};
use super::files::remove_dir_if_present;

/// The clones live under `root`, one directory per project.
pub struct GitSources {
    root: PathBuf,
}

impl GitSources {
    pub fn new(root: PathBuf) -> Self {
        Self { root }
    }

    /// Where the project's clone lives.
    pub fn clone_dir(&self, recipe: &Recipe) -> PathBuf {
        self.root.join(recipe.project)
    }

    /// Clones the repository with its history but no file contents, which
    /// git fetches when a commit is checked out. The clone is made beside
    /// its final place and renamed, so an interrupted clone leaves no
    /// half-made repository behind.
    fn clone(&self, recipe: &Recipe, dir: &Path) -> Result<()> {
        fs::create_dir_all(&self.root)
            .with_context(|| format!("cannot create {}", self.root.display()))?;
        let partial = dir.with_extension("partial");
        remove_dir_if_present(&partial)?;
        let clone = git(
            None,
            [
                OsStr::new("clone"),
                OsStr::new("--quiet"),
                OsStr::new("--filter=blob:none"),
                OsStr::new("--no-checkout"),
                OsStr::new("--"),
                OsStr::new(recipe.repository),
                partial.as_os_str(),
            ],
        );
        let cloned = run(clone).with_context(|| format!("cannot clone {}", recipe.repository));
        if let Err(err) = cloned {
            // Best effort: the clone error is what matters.
            let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&partial);
            return Err(err);
        }
        fs::rename(&partial, dir)
            .with_context(|| format!("cannot move the clone to {}", dir.display()))
    }

    /// Updates an existing clone: branches, and tags even when moved.
    fn fetch(&self, recipe: &Recipe, dir: &Path) -> Result<()> {
        let origin = run(git(Some(dir), ["remote", "get-url", "origin"]))
            .with_context(|| format!("{} is not a usable clone", dir.display()))?;
        let origin = origin.stdout.trim();
        ensure!(
            origin == recipe.repository,
            "{} is a clone of {origin}, not of {}; remove it and ai-ctl clones the right one",
            dir.display(),
            recipe.repository
        );
        run(git(
            Some(dir),
            ["fetch", "--quiet", "--prune", "--tags", "--force", "origin"],
        ))
        .with_context(|| format!("cannot fetch {}", recipe.repository))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// The commit `reference` names: a tag, a branch of the remote, or a
    /// commit, in that order. A full commit hash the clone does not have
    /// yet is fetched by itself, which reaches commits no branch holds.
    fn resolve(&self, recipe: &Recipe, dir: &Path, reference: &str) -> Result<CommitId> {
        let candidates = [
            format!("refs/tags/{reference}"),
            format!("refs/remotes/origin/{reference}"),
            reference.to_owned(),
        ];
        for candidate in &candidates {
            if let Some(commit) = rev_parse(dir, candidate)? {
                return Ok(commit);
            }
        }
        if CommitId::parse(reference).is_ok() {
            let fetched = run(git(Some(dir), ["fetch", "--quiet", "origin", reference]));
            if fetched.is_ok()
                && let Some(commit) = rev_parse(dir, reference)?
            {
                return Ok(commit);
            }
        }
        bail!(
            "`{reference}` is not a tag, a branch or a commit of {}",
            recipe.repository
        )
    }
}

impl Sources for GitSources {
    fn release_tags(&self, recipe: &Recipe) -> Result<Vec<String>> {
        let pattern = recipe.tags.ls_remote_pattern();
        let output = run(git(
            None,
            [
                "ls-remote",
                "--tags",
                "--refs",
                "--",
                recipe.repository,
                &pattern,
            ],
        ))
        .with_context(|| format!("cannot list the tags of {}", recipe.repository))?;
        Ok(parse_ls_remote_tags(&output.stdout))
    }

    fn checkout(&self, recipe: &Recipe, reference: &str) -> Result<Checkout> {
        ensure!(
            !reference.is_empty() && !reference.starts_with('-'),
            "`{reference}` is not a git reference"
        );
        let dir = self.clone_dir(recipe);
        if dir.join(".git").is_dir() {
            self.fetch(recipe, &dir)?;
        } else {
            ensure!(
                !dir.exists(),
                "{} exists but is not a git clone; remove it and ai-ctl clones {} again",
                dir.display(),
                recipe.repository
            );
            self.clone(recipe, &dir)?;
        }

        let commit = self.resolve(recipe, &dir, reference)?;
        run(git(
            Some(&dir),
            [
                "checkout",
                "--quiet",
                "--force",
                "--detach",
                commit.as_str(),
            ],
        ))
        .with_context(|| format!("cannot check out {commit}"))?;
        // Files a previous build or a person left in the tree would end up
        // in the build.
        run(git(Some(&dir), ["clean", "-ffdxq"]))
            .with_context(|| format!("cannot clean {}", dir.display()))?;
        let tags = run(git(Some(&dir), ["tag", "--points-at", commit.as_str()]))
            .context("cannot list the tags of the commit")?;
        Ok(Checkout {
            dir,
            commit,
            tags: tags.stdout.lines().map(str::to_owned).collect(),
        })
    }
}

/// A `git` command in `dir`. Git never waits for a password, and a transfer
/// slower than 1 KB/s for a minute is aborted rather than left hanging.
fn git<I, S>(dir: Option<&Path>, args: I) -> Command
where
    I: IntoIterator<Item = S>,
    S: AsRef<OsStr>,
{
    let mut command = Command::new("git");
    if let Some(dir) = dir {
        command.arg("-C").arg(dir);
    }
    command
        .args([
            "-c",
            "color.ui=never",
            "-c",
            "http.lowSpeedLimit=1000",
            "-c",
            "http.lowSpeedTime=60",
        ])
        .args(args)
        .env("GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT", "0");
    command
}

/// Runs a git command; its first error line explains a failure.
fn run(command: Command) -> Result<Output> {
    match command::execute(command, None) {
        Ok(output) if output.success => Ok(output),
        Ok(output) => Err(anyhow!(output.failure())),
        Err(RunError::NotFound) => Err(anyhow!("git not found; run `sudo dnf install git`")),
        Err(RunError::Failed(message)) => Err(anyhow!(message)),
    }
}

/// The commit `revision` names in the clone, or `None` when it names none.
fn rev_parse(dir: &Path, revision: &str) -> Result<Option<CommitId>> {
    let peeled = format!("{revision}^{{commit}}");
    let output = command::execute(
        git(Some(dir), ["rev-parse", "--verify", "--quiet", &peeled]),
        None,
    );
    match output {
        Ok(output) if output.success => {
            let hash = output.stdout.trim();
            CommitId::parse(hash)
                .map(Some)
                .with_context(|| format!("unexpected `git rev-parse` output for {revision}"))
        }
        // `--quiet`: an unknown revision fails without a message.
        Ok(_) => Ok(None),
        Err(RunError::NotFound) => bail!("git not found; run `sudo dnf install git`"),
        Err(RunError::Failed(message)) => bail!(message),
    }
}

/// Tag names in `git ls-remote --tags --refs` output, one
/// `<hash>\trefs/tags/<name>` line per tag.
pub fn parse_ls_remote_tags(output: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    output
        .lines()
        .filter_map(|line| line.split_once('\t'))
        .filter_map(|(_, reference)| reference.strip_prefix("refs/tags/"))
        .filter(|name| !name.ends_with("^{}"))
        .map(str::to_owned)
        .collect()
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::domain::recipe::{LLAMA_CPP, TagPattern};

    #[test]
    fn ls_remote_lines_give_tag_names() {
        let path =
            Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("tests/fixtures/git-ls-remote-tags.txt");
        let output = fs::read_to_string(path).unwrap();
        let tags = parse_ls_remote_tags(&output);
        assert_eq!(
            tags,
            [
                "b11538",
                "b11537",
                "b11536",
                "b10964-b10964-b29c606",
                "b9999",
                "b10000"
            ]
        );
        assert_eq!(
            LLAMA_CPP.tags.newest(tags.iter().map(String::as_str)),
            Some("b11538")
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn peeled_and_foreign_lines_are_ignored() {
        let output = "aaaa\trefs/tags/v1.0\naaaa\trefs/tags/v1.0^{}\nbbbb\trefs/heads/master\nwarning: redirecting\n";
        assert_eq!(parse_ls_remote_tags(output), ["v1.0"]);
        assert!(parse_ls_remote_tags("").is_empty());
    }

    /// A repository standing for the project's remote, with releases `b1`,
    /// `b2` and `b10` on `master`, tags that are not releases, and a
    /// `feature` branch one commit ahead.
    struct Upstream {
        _dir: tempfile::TempDir,
        url: String,
        commits: Vec<String>,
    }

    /// Runs git with neither the user's nor the system's configuration.
    fn plain_git(dir: &Path, args: &[&str]) -> String {
        let mut git = Command::new("git");
        git.arg("-C")
            .arg(dir)
            .args(["-c", "user.name=Test", "-c", "user.email=test@example.com"])
            .args(["-c", "commit.gpgsign=false", "-c", "tag.gpgsign=false"])
            .args(args)
            .env("GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL", "/dev/null")
            .env("GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM", "1")
            .stdout(std::process::Stdio::piped())
            .stderr(std::process::Stdio::piped());
        let output = command::spawn(&mut git)
            .unwrap()
            .wait_with_output()
            .unwrap();
        assert!(output.status.success(), "git {args:?}: {output:?}");
        String::from_utf8(output.stdout).unwrap().trim().to_owned()
    }

    fn upstream() -> Upstream {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let repo = dir.path();
        plain_git(repo, &["init", "--quiet", "--initial-branch=master"]);
        plain_git(repo, &["config", "uploadpack.allowFilter", "true"]);
        plain_git(repo, &["config", "uploadpack.allowAnySHA1InWant", "true"]);
        let mut commits = Vec::new();
        for (i, tag) in ["b1", "b2", "b10"].into_iter().enumerate() {
            fs::write(repo.join("version.txt"), format!("{i}\n")).unwrap();
            plain_git(repo, &["add", "version.txt"]);
            plain_git(repo, &["commit", "--quiet", "-m", tag]);
            plain_git(repo, &["tag", tag]);
            commits.push(plain_git(repo, &["rev-parse", "HEAD"]));
        }
        plain_git(repo, &["tag", "-a", "-m", "not a release", "b10-rc"]);
        plain_git(repo, &["tag", "backup/old", &commits[0]]);
        plain_git(repo, &["checkout", "--quiet", "-b", "feature"]);
        fs::write(repo.join("feature.txt"), "new\n").unwrap();
        plain_git(repo, &["add", "feature.txt"]);
        plain_git(repo, &["commit", "--quiet", "-m", "feature"]);
        commits.push(plain_git(repo, &["rev-parse", "HEAD"]));
        plain_git(repo, &["checkout", "--quiet", "master"]);
        Upstream {
            url: format!("file://{}", repo.display()),
            _dir: dir,
            commits,
        }
    }

    fn recipe(url: &str) -> Recipe {
        Recipe {
            project: "demo",
            repository: Box::leak(url.to_owned().into_boxed_str()),
            tags: TagPattern::Numbered { prefix: "b" },
            ..LLAMA_CPP
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn release_tags_are_read_from_the_remote() {
        let upstream = upstream();
        let sources = GitSources::new(PathBuf::from("/nonexistent"));
        let recipe = recipe(&upstream.url);
        let mut tags = sources.release_tags(&recipe).unwrap();
        tags.sort();
        assert_eq!(tags, ["b1", "b10", "b10-rc", "b2"]);
        assert_eq!(
            recipe.tags.newest(tags.iter().map(String::as_str)),
            Some("b10")
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_first_checkout_clones_then_later_ones_fetch() {
        let upstream = upstream();
        let root = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let sources = GitSources::new(root.path().join("sources"));
        let recipe = recipe(&upstream.url);

        let first = sources.checkout(&recipe, "b2").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(first.dir, root.path().join("sources/demo"));
        assert_eq!(first.commit.as_str(), upstream.commits[1]);
        assert_eq!(first.tags, ["b2"]);
        assert_eq!(
            fs::read_to_string(first.dir.join("version.txt")).unwrap(),
            "1\n"
        );
        assert!(!root.path().join("sources/demo.partial").exists());

        // A stray file disappears; a branch and a full hash resolve.
        fs::write(first.dir.join("stray.o"), "").unwrap();
        let feature = sources.checkout(&recipe, "feature").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(feature.commit.as_str(), upstream.commits[3]);
        assert!(feature.tags.is_empty());
        assert!(!feature.dir.join("stray.o").exists());
        assert!(feature.dir.join("feature.txt").is_file());

        let by_hash = sources.checkout(&recipe, &upstream.commits[0]).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(by_hash.tags, ["b1", "backup/old"]);
        let short = sources
            .checkout(&recipe, &upstream.commits[2][..8])
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(short.commit.as_str(), upstream.commits[2]);
        let mut tags = short.tags;
        tags.sort();
        assert_eq!(tags, ["b10", "b10-rc"]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_unknown_reference_is_refused() {
        let upstream = upstream();
        let root = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let sources = GitSources::new(root.path().to_owned());
        let recipe = recipe(&upstream.url);
        for reference in ["b99", "no-such-branch", &"0".repeat(40)] {
            let message = format!("{:#}", sources.checkout(&recipe, reference).unwrap_err());
            assert!(
                message.contains("is not a tag, a branch or a commit"),
                "{message}"
            );
        }
        for reference in ["", "--upload-pack=touch"] {
            let message = format!("{:#}", sources.checkout(&recipe, reference).unwrap_err());
            assert!(message.contains("is not a git reference"), "{message}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_clone_of_another_repository_is_refused() {
        let first = upstream();
        let other = upstream();
        let root = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let sources = GitSources::new(root.path().to_owned());
        sources.checkout(&recipe(&first.url), "b1").unwrap();
        let message = format!(
            "{:#}",
            sources.checkout(&recipe(&other.url), "b1").unwrap_err()
        );
        assert!(message.contains("remove it"), "{message}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_failed_clone_leaves_nothing_behind() {
        let root = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let sources = GitSources::new(root.path().to_owned());
        let missing = root.path().join("missing");
        let recipe = recipe(&format!("file://{}", missing.display()));
        let message = format!("{:#}", sources.checkout(&recipe, "b1").unwrap_err());
        assert!(message.starts_with("cannot clone"), "{message}");
        assert_eq!(fs::read_dir(root.path()).unwrap().count(), 0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_directory_that_is_not_a_clone_is_left_alone() {
        let upstream = upstream();
        let root = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        fs::create_dir(root.path().join("demo")).unwrap();
        fs::write(root.path().join("demo/notes.txt"), "mine").unwrap();
        let sources = GitSources::new(root.path().to_owned());
        let message = format!(
            "{:#}",
            sources.checkout(&recipe(&upstream.url), "b1").unwrap_err()
        );
        assert!(message.contains("is not a git clone"), "{message}");
        assert!(root.path().join("demo/notes.txt").is_file());
    }
}
</file>

<file path="src/infrastructure/host.rs">
//! The host probe: external commands, sysfs and D-Bus, all read-only.

use std::collections::HashSet;
use std::ffi::OsString;
use std::fs;
use std::io;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use crate::application::ports::HostProbe;
use crate::domain::machine::{
    CudaRepository, CudaToolkit, DRIVER_PACKAGES, HostCompiler, Probed, Tool, Version,
};

use super::command::{self, RunError};

/// Where the CUDA packages install `nvcc` when it is not on the `PATH`.
const NVCC_FALLBACK: &str = "/usr/local/cuda/bin/nvcc";
/// `host_config.h` relative to a toolkit root, newest layout first.
const HOST_CONFIG: [&str; 2] = [
    "targets/x86_64-linux/include/crt/host_config.h",
    "include/crt/host_config.h",
];
const POWER_SUPPLIES: &str = "/sys/class/power_supply";
const OS_RELEASE: &str = "/etc/os-release";
/// The power profiles D-Bus interface under its current and legacy names;
/// both `power-profiles-daemon` and `tuned-ppd` serve it.
const POWER_PROFILES: [[&str; 3]; 2] = [
    [
        "org.freedesktop.UPower.PowerProfiles",
        "/org/freedesktop/UPower/PowerProfiles",
        "org.freedesktop.UPower.PowerProfiles",
    ],
    [
        "net.hadess.PowerProfiles",
        "/net/hadess/PowerProfiles",
        "net.hadess.PowerProfiles",
    ],
];

/// Probes this machine.
pub struct SystemHost {
    path: Option<OsString>,
    power_supplies: PathBuf,
    os_release: PathBuf,
}

impl SystemHost {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            path: std::env::var_os("PATH"),
            power_supplies: PathBuf::from(POWER_SUPPLIES),
            os_release: PathBuf::from(OS_RELEASE),
        }
    }

    fn path_dirs(&self) -> Vec<PathBuf> {
        self.path
            .as_deref()
            .map(|path| std::env::split_paths(path).collect())
            .unwrap_or_default()
    }

    fn find_nvcc(&self) -> Option<PathBuf> {
        self.path_dirs()
            .into_iter()
            .map(|dir| dir.join("nvcc"))
            .chain(std::iter::once(PathBuf::from(NVCC_FALLBACK)))
            .find(|candidate| candidate.is_file())
    }
}

impl Default for SystemHost {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl HostProbe for SystemHost {
    fn cuda_toolkit(&self) -> Probed<CudaToolkit> {
        let Some(nvcc) = self.find_nvcc() else {
            return Probed::Missing;
        };
        let output = match command::run(&nvcc, &["--version"]) {
            Ok(output) if output.success => output,
            Ok(output) => return Probed::Failed(output.failure()),
            Err(err) => return failed_run(err),
        };
        let Some(version) = parse_nvcc_version(&output.stdout) else {
            return Probed::Failed("unexpected `nvcc --version` output".to_owned());
        };
        // `/usr/local/cuda/bin/nvcc` resolves to `/usr/local/cuda-13.2/bin/nvcc`,
        // whose grandparent is the toolkit root.
        let nvcc = fs::canonicalize(&nvcc).unwrap_or(nvcc);
        let max_gcc_major = match nvcc.parent().and_then(Path::parent) {
            Some(root) => read_max_gcc_major(root),
            None => Probed::Missing,
        };
        Probed::Found(CudaToolkit {
            version,
            nvcc,
            max_gcc_major,
        })
    }

    fn host_compilers(&self) -> Probed<Vec<HostCompiler>> {
        let mut candidates: Vec<(String, PathBuf)> = Vec::new();
        for dir in self.path_dirs() {
            let Ok(entries) = fs::read_dir(&dir) else {
                continue;
            };
            for entry in entries.flatten() {
                let name = entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().into_owned();
                // ccache's `g++` comes first on Fedora's `PATH`
                // (`/usr/lib64/ccache`): the compiler is the one behind it.
                if is_gxx(&name)
                    && !is_compiler_cache(&entry.path())
                    && !candidates.iter().any(|(known, _)| *known == name)
                {
                    candidates.push((name, entry.path()));
                }
            }
        }
        candidates.sort_by_key(|(name, _)| gxx_order(name));

        let mut compilers = Vec::new();
        let mut seen = HashSet::new();
        let mut first_error = None;
        for (command, path) in candidates {
            // `g++` may be a link to `g++-15`: report the binary once.
            if !seen.insert(fs::canonicalize(&path).unwrap_or_else(|_| path.clone())) {
                continue;
            }
            match command::run(&path, &["-dumpfullversion"]) {
                Ok(output) if output.success => match Version::parse(&output.stdout) {
                    Some(version) => compilers.push(HostCompiler {
                        command,
                        path,
                        version,
                    }),
                    None => {
                        first_error.get_or_insert(format!("{command}: unexpected version output"));
                    }
                },
                Ok(output) => {
                    first_error.get_or_insert(format!("{command}: {}", output.failure()));
                }
                Err(err) => {
                    first_error.get_or_insert(format!("{command}: {}", describe(err)));
                }
            }
        }

        match (compilers.is_empty(), first_error) {
            (false, _) => Probed::Found(compilers),
            (true, Some(error)) => Probed::Failed(error),
            (true, None) => Probed::Missing,
        }
    }

    fn tool_version(&self, tool: Tool) -> Probed<Version> {
        match command::run(tool.command(), &["--version"]) {
            Ok(output) if output.success => match parse_version_output(&output.stdout) {
                Some(version) => Probed::Found(version),
                None => Probed::Failed(format!("unexpected `{} --version` output", tool.command())),
            },
            Ok(output) => Probed::Failed(output.failure()),
            Err(err) => failed_run(err),
        }
    }

    fn cdi_devices(&self) -> Probed<Vec<String>> {
        match command::run("nvidia-ctk", &["cdi", "list"]) {
            Ok(output) if output.success => Probed::Found(parse_cdi_list(&output.stdout)),
            Ok(output) => Probed::Failed(output.failure()),
            Err(err) => failed_run(err),
        }
    }

    fn ac_power(&self) -> Probed<bool> {
        ac_online(&self.power_supplies)
    }

    fn power_profile(&self) -> Probed<String> {
        let mut first_error = None;
        for [service, object, interface] in POWER_PROFILES {
            let args = ["get-property", service, object, interface, "ActiveProfile"];
            match command::run("busctl", &args) {
                Ok(output) if output.success => {
                    return match parse_busctl_string(&output.stdout) {
                        Some(profile) => Probed::Found(profile),
                        None => Probed::Failed("unexpected busctl output".to_owned()),
                    };
                }
                Ok(output) if is_service_absent(&output.stderr) => {}
                Ok(output) => {
                    first_error.get_or_insert(output.failure());
                }
                Err(err) => return failed_run(err),
            }
        }
        first_error.map_or(Probed::Missing, Probed::Failed)
    }

    fn is_file(&self, path: &Path) -> bool {
        path.is_file()
    }

    fn fedora_release(&self) -> Probed<u32> {
        match fs::read_to_string(&self.os_release) {
            Ok(text) => parse_fedora_release(&text).map_or(Probed::Missing, Probed::Found),
            Err(err) if err.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Probed::Missing,
            Err(err) => Probed::Failed(format!("{}: {err}", self.os_release.display())),
        }
    }

    fn cuda_repositories(&self) -> Probed<Vec<CudaRepository>> {
        let ids = match command::run("dnf", &["repolist", "--enabled"]) {
            Ok(output) if output.success => parse_repolist(&output.stdout),
            Ok(output) => return Probed::Failed(output.failure()),
            Err(err) => return failed_run(err),
        };
        let mut repositories = Vec::new();
        for id in ids.into_iter().filter(|id| id.starts_with("cuda-")) {
            // The cache only: doctor stays fast and offline. The query sees
            // the repository's exclusions, as `dnf install` would.
            let mut args = vec![
                "repoquery",
                "--cacheonly",
                "--quiet",
                "--repo",
                &id,
                "--qf",
                "%{name}\\n",
            ];
            args.extend(DRIVER_PACKAGES);
            match command::run("dnf", &args) {
                Ok(output) if output.success => repositories.push(CudaRepository {
                    driver_packages: parse_package_names(&output.stdout),
                    id,
                }),
                Ok(output) => return Probed::Failed(format!("{id}: {}", output.failure())),
                Err(err) => return failed_run(err),
            }
        }
        Probed::Found(repositories)
    }

    fn toolkit_packages(&self) -> Probed<Vec<String>> {
        match command::run("rpm", &["-qa", "--qf", "%{NAME}\\n", "cuda-toolkit*"]) {
            Ok(output) if output.success => Probed::Found(parse_package_names(&output.stdout)),
            Ok(output) => Probed::Failed(output.failure()),
            Err(err) => failed_run(err),
        }
    }
}

fn failed_run<T>(err: RunError) -> Probed<T> {
    match err {
        RunError::NotFound => Probed::Missing,
        RunError::Failed(message) => Probed::Failed(message),
    }
}

fn describe(err: RunError) -> String {
    match err {
        RunError::NotFound => "not found".to_owned(),
        RunError::Failed(message) => message,
    }
}

/// Reads the newest GCC major version the toolkit accepts.
fn read_max_gcc_major(root: &Path) -> Probed<u32> {
    let Some(header) = HOST_CONFIG
        .iter()
        .map(|relative| root.join(relative))
        .find(|path| path.is_file())
    else {
        return Probed::Missing;
    };
    match fs::read_to_string(&header) {
        Ok(text) => match parse_max_gcc_major(&text) {
            Some(major) => Probed::Found(major),
            None => Probed::Failed(format!("no GCC limit in {}", header.display())),
        },
        Err(err) => Probed::Failed(format!("{}: {err}", header.display())),
    }
}

/// `Cuda compilation tools, release 13.2, V13.2.86` → 13.2.86.
pub fn parse_nvcc_version(output: &str) -> Option<Version> {
    let line = output.lines().find(|line| line.contains("release"))?;
    let full = line
        .split_whitespace()
        .find_map(|word| word.strip_prefix('V'))
        .and_then(Version::parse);
    full.or_else(|| {
        let release = line.split("release").nth(1)?.split_whitespace().next()?;
        Version::parse(release.trim_end_matches(','))
    })
}

/// The `N` of the first `#if __GNUC__ > N` in `crt/host_config.h`: nvcc
/// rejects any GCC whose major version is above it.
pub fn parse_max_gcc_major(header: &str) -> Option<u32> {
    header.lines().find_map(|line| {
        let words: Vec<&str> = line.split_whitespace().collect();
        match words.as_slice() {
            ["#if", "__GNUC__", ">", major] => major.parse().ok(),
            _ => None,
        }
    })
}

/// The version after the word `version` on the first line that has one, as
/// printed by `cmake`, `git`, `podman` and `nvidia-ctk`.
pub fn parse_version_output(output: &str) -> Option<Version> {
    output.lines().find_map(|line| {
        let mut words = line.split_whitespace();
        words.find(|word| *word == "version")?;
        Version::parse(words.next()?)
    })
}

/// CDI device names, one per line, such as `nvidia.com/gpu=all`.
pub fn parse_cdi_list(output: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    output
        .lines()
        .map(str::trim)
        .filter(|line| {
            line.split_once('=')
                .is_some_and(|(kind, _)| kind.contains('/') && !kind.contains(' '))
        })
        .map(str::to_owned)
        .collect()
}

/// The `VERSION_ID` of `/etc/os-release` when its `ID` is `fedora`.
pub fn parse_fedora_release(text: &str) -> Option<u32> {
    let field = |name: &str| {
        text.lines().find_map(|line| {
            let value = line.strip_prefix(name)?.strip_prefix('=')?;
            Some(value.trim().trim_matches(['"', '\'']))
        })
    };
    if field("ID")? != "fedora" {
        return None;
    }
    field("VERSION_ID")?.parse().ok()
}

/// The repository ids of `dnf repolist`: the first word of each line after
/// the header.
pub fn parse_repolist(output: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    output
        .lines()
        .skip_while(|line| !line.starts_with("repo id"))
        .skip(1)
        .filter_map(|line| line.split_whitespace().next())
        .map(str::to_owned)
        .collect()
}

/// Package names, one per line, sorted and without duplicates: a package
/// available in several versions is listed once.
pub fn parse_package_names(output: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut names: Vec<String> = output
        .lines()
        .map(str::trim)
        .filter(|line| !line.is_empty())
        .map(str::to_owned)
        .collect();
    names.sort();
    names.dedup();
    names
}

/// `s "performance"` → `performance`.
pub fn parse_busctl_string(output: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let value = output.trim().strip_prefix("s ")?;
    let value = value.strip_prefix('"')?.strip_suffix('"')?;
    Some(value.to_owned())
}

/// True when busctl failed because no service provides the name.
fn is_service_absent(stderr: &str) -> bool {
    stderr.contains("not activatable") || stderr.contains("not provided by any .service")
}

/// True when `path` resolves to a compiler cache such as ccache, which
/// stands in for a compiler under the compiler's name.
fn is_compiler_cache(path: &Path) -> bool {
    fs::canonicalize(path).is_ok_and(|target| {
        target
            .file_name()
            .is_some_and(|name| name == "ccache" || name == "sccache")
    })
}

fn is_gxx(name: &str) -> bool {
    name == "g++"
        || name
            .strip_prefix("g++-")
            .is_some_and(|major| !major.is_empty() && major.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit()))
}

/// `g++` first, then `g++-N` from the newest major down.
fn gxx_order(name: &str) -> (bool, std::cmp::Reverse<u32>) {
    let major = name
        .strip_prefix("g++-")
        .and_then(|major| major.parse().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0);
    (name != "g++", std::cmp::Reverse(major))
}

/// Whether an external supply (mains, USB-C…) is online, from the
/// `type` and `online` attributes under `/sys/class/power_supply`.
pub fn ac_online(root: &Path) -> Probed<bool> {
    let entries = match fs::read_dir(root) {
        Ok(entries) => entries,
        Err(err) if err.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Probed::Missing,
        Err(err) => return Probed::Failed(format!("{}: {err}", root.display())),
    };
    let mut external = Vec::new();
    for entry in entries.flatten() {
        let read = |attribute: &str| fs::read_to_string(entry.path().join(attribute));
        let Ok(kind) = read("type") else { continue };
        if kind.trim() == "Battery" {
            continue;
        }
        if let Ok(online) = read("online") {
            external.push(online.trim() == "1");
        }
    }
    if external.is_empty() {
        Probed::Missing
    } else {
        Probed::Found(external.contains(&true))
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn fixture(name: &str) -> String {
        let path = Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))
            .join("tests/fixtures")
            .join(name);
        fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap_or_else(|err| panic!("{}: {err}", path.display()))
    }

    #[test]
    fn nvcc_version_comes_from_the_release_line() {
        assert_eq!(
            parse_nvcc_version(&fixture("nvcc-13.2.txt")),
            Some(Version::new(13, 2, 86))
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn nvcc_version_falls_back_to_the_release_number() {
        let output = "Cuda compilation tools, release 13.4\n";
        assert_eq!(parse_nvcc_version(output), Some(Version::new(13, 4, 0)));
    }

    #[test]
    fn nvcc_version_rejects_other_output() {
        assert_eq!(parse_nvcc_version(""), None);
        assert_eq!(parse_nvcc_version("nvcc fatal : No input files"), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn gcc_limit_comes_from_host_config() {
        assert_eq!(
            parse_max_gcc_major(&fixture("host_config-13.2-excerpt.h")),
            Some(15)
        );
        assert_eq!(parse_max_gcc_major("#if defined(__GNUC__)\n#endif\n"), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn gcc_dumpfullversion_parses() {
        assert_eq!(
            Version::parse(&fixture("gcc-15-dumpfullversion.txt")),
            Some(Version::new(15, 3, 1))
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn tool_versions_parse() {
        let cases = [
            ("cmake-version.txt", Version::new(4, 3, 0)),
            ("git-version.txt", Version::new(2, 56, 0)),
            ("podman-version.txt", Version::new(5, 8, 7)),
            ("nvidia-ctk-version.txt", Version::new(1, 20, 1)),
        ];
        for (name, expected) in cases {
            assert_eq!(
                parse_version_output(&fixture(name)),
                Some(expected),
                "{name}"
            );
        }
        assert_eq!(parse_version_output("no number here"), None);
        assert_eq!(parse_version_output("version"), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn cdi_list_keeps_device_names_only() {
        let mut output = fixture("nvidia-ctk-cdi-list.txt");
        output.insert_str(
            0,
            "time=\"2026-10-09T10:46:19+02:00\" level=info msg=\"Found 3 CDI devices\"\n",
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse_cdi_list(&output),
            [
                "nvidia.com/gpu=0",
                "nvidia.com/gpu=GPU-00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000",
                "nvidia.com/gpu=all",
            ]
        );
        assert!(parse_cdi_list("").is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn busctl_strings_parse() {
        assert_eq!(
            parse_busctl_string(&fixture("busctl-active-profile.txt")).as_deref(),
            Some("performance")
        );
        assert_eq!(parse_busctl_string("u 3"), None);
        assert_eq!(parse_busctl_string(""), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_absent_power_service_is_recognised() {
        assert!(is_service_absent(&fixture("busctl-not-activatable.txt")));
        assert!(is_service_absent(
            "Failed to get property ActiveProfile: The name net.hadess.PowerProfiles was not provided by any .service files"
        ));
        assert!(!is_service_absent("Access denied"));
    }

    /// Writes an executable script.
    fn script(path: &Path, body: &str) {
        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
        let _exclusive = command::exclusive();
        fs::write(path, format!("#!/bin/sh\n{body}\n")).unwrap();
        fs::set_permissions(path, fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o755)).unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
